# Aura3D live games and 4.5.0 audit (2026-10-29)

This audit covers the 25 live games listed in `docs/workflows/game-inventory.json` and the engine changes shipped in 4.5.0 (`git diff v4.3.7 v4.5.0`, 753 files). It was done read-only at origin/main 31f8945ad, which contains tag v4.5.0 (6813d5d24) plus post-release docs and evidence commits. Each game was read at source level (game code, its 4.5.0 upgrade guide, its evidence folder) and probed on production (https://aura3d.auraone.ai, serving 4.5.0) in the Prism cloud browser, which renders with software GL. The release was audited in five areas: render path, lighting, shadows and instancing, animation/physics/API, and release hygiene. Targeted unit tests were run with vitest; findings carry file:line, a command output or a measured number. Not done: no real-GPU run, no 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state capture for the games that lack one, no remote Playwright suite, no fixes. `node tools/game-checks/run.mjs <slug>` reports 0 errors and 0 warnings for every game, so it is not repeated here. Severity: P0 broken or unplayable on the live site, P1 wrong behaviour a player sees or a 4.5.0 regression, P2 quality, feel, perf or robustness, P3 polish.

Totals across the 25 games: 8 P0, 98 P1, 215 P2, 190 P3. The release audit adds 1 P1 (LIGHT-01), 22 P2 and 29 P3; it found no P0. No game is black or crashes on load: all 25 pages answer 200 and render. Two games are rated broken because their core loop cannot be completed (Aura Diamond 3D, Aura Links 3D).

## Outcome

Status after the audit-fix program (lanes E1 to E5 and G01 to G25, merged on `audit-fix/integration`). Every row of the release tables and of each game's Fix and Enhance tables has a Status cell: `done <commit> [release]`, `not done: <reason>`, `not a defect: <reason>` or `withdrawn: <reason>`. Commits come from the lane reports in `docs/audits/lanes/` and, for G12 (no report file), from its commit messages. Engine rows that appear inside a game section carry the engine lane's outcome. The release in brackets is checked per commit against tag `v4.6.0`: 4.6.0 shipped E1 to E5, G01 to G09, G12 and G15 to G17; 4.6.1 carries the other twelve games (G10, G11, G13, G14, G18 to G25) and the wave-3 changes. A row whose commits are not all in `v4.6.0` is marked 4.6.1.

Counts are per table row: 562 rows, 52 in the release tables and 510 in the game tables. 21 game-table rows repeat an engine row (for example RP-04 appears in seven games) and are counted in each place.

RH-01 is listed with no severity because the owner withdrew it. Of the 555 done rows, 327 shipped in 4.6.0, 226 in 4.6.1 and 1 (IC-12) in 4.6.2; RH-13 was done by the owner's own agent. SO-6 and WW-16 were closed in wave 3 after the first count (a51efc5ea; fae2d5510 and 1452dcdc7). IC-12 was closed after that, once Meshy access through Kiro Prism was available (296d7b781). The three remaining "not done" rows each need a real-GPU run (`tools/real-gpu/run.sh`), which the owner does.

### Not done

Attempted 2026-10-30 04:00-04:45 UTC: Azure has no GPU quota in any region; GCP needs an interactive re-login; AWS through the gated runner template launched g6.xlarge and g6.2xlarge (NVIDIA L4) in us-west-2d, but the runner subnet's only egress is the allowlisted package proxy, so the NVIDIA image's SSM agent timed out on ssm.us-west-2.amazonaws.com and no probe could run; both instances terminated, 0 volumes left. The probe is ready to run (tools/real-gpu, plus the IC-07/LIVE-1/P-3 probe in evidence/real-gpu/2026-10-30/probe.mjs) on any GPU machine with internet (`evidence/real-gpu/2026-10-30/README.md`). Also tried after that: `az-auraone-gchahal` (the only Azure subscription with GPU quota, NVSv4) across 18 regions, where the size was either not offered to the subscription or out of capacity; and an AWS CodeBuild `LINUX_GPU_CONTAINER` build on `auraone-pr-validation`, which timed out in the queue without a GPU. Every attempt was cleaned up (0 Azure groups, 0 AWS instances).

- **IC-07 (Iron Crown)**, P2: needs a real-GPU check (`?vfx=on&vfxdebug=1`); the remote runner is software GL, so the override stays; owner runs `tools/real-gpu/run.sh`
- **LIVE-1 (Aura Diamond 3D)**, P2: needs a 15-minute real-GPU session; the software-GL runner cannot reproduce a renderer crash (park-switch heap measured at 468 MB, no page errors)
- **P-3 (Serpentine Sky Run)**, P3: the fix asks for a real-GPU re-judge first and the lane has only software GL; on software GL the runner crop is back at the 4.3.7 contrast (193.4 against 206.0)

### Withdrawn and not a defect

- **RH-01 (release audit)**, withdrawn: the owner wants the three.js comparison claims; fix 93f374ffd was reverted in 4c2f6b2a1
- **RH-02 (release audit)**, withdrawn: its fix 93f374ffd went out with the RH-01 revert (4c2f6b2a1); RESULTS.md stays the named comparison source by owner decision
- **IC-09 (Iron Crown)**, not a defect: `model(asset, { material })` lowers to `baseColorMultiply`, which keeps the base-colour textures; `setTint` is a legacy seam this path does not use (remote swatches keep belt, skin and face detail)
- **S-07 (Shadowstep Gauntlet)**, not a defect: the HDRI texture is a plain object, so the scene lights through the neutral room, which already counts the hemisphere; removing the light would remove fill (comment added in 4678c26df)

Wave 3 closed the open handoffs of DD-7 (a665a8ebb), DD-10 (8c35fdeef), HP-04 (a51efc5ea), S-04 (12d4cd717), C9 (56b22eba1) and C11 (1452dcdc7); those rows are fully done. The rows below still have an open part.

- **C12 (Duneglide)**: no real-GPU or phone profile

## Fix first

Ordered: P0 first (game cannot be played or finished), then the release P1, then game P1s grouped by how many players they reach (touch/mobile and run-ending bugs before scoring and cosmetic ones).

### P0

1. Aura Diamond 3D, C1: `triggerSwing` forces `timingErr 0` (main.ts:2666-2675). Grade the swing from the real timing error so manual at-bats can make outs.
2. Aura Links 3D, C1: gate the auto-swing at main.ts:1997-2017 on `autopilot`, size putts against the real green friction, and add a stroke cap.
3. Aura FC 3D, FC-01: Touch Lab sets `state.players = [me]` (sim.ts:1246). Guard team-1 lookups in `outOfBounds`/restarts or add a dummy keeper.
4. Bunker Zero, C1: give boss-summoned guards a scene node (or skip `app.nodes.require` for nodeless enemies) so floor 6 can be won.
5. Pip's Big Leap, C1: read `hurtTimer` and return Pip from `hurt` to `idle` when it expires (player.ts:115-117, 277-280).
6. Aura Hoops 3D, C1: show `#touch` on coarse-pointer devices (index.html:761-763 media query).
7. Duneglide, C1: add touch `interact` and brake buttons (main.ts:168-178).
8. Glint Courier, DD-1: hide `dd-gameover` in `closeOverlays` (hud.ts:118-123).

9. Engine, LIGHT-01: four lights-only ports lost the neutral-room IBL in 4.5.0 (renderer.ts:152 `implicitFill:false`). Measured on Chroma Roll (C1): towers 220 to 134 luma, islands 189 to 95. Add `environments.neutral({ intensity })` to heatsink-port, splat-rush-port, serpentine-sky-run-port (source in `examples/saiyan-snake-way-port`) and strange-orbit-port, calibrated to 4.3.7 captures.

## Cross-cutting issues

- **No or broken touch input.** No touch at all: heatsink, teleoperator, strange-orbit-port, drift-and-deliver-port, heatsink-port, last-train-port, roadster-hours-port. Touch present but broken: aura-hoops-3d (hidden), aura-fc-3d (stick never mounts), iron-crown (wrong action names), silt-and-signal-port (no interact). Missing touch pause: ninja-runner-port, pips-big-leap. A game-check that fails a game with keyboard-only gameplay input would catch all of these.
- **Portrait phones.** Fixed vertical FOV with no aspect compensation: top-tennis, aura-fc-3d, dot-dash-3d, slingshot-siege. Live wrappers missing mobile CSS that exists in the example page: where-the-wind-wanders-port, silt-and-signal-port.
- **"Built with Aura3D" credit link** (z-index 2147483000) sits over game controls: splat-rush-port (mute button), where-the-wind-wanders-port (joystick).
- **Missing end states.** heatsink (laps wrap), drift-and-deliver-port (no end), dot-dash-3d (no win screen), cloud-runner-port (death soft-lock), bunker-zero (floor 6 loop), last-train-port (keep riding stuck), aura-links-3d and aura-diamond-3d (holes and innings never finish).
- **Capped lists drained by index.** bunker-zero events (cap 40, drained by index) and splat-rush-port popups (cap 64) stop working once the cap is hit.
- **Node pools smaller than the simulation.** roadster-rush (traffic, sky cars, gantries, bolts), slingshot-siege (72 nodes for 83 pieces, stale pooled materials).
- **Fixed-per-frame input or dt.** Held-key reads where an edge is needed: roadster-hours-port (R), aura-hoops-3d (`buffered()`), slingshot-siege (double update). Fixed 1/60 per rendered frame: top-tennis.
- **Engine: 4.5.0 lighting changes unmeasured per game.** Only Diamond, Hoops (3 of 6 courts), Pip's, Glint Courier and Iron Crown Neo-Tokyo have steady-state 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 luma. Lights-only IBL loss (LIGHT-01): four ports. Hemisphere now counted next to ambient (LIGHT-03): top-tennis, bunker-zero, aura-links-3d, dot-dash-3d (+18% measured). Runtime look swap keeps the fill (LIGHT-04): cross-hop.
- **Engine: authored hemisphere lights still dropped next to an environment (LIGHT-05)** in roadster-rush, heatsink, teleoperator, slingshot-siege, cloud-runner-port, drift-and-deliver-port, ninja-runner-port, silt-and-signal-port, aura-links-3d. Same as 4.3.7; the upgrade README says otherwise.
- **Engine: SSAO pass dropped for missing `ssao:normals`** on bunker-zero, dot-dash-3d, last-train-port, cloud-runner-port, so the 4.5.0 real-depth AO fix does nothing there while AO still costs the HDR chain.
- **Engine: fused bloom (`separable`, default on quality auto) never compared per game (RP-05).** Applies to the 20 bloom games.
- **Engine: instanced nodes pick one shadow cascade from the node centre (SI-1)**, so spread-out instances get no sun shadow. Measured on aura-links-3d conifers; also silt-and-signal-port, ninja-runner-port, cloud-runner-port, roadster-hours-port, cross-hop, last-train-port. Predates 4.5.0.
- **Stale evidence and posters (RH-05).** Every game's screens and index posters predate the 4.5.0 render changes; dot-dash-3d and aura-hoops-3d posters also predate gameplay or grounding fixes. 234 upgrade-guide checks are still open (RH-04).
- **Shared 2.6 MB asset catalog with 522 dead URLs (RH-06)** loaded by aura-links-3d, bunker-zero, heatsink, iron-crown, pips-big-leap, roadster-rush, slingshot-siege, teleoperator.
- **Third-party names in shipped bundles.** serpentine-sky-run-port (`saiyan-snake-way`), cloud-runner-port (`kid-goku`), teleoperator (mindblown.ai toast; Waymo/Tesla/SpaceX in package.json).
- **Audio not suspended on pause or hidden tab:** drift-and-deliver-port, ninja-runner-port, roadster-hours-port; roadster-rush first run silent; slingshot-siege mute does nothing.
- **Load size with no progress UI:** ninja-runner-port (26.75 MB, GLBs served max-age=0), where-the-wind-wanders-port (15.7 MB uncompressed GLBs).
- **Debug URL typos stop every game mounting (RP-04):** an invalid `?aura3d-render=` or `?aura3d-bloom-fusion=` throws inside mount on all 25 pages.

## 4.5.0 release audit

Scope: `git diff v4.3.7 v4.5.0` plus the post-tag docs and evidence on origin/main 31f8945ad, checked against the 25 live games. No release finding is P0. One is P1: LIGHT-01, a 4.5.0 lighting regression measured on Chroma Roll.

### Dropped on verification

Both release P1 findings were re-checked against the cited code.

- **LIGHT-01 kept at P1.** `compiler/renderer.ts:152` passes `implicitFill: false`. `environment.ts:172-179` then returns preset `authored-lights` with `environmentMapIntensity: 0` and `environmentMapSpecularIntensity: 0` (`explicitLightFillFree`, environment.ts:50-79). `prd02ProbeBindIntensities` (environment.ts:386-392) turns that into 0/0 for the neutral-room probe. All four ports author only `lights.hemisphere` plus `lights.directional`, and rg finds no `environments.*` or `looks.preset` in any of them (heatsink-port/world.ts:101-102, splat-rush-port/backdrop.ts:50-52, strange-orbit-port/world.ts:187-189, saiyan-snake-way-port/world.ts:158-159). The 4.3.7 environment.ts has no `implicitFill` or `authored-lights`. Chroma Roll measured the effect: tower luma 220.4 to 134.1, island underside 189.3 to 94.9, with the custom-shader road as control (151.2 to 150.8).
- **LIGHT-02 downgraded from P1 to P2.** The evidence holds (the steady-state harness covers 10 targets in 5 games), but it is a test gap, not a defect a player sees. The visible parts are LIGHT-01 and LIGHT-03.

No other finding was dropped.

### Render path

Verified correct: under quality `auto` all 25 games take `hdr-msaa`, because each authors an effect, custom material, output option or look (live: Gridlock Courier `bloomFusion: separable`, Chroma Roll `bloomFusion: off`). No live game is on the direct path. When the direct path is used, it keeps r185 ACES plus the sRGB OETF inside the shader (ProgramGenerator.ts:69-94) and applies fog before the encode. `setScene` re-decides the path. `setOutput` warns on the direct path. `preserveDrawingBuffer` follows `?capture`/`?qa`. `app.screenshot()` re-renders when idle. The bloom-fusion gating, the GL error-check cadence, the state-cache memoisation, StructuralMemo and per-instance cull bounds are all correct.

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Games | Status |
|----|-----|-------|----------|-----|-------|---|
| RP-01 | P2 | Direct-path blockers ignore a GLB's own BLEND and transmission materials | renderPath.ts:127-134 reads only the authored `node.material`. Computed: 50% white over black is 197/255 on HDR and 113/255 on direct | Block on asset material facts from `aura.assets.json`, or re-decide after the GLB decodes; add a glass-GLB parity case | none live | done 0c563444e [4.6.0] |
| RP-02 | P2 | Background image on the direct path uses the Narkowicz ACES fit, not r185 | renderPath.ts:103-136 never checks `backgroundImage`; ShaderLibraryCore.ts:883-888. Linear 0.18 grey gives 141 (direct) against 127 (HDR) | Make backgroundImage a blocker, or use OUTPUT_SRGB_CHUNK_GLSL in the background and legacy encodes | none live | done 0c563444e [4.6.0] |
| RP-03 | P2 | Effects or transparent nodes added at runtime, and `app.post.addPostPass`, are dropped silently on a direct-path app | Path decided once (frameLoop.ts:83-87); `addSubtree` does not remount (runtimeNodes.ts:138-142); renderInput.ts:402 forces `postprocess: undefined` | Remount through setScene when a blocker appears, or emit `POST_IGNORED_DIRECT_PATH` | none live | done 0c563444e [4.6.0] |

### Lighting

Verified correct: A3D_QR_LIGHTING and A3D_QR_COMPILER are on by default (flags.state.ts:21,45). The live site serves the calibrated look fill (0.85/0.65/1.0 in `guard-BFw69cBA.js`). look-fill-game-composition.test.ts passes 8/8. The no-HDRI looks were measured at steady state: Diamond Skyline 71.0 to 74.5, Lunar 82.8 to 86.1; Hoops Shanghai 53.5 to 55.6, Orbit 109.4 to 113.3; Pip's w6 108.1 to 111.1; Iron Crown Neo-Tokyo 31.4 to 33.4. Games with an HDRI look or an explicit environment are not affected by the lights-only change. where-the-wind-wanders-port authors no lights, so its fallback is unchanged. All 8 contact-shadow games have a shadow-casting key light. Variant switches go through setScene, so the fill-yield rule is recomputed per variant.

### Shadows and instancing

Verified correct: cascade skipping uses the forward pass's own selection function and camera inputs (CascadeUse.ts:42), is recomputed every frame, and is off for per-fragment lookups and quality `production`. Per-instance culling runs only in ForwardPass against its own view-projection, never in depth passes; skinned, morph, custom-shader and `frustumCulled:false` items are excluded, and cull bounds are correct for scaled and rotated instances. The upload skip compares contents, so a typed array changed in place still uploads. Dense depth draws and the shadow proxy stay off on every live game (largest node 900 instances, threshold 1,024; `__A3D_SHADOW_PROXIES__` null live). The VAO index leak fix is in place. PCF tap tiers are opt-in. The PRD-2 visual-regression run's only candidate-only failure also fails on main.

### Animation, physics and API

Verified correct: an AST scan of the 25 games (360 files, 533 `primitives`/`instances`/`interactions` calls) found 0 option keys outside `PRIMITIVE_OPTION_KEYS`. No game passes the newly forwarded options (renderOrder, frustumCulled, layers, userData, rotationOrder, quaternion), and none sets `strict`. No game uses playLayer, masks, addClipsFrom, IK, root motion, bindNodes, scene physics, Clock, ShapeGeometry or SphericalHarmonics3, so none of these changes alters a live game. The 21 templates pass only `target` to `interactions.orbit`. OPTION_IGNORED reaches strict mode. The 12 targeted unit files pass (70 tests). These also hold: the step-track, ShapeGeometry, SH3 and WEIGHTS_0 fixes, the codemod counts (48/150/47/0), the `@aura3d/math/three` export, and the AuraCanvas remount fix.

# Per-game audit

## 01. Aura Slam 3D (`top-tennis`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-top-tennis/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-top-tennis` · **Genre:** sports

**State:** The production page loads with no console errors and no failed requests. `__AURA3D_ROUTE_READY__.status` is `ready`, all 3 typed assets are `ready`, and the backend is WebGL2 with 119 draw calls. It drops straight into a match. The probe ran on SwiftShader software GL. Mean luma was 52.5 (49.3 mid-rally), 7.9% of pixels were below luma 12 and none clipped, so the frame is lit and not uniform. Serve, fault and second serve all worked. A full point and the results screen were not reached at 0.4-0.85 fps. The game is playable on desktop. It has visible defects: a white crowd, wrong key hints, and no athletes in view on portrait phones.

**4.5.0 impact:** The game source did not change. `git diff --stat v4.3.7 HEAD -- examples/aura-slam apps/showcase-top-tennis` shows only the version bump in `package.json`. All changes come from the engine:
- The scene has bloom, vignette and colorGrade, so it stays on the HDR chain. Under quality `auto` it gets separable bloom fusion and the cascade skip.
- It has 24 instanced spectators, below the 1,024 dense-instancing threshold, and no OPTION_IGNORED triggers.
- The scene authors both ambient and hemisphere lights (`view/lighting.ts:19-20`), and 4.5.0 now counts the hemisphere. The computed ambient term rises from 0.492 to 0.861 (x1.75).
- The only measurement is the exposure-parity row: 60.9 to 61.4 (+1%), taken on `/examples/aura-slam/?heal=0` at 320x200 after a 9 s boot. That does not match the computed 1.75x, and no steady-state capture of the live route exists.
- The fused bloom was never compared against the production chain for this game.

The release looks safe here but is unverified. `docs/upgrade-4.5.0/top-tennis.md:3-5` says nothing was built, run or measured.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| top-tennis-C1 | P1 | All 24 spectators render white: the per-frame update wipes their per-instance colours. | `examples/aura-slam/view/spectators.ts:29-36` builds the capsules with `colors` and material `#ffffff`. `updateSpectators` (`spectators.ts:47-53`, called every render from `view/renderer.ts:181`) calls `handle.setInstances(transforms)` with no colours. `packages/engine/src/agent-api/app/runtimeNodes.ts:449` then sets `node.instanceColors = colors ? [...colors] : undefined`. `evidence/aura-slam/screens/final/1280-04-mid.jpg` shows plain white/grey capsules. | Pass a cached colour list on every update, or use `setInstanceMatrices(Float32Array, count, rgba)` with preallocated buffers. Add a unit test that the node keeps 24 colours after `updateSpectators`. | done aa9f7815c [4.6.0] |
| top-tennis-C2 | P1 | Portrait phones (390 px) show neither athlete during play. | `390-04-mid.jpg` shows only the centre strip of the court, the black net band and the ball at the left edge. `390-03-action.jpg` crops the rival and the player is not in frame. In portrait, `ui/styles.css:229-230` shrinks `#game` between a 120 px score band and the control band. `view/camera.ts:11` keeps a fixed vertical FOV with no aspect compensation, so neither sideline is in frame. | Make the camera aspect-aware: widen the vertical FOV, or dolly the eye back, when aspect < 1. Use the same value in `projectWorldPoint` (camera.ts:39-41) and `debug()`. Keep the current FOV at aspect >= 1.6 so desktop frames do not change. Add a 390x844 capture that asserts both athletes project inside the canvas. | done aa9f7815c, dc64a21bd [4.6.0] |
| top-tennis-C3 | P2 | The control hint names the wrong keys: it shows E as LOB and R as POWER. | `apps/showcase-top-tennis/index.html:117` and `examples/aura-slam/index.html:141` read "DROP SHOT: Q · LOB: E · POWER: R". `contract/input.ts:29` binds `deep: KeyE`, `lob: KeyR`, `power: ShiftLeft/ShiftRight`. The hint is visible at the bottom of `1280-04-mid.jpg`, is static HTML, and ignores rebinds. | Build the hint from `controls.labelFor(...)` and refresh it on `controls.subscribe`. Correct text: "Q short · E deep · R lob (Pro/Legend) · Shift power". | done 4e010c3e6 [4.6.0] |
| top-tennis-C4 | P2 | Serve-setup movement and autoplay timing depend on the display refresh rate. | `shell/boot.ts:328` calls `autopilot.step(s, FIXED_DT)` inside `render()`. `render()` runs once per rAF tick (`boot.ts:148`), on every resize (`boot.ts:461`), and on control or settings changes (`boot.ts:236`, `472-473`). `shell/controller.ts:62-65` moves the server by `vx*dt` with `vx = 1.35` and a fixed dt of 1/60. At 120 Hz that is 2x speed, and at 30 Hz half speed. The auto-serve timer (`controller.ts:80-84`, 0.45 s) and rally chase (`controller.ts:95-104`) scale the same way. | Run the adapter in the fixed-step callback once per 1/60 s sim step, and drop it from resize/settings re-renders. The match keeps simulating behind it. | Call `preventDefault()`, pause, and show "Graphics reset, tap to resume". On restore, remount or reload. **Tests and evidence gaps**
- The game's evidence and screens are dated 2026-10-25 (`git log -1 --format=%cs -- evidence/aura-slam`), before 4.5.0. The results screen (06) was never captured at 1280 or 390.
- No 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state luma capture exists for the live route, and no fused-bloom-vs-production diff exists (`?capture=1` vs `?capture=1&aura3d-render=production`).
- None of the 31 files in `tests/unit/aura-slam` covers spectator colours (C1), refresh-rate independence of the adapter (C4), serve-position legality (C5) or portrait framing (C2).
- The live probe could not finish a point at software-GL speed. The scoring, reset and game-over paths need a QA-hook run (`startOffline` + `stepFrames`) on the remote runner.
- `info().render.triangles` reported 0 alongside 119 draw calls. The cause was not traced, so do not use this counter as evidence until it is checked.

**Dropped on verification**
- C15 was merged into the bullets above, and LIGHT-02/RH-04/RH-05/RP-05 into LIGHT-03 and the bullets, because they all describe the same missing post-4.5.0 captures.

## 02. Apex Overdrive (`roadster-rush`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-roadster-rush/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-roadster-rush` · **Genre:** racing

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. The live probe ran on SwiftShader software GL in Prism and found no console errors and no failed requests (45 resources). Steering, crash to "Wrecked!", the saved best score, Space-to-restart and bolt pickup all worked. The frame is lit and varied: mean luma 83-85, 1-1.6% of pixels below luma 12, nothing clipped. The draw call count is high, 868-895 per frame (`__GAME__.getState().drawCalls`). The simulation is sound, but the renderer's fixed node pools are smaller than what the simulation contains. As a result the player meets traffic, sky cars, bolts and sign gantries that are not drawn, all Meshy traffic faces backwards, and the first run has no sound.

**4.5.0 impact:** 4.5.0 changed no game source; `git diff --stat v4.3.7 v4.5.0 -- apps/showcase-roadster-rush` shows only `package.json`. Engine changes do reach the game. `looks.preset("outdoor-day")` (main.ts:218) adds an AO pass, and AO now reads real depth, so the frame changes from a constant darkening to crease AO. Bloom runs on quality `auto`, which means fused `separable` bloom. The authored `lights.hemisphere` (main.ts:231) is still dropped because the scene has an HDRI environment (LIGHT-05); this matches 4.3.7. None of these changes broke the game live. No 4.5.0 frame of this route has been captured, though: the final screens date from 2026-10-25 (commit e62b5fd42), so the size of the AO and bloom change is unknown.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RR-1 | P1 | The ground-traffic and sky-car pools are too small. Vehicles pop in close, heavy vehicles are drawn as cars, and some lethal sky cars are never drawn. | main.ts:66-74 creates 21 traffic nodes (bus 1, boxtruck 2, carrier 2) and main.ts:93 sets `SKY_CAR_POOL_COUNT = 5`. main.ts:1333-1335 takes every vehicle in [pz-235, pz+28] and sorts by absolute distance, so slots go to cars up to 28 m behind the player. main.ts:1350-1358 sends heavy types to the sedan…pickup pools when theirs are full, and main.ts:1359 `continue`s when nothing is free. The carrier ramp is set only when `chosenType === "carrier"` (main.ts:1398). The sky loop stops at 5 (main.ts:1458). In a headless replay (seed 42), the window holds 46.5 vehicles on average at 20-30 s and 62.9 at 50-60 s, and up to 24 sky cars. Undrawn vehicles appear 26-42 m ahead and undrawn sky cars 7-16 m ahead. Sky cars are lethal (traffic.ts:382-387). | Leave out vehicles more than 8 m behind the player and sort by distance ahead. Size each type's pool to the measured peak (about 64 ground vehicles, 26 sky cars), or move each type to `instances.*` with `setInstanceTransforms`. Never draw a heavy type with a car node. | done 3134cfcec, 352bbde2c, 54dec6334, 5c4087761 [4.6.0] |
| RR-2 | P1 | Three of the five collidable sign gantries are never drawn, so the player crashes into invisible "sign"s. | world.ts:15: `GANTRY_COUNT = ceil(500/155)+1` = 5, and world.ts:84 collides every slot. main.ts:103: `GANTRY_POOL_COUNT = 2`, and main.ts:1104-1108 draws only `worldGantries[0]` and `[1]`. In a 6 km sweep, 18 of 45 gantry passes were slot 0 or 1. | Export `GANTRY_COUNT` from world.ts, use it as the pool size, and hide slots at `NO_GANTRY_Z`. | done 3134cfcec, 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-3 | P1 | All Meshy traffic is drawn rotated 180°, so the grilles face the chase camera and the carrier ramp and brake bars sit at the cab end. This is lesson M15 again. | meshy-vehicles.ts:5 says the GLBs are baked "front toward -z", and traffic drives toward -z. The hero is drawn with yaw 0, but traffic uses `vyaw + Math.PI` (main.ts:1393). The ramp is placed at `vz + vl*0.5` (main.ts:1404) and the brake bars at the same end (main.ts:1413). In evidence/apex-overdrive/screens/final/key-ramp.1280x720.jpg the truck cabs and the van's headlights face the camera, and the yellow ramps sit under the cabs. The live bundle contains `setRotation(-cs,_e+Math.PI,-Rt)`. | Drop `+ Math.PI` when `meshyVehicles.enabled` (keep it for `?models=0` only if the old assets need it). Check the blinker side, then recapture key-ramp. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-4 | P1 | Bolts are drawn in array order, not by distance, so bolts at the player's position can be invisible yet still collectable. | main.ts:1505-1516 walks `traffic.pickups` in order over [pz-235, pz+16] and stops at `BOLT_POOL_COUNT = 28` (main.ts:94). The replay saw up to 129-142 bolts in the window; the nearest undrawn bolt was 0 m ahead, with 1,429-6,724 undrawn bolt-frames within 40 m in each 10 s bucket. Power-up orbs (pool of 8, main.ts:1524) use the same order. | Draw the nearest bolts ahead first, or use one `instances.torus` node holding about 150 instances. | done 3134cfcec, 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-5 | P1 | The first run is silent. | main.ts:1761 calls `ensureLivePlaying()` at boot. That calls `game.start()`, which runs `audio.unlock()` (game.ts:218 → audio.ts:44-47 → AudioContextManager.ts:57-62 `context.resume()`) before any user gesture, so the context stays suspended. KeyboardInput, the HUD and the mute button never call unlock again (grep for `unlock` in input.ts and hud.ts finds nothing). `musicTick` returns early unless the context is `running` (audio.ts:140). Sound starts only once `start()` runs again, after a crash and Retry or a toolbar scenario press. | Unlock audio from a one-shot `pointerdown`/`keydown` listener, or use `createCueBank` (lessons-learned P8). | done 352bbde2c, 42f6db5e0 [4.6.0] |
| RR-6 | P2 | The sign collision band is about 1 m wider than the drawn sign. | world.ts:84 crashes when `maxY > 6.95 && minY < 11.1`. The drawn boards are centred at 8.85 m and 2.3 m tall, so they span 7.7-10.0 m, and the beam is at 9.0 m (main.ts:381-391). A car whose roof is at 7.0-7.7 m visibly clears the sign and still crashes. | Derive the collision band and the board geometry from one shared constant. | done 3134cfcec, 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-7 | P2 | The scenario and Auto buttons work as cheats, and assisted runs still set the saved best score. | main.ts:1602-1672: "Live Freeway" sets `p.air = 100`, "Rocket Send" gives a 6 s invulnerable rocket, "Sky Dogfight" teleports to y = 14.2, and "Auto" runs an autopilot. All of them work mid-run. `gameOver()` writes `roadsterRush.best` with no flag for assisted runs (game.ts:250-264). | Show these buttons only with `?qa=1`/`?demo=1`, or mark any run that used them as assisted and leave the best score unchanged. Keep Sunset, Dusk and Night. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-8 | P2 | The skyline buildings snap back instead of scrolling past the camera. | main.ts:112 `SKYLINE_POOL_COUNT = 2` gives one building per side at `floor(pz/140)*140 - 65` (main.ts:1141-1146). Each building jumps from about 66 m ahead back to about 205 m ahead every 140 m, roughly every 2.2 s at 64 m/s. The poles (POLE_POOL_COUNT 8, main.ts:109) appear about 116 m ahead. | Use 4 or more slots per side, recycle each one only after it passes behind the camera, or move these props to instances. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-9 | P2 | No pause on tab hide or focus loss. The drone and music keep playing in the background, and the music keeps playing on the Paused card. | `rg visibilitychange apps/showcase-roadster-rush/src` finds nothing. The window `blur` handler only clears input (input.ts:59-61). The music runs on `setInterval` (audio.ts:160). Pause quiets only the engine (game.ts:234). | On `document.hidden` or blur, pause and suspend the context. Stop the music while paused. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-10 | P2 | There is no title card or objective. The run starts behind the boot fade, and phones get no instructions at all. | `boot()` enters `title`, but main.ts:1761 starts the run at once. The hint bar (index.html:158-160) is hidden at 760 px and below (index.html:138-140). This breaks checklist G1. | Keep the title state: a card with the goal, controls for the current input and the best score, started by Space, tap or Start. Auto-start only with `?autoplay=1` or `?capture=1`. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-11 | P2 | The 4.5.0 HDRI preset lights and effects stack on top of the authored ones: two shadowed directionals, two blooms and two grades. | The `outdoor-day` v0 expansion adds an HDRI, a "look key light" directional at 3.0 with shadow, colorGrade, AO and bloom (looks.ts:291-350, lookPresets.ts:64, 112-125). main.ts:229-256 adds another shadowed directional at 1.9 from a different direction, plus bloom and colorGrade. Only the no-HDRI fill is deduplicated. | Read `app.diagnostics()` for the pass and light counts on 4.5.0. Then drop the authored duplicates or move their values into look overrides. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-12 | P2 | The HUD is one `aria-live` region whose text changes every frame. | index.html:162 has `<div id="hud" aria-live="polite">`, and hud.ts rewrites the score and distance text whenever they change. | Put `aria-live` only on the popup container and throttle its announcements. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-13 | P3 | Boot has no error path and does not warm shader programs. | main.ts:1770 is `void bootRoadsterRushAura3D()` with no catch, so a failed boot leaves the "Loading 100%" overlay up with no message. main.ts:1761-1767 marks boot done without `warmPrograms` (checklist R2; `rg warmPrograms` finds no call in this app). | Show an error card on catch, and run `await warmPrograms(auraApp)` before adding `.done`. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-14 | P3 | A dead background write, and one-shot buttons that stay highlighted. | main.ts:891 writes `(auraApp.scene as any).background`. `scene` is a read-only snapshot (runtime.ts:309), so the write has no effect. "Barrel Roll" and "Rocket Send" keep the `.active` class after the action ends (main.ts:1694-1719). | Delete the write. Make the one-shot buttons momentary and blur them after a click. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |

**Enhance**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RR-E1 | P2 | Draw cost is high for a lane runner, and per-frame allocation is heavy. | The live probe measured 868-895 draw calls per frame, and ART-PASS.md:11-13 records 788-899 with "no instancing of lane dashes/poles". Each frame, main.ts:1333-1335 and 1453-1455 run filter+sort on new arrays, main.ts:1554-1588 publishes a full beacon object, and presentation.ts:66-77 copies every vehicle. The 56 dashes, 24 pole parts, 28 bolts and 14 gantry boxes are separate nodes. | Move the dashes, poles, bolts and traffic to `instances.*` with `setInstanceTransforms`, reuse scratch arrays, and publish the beacon every 10th frame. Record draw calls before and after. | done 352bbde2c, 54dec6334, 5c4087761, f47825292 [4.6.0] |
| RR-E2 | P2 | Crashes, landings and boosts get no camera response. | player.ts sets `shakePulse` and `speedLinesAmount` (player.ts:106-108, 260, 436, 457), but `rg` finds no reader in main.ts. The camera is fixed at [0, 3.6, 6.2] (main.ts:220-227). This falls short of checklist M6 (shake, hit-stop). | Drive a small camera offset or FOV kick from these two values, add a short hit-stop on crash, and use a chase camera that lags steering and rises with altitude. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-E3 | P2 | Power-ups differ only by colour, and two pairs of colours are near-identical. | All power-ups are the same 0.95 m sphere (main.ts:115-121, 1527-1541): magnet #ff2a3c and rocket #ff5030 are both red, air #2f7dff and shield #29c6ff are both blue. | Give each kind its own shape or icon billboard, and keep colour as a secondary cue. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-E4 | P3 | Accessibility polish is missing. | hud.css has no `prefers-reduced-motion` rule, yet the plume flickers at about 8.8 Hz (main.ts:1207) and the shield blinks at about 3.5 Hz (main.ts:1289). The pause and mute buttons are 34 px (hud.css:28) and the toolbar buttons 26 px (index.html:82), against the 44 px in checklist U1. The mute state is not saved. | Honour reduced motion, make touch targets at least 44 px, and save the mute state. | done 352bbde2c [4.6.0] |
| RR-E5 | P3 | Time of day changes only the sky and ground, and the HUD has no speed readout. | ART-PASS.md:13 says "scene lighting does not change with time of day". Speed ramps from 26 to 64 m/s (constants.ts:43-44) with no gauge on screen. | Tie the light intensity and colour to `timeOfDay`. Add a speed gauge and a next-milestone bar. | done 352bbde2c, 4ae802d44 (in part: speed gauge and distance bar added; light still does not follow time of day, the game does not yet call `app.lighting.setEnvironmentIntensity`, which E2 made live in 7e3b36b5d) [4.6.0] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- No test checks that the renderer draws what the simulation holds. Extract the slot assignment in `syncRoadsterWorld` as a pure function, replay seeds for 90 s, and assert that every vehicle, sky car, bolt and gantry within 120 m ahead has a node of its own type (covers RR-1, RR-2 and RR-4). The browser specs step frames manually and never reach the 20-60 s density where the pools overflow.
- There is no facing check for traffic (M15) and no check of `AudioContext.state` after the first keydown (RR-3, RR-5).
- No 4.5.0 frame exists. Recapture title, early, key-ramp, mid and results on the remote runner, and add this route to the steady-state luma harness with an AO-on/AO-off pair and a `?aura3d-render=production` bloom comparison (LIGHT-02, LIGHT-06, RP-05, RH-05).
- All existing captures are from software WebGL2; there is no real-GPU frame.
- RH-06: this page loads the shared 2.6 MB `aura-assets-BECYe7eh.js` catalog, many of whose URLs return 404. Switch to the per-app manifest.
- The probe saw one held KeyD move the car two lanes, although input.ts ignores `e.repeat`. This is likely a Prism key-hold artifact; recheck on a real keyboard.

**Dropped on verification**

- RP-04 (a bad `?aura3d-render=` value stops the mount): this is an engine-wide debug-URL issue with no game-specific effect, so it belongs in the release section.
- LIGHT-05 (hemisphere dropped): kept in the 4.5.0 impact note instead of the fix table, because the behaviour is unchanged from 4.3.7 and the fix is in the engine.

## 03. Thermal Drift (`heatsink`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-heatsink/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-heatsink` · **Genre:** racing

**State:** The game loads and renders on production. A Prism cloud browser run (SwiftShader software GL) logged 0 console errors and 0 failed requests out of 72 resources. Mean luma was 87.5 at t≈136 s and 92.8 after about 20 s of racing, with 0.1% dark and 0% clipped pixels. Space (fire) and Shift (afterburner) both respond, and the lap timer and sectors advance. As a game it is incomplete. The race never ends, the player cannot take damage or lose, the race position is cosmetic, and phones get no controls. Health: degraded.

**4.5.0 impact:** The release changed only `package.json` in this app (`git diff --stat v4.3.7 v4.5.0 -- apps/showcase-heatsink`: 1 file, 1 line). The authored-lights-only change and the no-HDRI neutral fill do not apply, because `looks.preset("outdoor-day")` (main.ts:600) brings an HDRI. Two engine changes do reach the game. The look's ambient-occlusion pass now reads real depth, and bloom now runs through the fused 'separable' path. No frame of this game has been captured on 4.5.0, so the size of either change is unknown. The authored hemisphere light (main.ts:614, 0.88) is still dropped next to the HDRI environment, as it was on 4.3.7.

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- The game has no unit or replay tests. `tests/unit/heatsink-port/*` covers Ion Circuit (examples/heatsink-port), not this app. The sim lives inside the `onFrame` closure of a 2,013-line main.ts and cannot be imported.
- The final screens date from commit d658eb2a1 (2026-10-25). The game has no capture on 4.5.0 (RH-05), no 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state luma (LIGHT-02), no AO on/off pair (LIGHT-06), and no fused-vs-production bloom diff (RP-05).
- The exposure-parity reading (evidence/exposure-parity/frames/scenes.json, 22.904 before and after) came from a 9 s boot. The final-screen ready times were 41-99 s, so that frame was probably the loading overlay and does not count as evidence.
- The 4.5.0 draw-call figure (1121) comes from SwiftShader, where measured rAF was 0.24 fps. No real-GPU frame time exists.

**Dropped on verification**

- Probe "aria status reads 'POS 4//12'": the source does not support it. The chip is `P` + `#h-pos` + `#h-posof` ("/12") (index.html:867), and main.ts:1882 writes a single "/". No source adds a second slash.
- Probe "diagnostics fps 60 vs measured 0.24": this is an engine diagnostics and SwiftShader artifact, not a defect in this game. It is noted under evidence gaps only.
- heatsink-C18 and heatsink-C19 were merged into the evidence gaps above.

## 04. Grid Dispatch (`teleoperator`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-teleoperator/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-teleoperator` · **Genre:** racing

**State:** The game loads and renders on production. The live probe (Prism, SwiftShader software GL) saw no console errors, no failed requests out of 60 resources, and a bright daytime frame: mean luma 106-108, 0-0.7% dark pixels, 0% clipped, about 97 coarse colours. Keyboard input works: holding W raised speed from 11.3 to 12.9 m/s. Nothing is P0. The real problems are in the game itself. The fare loop needs no skill, the car is drawn on the centre line, phones get no controls, and two camera modes sit inside most of the cars.

**4.5.0 impact:** The scene uses `looks.preset("outdoor-day")`, which has an HDRI, plus authored ambient, hemisphere, directional and point lights, bloom, vignette and colorGrade. That keeps it on the HDR post chain and the explicit-environment lighting branch, so the "authored lights only", no-HDRI fill and direct-path changes do not apply. The changes that do apply are separable bloom fusion, real-depth AO from the look preset, and the cascade skip. None of them has been measured on this game. The last frames were taken on 2026-10-25, before the 4.5.0 engine. The authored hemisphere light (`src/main.ts:560-565`) is still silently dropped, as it was on 4.3.7. No primitive option triggers OPTION_IGNORED. Health on 4.5.0 is unproven but nothing is visibly broken.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | P1 | Pickup and dropoff fire in any lane and at any speed, so the fare loop plays itself in manual mode | `src/main.ts:2462` `distToBeacon = Math.max(0, playerZ - beaconWorldZ)` is the only gate, and it has no `playerX` term. Boarding fires at `:2475` (`< 4.2`) and alighting at `:2506`. Manual speed comes only from `:2560-2582`, which has a 9.5 m/s coasting floor, so `targetSpeedMps = 0` is ignored. Once the car passes the beacon, `distToBeacon` clamps to 0 and the trigger always fires. That means the "passed the beacon" respawn at `:2584` can never run. | Gate on `abs(playerX - (CURB_BEACON_X - 0.9)) < 1.2` and `playerSpeedMps < 2` in MAN mode. Show a "slow down / move to kerb" hint. Make a missed pass respawn the beacon with a penalty. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |
| C2 | P1 | Road strips ignore the lateral world shift, so the car is drawn on the double yellow line | `rx = wx - playerX*0.72` (`:2670`) moves dashes, buildings, traffic and the car (`:2675`). `sf-avenue-asphalt`, `sf-center-yellow-l/r`, the cable rails, curbs and sidewalks (`:610-698`) are never referenced again (grep finds no per-frame update). In the right lane (x=3.4) the car is drawn at view x 0.95 m, and the yellow line sits fixed at 0.18 m. Visible in `evidence/teleoperator/screens/final/1280-04-mid.jpg` and `1280-05-modes.jpg`. | Apply `rx()` to every road-strip node each frame, or drop the 0.72 parallax and move the camera. Recapture afterwards. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |
| C3 | P1 | Phones get no driving controls, and the default mode is manual | The only driving input is the window `keydown`/`keyup` listeners (`:1780`, `:1822`). There are no touch, pointer or gamepad handlers. `autopilotLevel` is "MAN" unless `?autoplay=1` (`:1529-1530`). On a phone the car coasts at 9.5 m/s with no steering, and with C1 it still completes fares. | Add a touch layer with steer zones and throttle/brake pads (44 px targets), or default to L4/L5 when there is no keyboard. Add a 390x844 touch spec on the remote runner. | done cacde6cd8, 1008195e9 [4.6.0] |
| C4 | P1 | Roof LiDAR and Bumper cameras sit inside four of the five cars | Only the default car has `cams` (`:271`). The others fall back to roof `{y:1.45,z:5.2}` and bumper `{y:2.15,z:7.4}` (`:2665-2666`). The camera is fixed at z 6.5 (`:531`) and the car is drawn at z `-0.8 + camOff.z` (`:2675`). In bumper mode the car's centre is at z 6.6, 0.1 m from the camera, so the camera is inside any car longer than 0.2 m. `evidence/teleoperator/art-pass/README.md` says these modes were not checked. | Derive the roof and bumper offsets per car from its fitted bounds, or use `createCameraClearance`. Capture all four camera modes for all five cars. | done cacde6cd8, 83395e1d9, e58291ae6 [4.6.0] |
| C5 | P1 | Scripted peers and live traffic drive through each other | Peers sit at laneX -3.2, -3.2 and -7.6 (`:1595`, `:1633`, `:1652`), which are the oncoming traffic lanes `AVENUE_LANES_X[0..1]` (`:482`, `:1729`). Peers have no collider and traffic does no avoidance. Peers wrap from -13 m to -64 m in one frame (`:2830`). | Add peers to the traffic contact and recovery sets, or reserve lane slots for them. Replace the wrap teleport with an off-screen respawn. | done cacde6cd8, 22cdc89a4 [4.6.0] |
| C6 | P1 | A player-visible toast shows the original game's domain, and the package description names real brands | `:2284` `showToast("Invite Link Copied", "https://teleoperator.mindblown.ai/?room=SF-401")`. The live bundle contains this string. There is no `navigator.clipboard` call, so nothing is copied. `package.json` description: "1:1 3D port of Teleoperator (teleoperator.mindblown.ai) ... Waymo Jaguar I-PACE, Tesla Cybercab, Cybertruck FSD, Corvette ZR1X, SpaceX Roadster". Ids and keys (`waymo`, `WAYMO_FARES`, `teleopCybertruck`) break checklist G2/N1. | Copy the real invite URL and show it. Remove the mindblown URL. Rewrite the description. Rename ids, fares and asset keys to generic names and keep aliases for route-health. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |
| C8 | P2 | Weather presets leave the lighting at noon, and "Night" replaces the scene's `background()` method with a string | `setWeatherPreset` (`:2135-2195`) swaps only the materials of the sky box, sun, ground and water. The lights at `:559-582` never change. `:2184` assigns `scene.background = cfg.sky`, but `background` is a method (`packages/engine/src/agent-api/nodes/scene.ts:99`). The clear colour never updates, and any later `background(...)` call would throw. | Keep handles to the lights and change them per preset, or switch the look preset. Enable fog for the fog preset. Delete the cast assignment. | done cacde6cd8, 70254121b, e58291ae6 [4.6.0] |
| C9 | P2 | Keys stick after focus loss, and Space or the arrows activate the focused dock button | `keysHeld` is cleared only on keyup (`:1822`). Ignore keydown events whose target is a button. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |

| RH-06 | P2 | The game bundles the 2.6 MB root asset catalog, and many of its URLs are 404 | `src/main.ts:15` imports `../../../src/aura-assets`. The shared chunk `aura-assets-BECYe7eh.js` is 2,619,707 bytes (361 KB gzip), and 522 of the URLs checked in it return 404. | Import only the app's own assets and prune the root manifest. | done cacde6cd8 (game), 4f19c980c, bdbd5a4a6 (engine or shared) [4.6.0] |
| RP-04 | P3 | A typo in `?aura3d-render=` or `?aura3d-bloom-fusion=` stops the game mounting (shared engine issue) | `renderPath.ts:138-143` and `rendererOptions.ts:266-273` throw inside `startProductionRender`. | Warn and fall back to the default for URL-sourced values. | done 0c563444e [4.6.0] |

**Enhance**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C7 | P2 | Scoring has no stakes and the shift never ends. KeyF and Next Fare pay a full fare with no driving. | The tip is a constant 6.5 or 9.0 (`:2517`), whatever the comfort score or collisions. The summary always shows five stars (`:2026`). TIME LEFT reaching 00:00 does nothing. The "near-miss multiplier" promised at `:2040` has no code. Live probe: four KeyF presses took rides from 16 to 18 and earnings from $1494 to $1568, with no beacon reached (`:1807`, `:2049-2074`). | Make the tip depend on time, comfort and collisions, and reset comfort for each ride. Add a timed 5-fare shift with a results screen, Retry and a best score. Make Next Fare a skip that pays nothing. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |
| C11 | P2 | Nameplate placement forces layout several times per frame | `placeNameplate` (`:1670-1710`) reads `getComputedStyle` and `getBoundingClientRect` right after the previous peer's transform write (`:2863`), and does this 5 times per frame. Each frame also allocates the QA object with 8 closures (`:2983-3017`) and makes about 20 `getElementById` calls. | Batch all reads before any writes, build the QA object once, and cache element refs. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |
| C12 | P2 | About 630 draw calls, partly from roughly 150 repeated primitives that could be instanced | The CHANGELOG art-pass entry records draw calls going from 691 to 630. Dashes, lamps, trees, crosswalks and sparks are separate nodes (`:873-1020`, `:1150-1231`), including 9 auxiliary player primitives that are never shown. | Move them to `instances.*` with `setInstanceTransforms`, delete the hidden nodes, and record `app.diagnostics()` before and after. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |
| C17 | P2 | Accessibility gaps: no reduced-motion handling, small targets, and dead toggles | There is no `prefers-reduced-motion` check, so shake, beacon pulse and shimmer always run (`:1746`, `:2760`, `:2810`). Dock buttons are 26 px high (`index.html:963`), against the checklist's 44 px. ABS, TCS and ESP are `<i>` elements with click handlers and no role or tabindex (`index.html:1456-1458`). `absOn` and `tcsOn` are toggled (`:2260-2267`) but nothing reads them. | Honour reduced motion, use 44 px targets on phones, and make the toggles real buttons wired to handling, or remove them. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |
| C18 | P2 | The only sound is a collision cue, and there is no mute | `COLLISION_CUES` (`:24-26`) is the only cue bank, and it is created with `muteKey: false` (`:1747`). | Add pickup, dropoff and earnings cues, an engine loop pitched by speed, and a mute key and button. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |
| C19 | P3 | Assist levels and HUD labels do not match the behaviour | L3 and L4 use the same autonomous branch as L5 (`:2587-2603`). `#gmode` shows the L5 label in MAN mode (`:2082-2083`). An "R" gear appears with no reverse (`:2918`). The drift overlay has no physics. Red lights are ignored, and the player can drive the oncoming lanes (x down to -9.2, `:2563`) with no cue. | Make each assist level behave differently, derive the label from the level, and remove or implement R and drift. Make red lights matter and add a wrong-way warning. | done cacde6cd8, 22cdc89a4 [4.6.0] |
| C20 | P3 | The game does not use the fixed-step loop, `warmPrograms`, `installQaHooks` or the safety-net kits | The sim integrates a clamped variable `dt` inside `onFrame` (`:2451-2456`). The boot screen is dismissed after one step (`:3020-3021`). | Adopt `createFixedStepLoop`, await `warmPrograms` before hiding boot, and use `installQaHooks`/`installSafetyNet`. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |
| P-01 | P3 | Career stats are invented at boot, and one fare line has a typo | `:1556-1557` set $1,420 and 14 rides on a fresh load. `:402` says "glad a Apex", which should be "an Apex". | Start at zero or load persisted stats. Fix the typo. | done cacde6cd8 [4.6.0] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- No test covers the ride state machine, scoring or the per-car camera offsets. The only teleoperator unit tests are the collision and likeness ones (C21).
- There is no frame from the 4.5.0 engine. The final screens date from 2026-10-25. The results screen was never reached (`meta.json`: "results: not reached"). Ready shows 240 s, "never lit" (RH-05, C21).
- No 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state luma pair exists, and no capture compares AO on and off. The game is not in `tests/browser/game-luma-capture.spec.ts` TARGETS (LIGHT-02, LIGHT-06).
- Bloom fusion has not been diffed against `?aura3d-render=production` (RP-05).
- `docs/upgrade-4.5.0/teleoperator.md` checks are still open (RH-04).
- No 390x844 touch spec, and no camera-mode sweep across the five cars.

**Dropped on verification**

- C22 (enhancement list) was merged into C7, C1, C19 and C3. It adds no separate defect.
- The probe note about the boot text staying in `innerText` was dropped. The boot element is hidden after load, and players never see the text.
- The probe's 0.3 fps was not reported. It reflects SwiftShader software GL, not the game.

## 05. Iron Crown (`iron-crown`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-iron-crown/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-iron-crown` · **Genre:** action

**State:** The game loads on production with no console errors and no failed requests (58 resources, 6 typed fighter assets, 176 draw calls). The probe ran in the Prism cloud browser on SwiftShader software GL. Steady-state mean luma was 56.0 to 56.3, with 14 to 15% of pixels below luma 12, 0% clipped, and the centre brighter than the border (77 to 82 vs 53). Quick Bout starts on its own. Keyboard movement and guard work, and the HUD clock and hit counters update. At 0.12 to 0.29 fps the probe never reached a round end. On desktop the game plays. On phones it is broken, because the touch Left/Right buttons do nothing (IC-01).

**4.5.0 impact:** The release changed only the version line in `apps/showcase-iron-crown/package.json` (`git diff --stat v4.3.7 v4.5.0 -- apps/showcase-iron-crown`: 1 file, 1+/1-), so every change reaches the game through the engine. Most of those engine changes leave it alone. Every stage look has either an HDRI or the explicit night-city neutral fill. No `lights.ambient` is authored. Bloom, vignette and contactShadows keep it on the HDR chain, and every primitive option is in `PRIMITIVE_OPTION_KEYS`. Three engine changes do alter the frames, and none of them has been captured on 4.5.0: contact shadows are now a real mask (`main.ts:239`), AO now reads real depth, and Neo-Tokyo is +6.4% brighter (31.4 to 33.4, `evidence/games450/iron-crown-neo-tokyo.md`). This needs a recapture, not code changes.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IC-01 | P1 | Touch Left/Right buttons drive actions that do not exist, so phone players cannot walk. | `src/main.ts:313-314` binds `left: "left", right: "right"`. The declared actions are `moveLeft`/`moveRight` (`main.ts:98-99`). `TouchControls.ts:156,163` calls `input.setAction(action)` with the bound name. `GameRuntime.ts:2111-2119` adds an override only for that exact name, and `resolveAxisRaw` reads `isActionHeld(positive/negative)` (`GameRuntime.ts:1836`), so `moveX` never changes. The live bundle `showcase-iron-crown-BIhR673D.js` contains `left:"left",right:"right"`. There are no touch bindings for throw, crouch, dash or pause. | Bind `left: "moveLeft", right: "moveRight"`, and add throw and dash buttons. Add a game-check that every touch binding value is a declared action. | done afc12dee9, 8561efb27, 79c26b1de [4.6.0] |
| IC-02 | P1 | The player's special ignores the SUPER meter and can be used at 0 meter. | `main.ts:853` gates only `f.side === "cpu" && asnap.meter < METER_MAX`. README.md:17 says "specials cost 100", and README.md:51 says "Special (needs 100 meter)". The engine's `beginAttack` has no meter check. | Gate both sides and play the registered but unused `denied` cue on refusal. Add a unit test. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-03 | P2 | Tanky fighters (Boulder defense 0.78, Sovereign 0.82) are KO'd by raw damage before the defense reduction is applied. | The engine sets `knockedOut = true` and pushes a knockout event when raw damage reaches 0 HP (`GameRuntime.ts:3810-3822`). The app then corrects health by `tSnap.health - (effective - raw)` (`main.ts:1022-1025`). `setActor` ORs `knockedOut` and cannot clear it (`GameRuntime.ts:2855-2859`), and the knockout event still fires `onKnockout` (`main.ts:1068-1069`). Example: Boulder at 10 HP takes a raw-12 heavy, which is 9.36 after defense. He should survive on 0.64 HP. Instead he is KO'd, and his bar shows 2.64 HP at K.O. Stats: `fighters.ts:229,268`. | Scale the move damage by the target's defense before `beginAttack`, or add a per-actor damage multiplier to the engine. Remove the after-the-fact correction. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-04 | P2 | The bout-result timer is never cancelled. A stale result card can appear, and a Ladder rung can be skipped. | `main.ts:1101` calls `window.setTimeout(() => showBoutResult(p1Won), 1600)` and keeps no handle. It is the only timer in the file. The mode buttons start a new bout in any phase (`main.ts:744-752`); only `selectStage` checks for `boutEnd` (`:758`). After a Ladder win, clicking Crown Ladder within 1.6 s lets the stale callback reach `advanceLadder(l)` (`main.ts:1149-1151`) on the new ladder. | Store the timeout id and a bout serial number. Clear the timeout in `configureBout`, and return early from `showBoutResult` if the serial changed or the phase is no longer `boutEnd`. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-05 | P2 | A gamepad cannot leave the pause menu. | GamepadStart opens pause through `input.pressed("pause")` in onFrame (`main.ts:108,1397-1399`). `setPaused(true)` pauses the app loop. The only resume paths are the Escape keydown (`main.ts:468-471`) and the HUD buttons (`:431-433`). `rg getGamepads src` returns nothing. | While paused, poll `navigator.getGamepads()` in a small rAF loop and resume on Start or B. Focus `#pause-resume` when the menu opens. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-06 | P2 | Holding away from the opponent blocks in place, so the player can never walk backwards. | `main.ts:921-924` sets `guard = input.held("guard") \|\| holdingAway`, and `main.ts:987` blocks movement while `guardIntent` is set. Shift-dash always goes toward the foe (`main.ts:927`). The probe confirmed that holding KeyA puts P1 in clip `guard` without moving. The CPU walks back freely, so it has a spacing tool the player lacks. | Let hold-back walk back at about 60% speed while guarding. Keep Q as the stand-still block. Make Shift + away a back-dash. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-07 | P2 | Runtime hit effects are still off: no hit or block sparks, aura bursts, shockwaves or dash trails draw. | `main.ts:358-359` sets `(app as any).effects.runtime = "off"` "until the floating-dot report is root-caused" (turned off in 4.3.5, CHANGELOG line 60). Calls such as `runtimeEffects.auraBurst` and `ringShockwave` at `main.ts:859-860` therefore draw nothing. package.json promises "full combat juice". | Run the real-GPU `?vfx=on&vfxdebug=1` check that 4.3.5 asked for. Remove the override if `VFX_LINGERING_PARTICLES` stays clean; otherwise enable the bounded hitSpark and blockSpark presets one at a time. | not done: needs a real-GPU check (`?vfx=on&vfxdebug=1`); the remote runner is software GL, so the override stays; owner runs `tools/real-gpu/run.sh` |
| IC-08 | P3 | The KO slow-down is applied twice and ignores reduced motion. | `main.ts:1079` sets `state.slowmo = reducedMotion ? 1 : 0.28`, but `main.ts:1080` always calls `session.slowMo(0.28, 1400)`. `simDt = dt * s.slowmo` (`main.ts:1428`) multiplies on top of the session time scale, so the first KO frames run at about 0.08x, and reduced-motion users still get the 1.4 s slow-down. | Use only `session.slowMo`, and skip it when reduced motion is on. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-09 | P3 | Player Studio palettes probably replace the whole fighter texture with a flat colour. Not seen in a frame yet. | `fighters.ts:452-462` passes `material.pbr({ color: tint.color, ... })`. Under the QR materials flag, `TypedGLBActor.setTint` lowers this to `{ baseColorReplace, replaceTextures: true }` (`TypedGLBActor.ts:321-327`). The game enables qualityRebuild `['game']` (`main.ts:345`). | Use a multiply tint that keeps the textures, and capture one frame per swatch. | not a defect: `model(asset, { material })` lowers to `baseColorMultiply`, which keeps the base-colour textures; `setTint` is a legacy seam this path does not use (remote swatches keep belt, skin and face detail) |

**Enhance**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IC-10 | P2 | Fighter GLBs total about 36 MB of uncompressed PNG textures, and the phone capture took 44 s to become ready. | Each of the six fighter GLBs is 4.7 to 7.6 MB, of which 2.1 to 5.0 MB is PNG, with no `KHR_texture_basisu`. `evidence/iron-crown/screens/final/meta.json`: 390 profile ready in 44 s, 1280 profile in 4 s. `fightingGame.setScene` reloads the GLBs whenever the fighter or tint changes (`main.ts:588-610`). | Convert the atlases to KTX2 (or 2k WebP base colour). Preload only the two fighters in the bout, and reuse loaded GLBs across `setScene`. | done 2df0f6014 [4.6.0] |
| IC-11 | P2 | Every 60 Hz step allocates repeatedly: about 15 full combat snapshots, an evidence object and a copy of the whole input log, which grows without limit. | `snap()` (`main.ts:828-831`) always calls `combat.snapshot()`, so the cache it writes never saves a call. It runs from `isAttacking` (`:838`), `desiredClip` (`:1228`), `main.ts:1395` and more. `main.ts:1432` calls `input.recorded().slice(-8)`, and `recorded()` returns `[...replayEvents]` (`GameRuntime.ts:2122`), which only grows. `rg clearReplay src` finds no calls. | Take one snapshot per step and pass it down. Publish evidence every 30 frames or on demand. Read the input trail through a cursor, and call `input.clearReplay()` between bouts. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-12 | P3 | The neutral stance and crouch both use the `Block1` clip, and throw uses a combo clip. | `fighters.ts:111,115` map idle and crouch to `Block1` (`standIn: true`). This was done on purpose (comment at `:109-110`). The live `clipStandIns` reads idle, crouch → Block1, dash → Walk_Fight_Forward, throw → Triple_Combo_Attack and win → Regular_Jump. | Add a fighting-idle, a crouch and a throw clip to the rig (Meshy animate), and keep `Block1` for guard only. | done 296d7b781 [4.6.2] |
| IC-13 | P3 | The CPU repeats an attack for its whole think cooldown, and `techChance` is never used. | `ai.ts:165-169` sets `holdT` only for non-attack decisions, so while the cooldown runs, `think()` keeps returning the attack intent each frame. One decision therefore becomes 2 to 3 jabs. `rg tryTech src` finds only its definition (`ai.ts:74,171`). In the probe the CPU landed 5 hits and 3 throws to 0 in about 15 s of sim, though at software-GL speed. | Return the attack only once per decision. Call `brain.tryTech()` when a player throw becomes active. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-14 | P3 | Onboarding and menus are incomplete. | The boot hint (`index.html:236`) leaves out W jump, Shift dash, Q or hold-back block, and the SUPER rule. `openSheet` is only called for "versus" (`main.ts:1215`), so the `#menu-ladder`, `#menu-studio` and `#menu-mode` sheets in index.html are never shown. Each Player Studio click cycles the palette and restarts the bout (`main.ts:747-751`). Bindings are hard-coded, with no remap screen. | Open the existing sheets from the mode buttons. Generate the hint from `inputOptions`. Add a controls sheet with rebinding saved to localStorage. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-15 | P3 | Upper-body attack layering is declared but not used. Punches replace the leg pose. | `fighters.ts:390-393` declares an `upper-body` layer, but playback is one full-body `setAnimation` per fighter (`main.ts:1305-1310`). 4.5.0 made `playLayer(..., {mask:{humanoid:"upper-body"}})` work. | Play attack clips on an upper-body layer over the locomotion clip, and check with a filmstrip that the feet stay planted. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-16 | P3 | The throne pedestal reads as a pale placeholder box in front of the chair. | `evidence/iron-crown/screens/final/1280-03-action.jpg`. Pedestal at `[0,0,-2.5]` and chair at z -3.2 (`stages.ts:455-461`). | Give the pedestal a stone or gold material, or drop it, and seat the crown on its top. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-17 | P3 | Every strike plays the same hit sound, and there is no music or crowd bed. | `main.ts:1034-1035` always cues `hit`. A lost bout cues `ko` again (`main.ts:1099`). The `sound.cues` block (`main.ts:322-336`) has no ambience. | Add hit cues keyed by `ev.moveId`, a lose stinger and a crowd loop for each stage. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |
| IC-18 | P3 | The combo counter counts any hits within 1.35 s, not true combos. | `main.ts:1042-1045` adds to the count on every hit, whether or not the target recovered from hitstun in between. | Add to the count only if the target was still in hitstun when hit. | done afc12dee9 [4.6.0] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- There are no unit tests for meter gating, the defense KO, ladder advance, round, time-up or draw logic, or touch bindings. The existing tests cover only faces, restyle, stage props and the quick bar (`tests/unit/iron-crown/`).
- The final screens date from 2026-10-25 (commit 55bc3de3a), before 4.5.0's real contact-shadow mask, real-depth AO and particle fix. `06-results` is "not reached" at both 1280 and 390, and the 390 mid frame still shows pre-fix floating gold orbs. Retake on 4.5.0 for Crown Arena and Neo-Tokyo, including a results frame, and record `diagnostics().drawCalls`.
- The Neo-Tokyo luma evidence relies on `/tmp/games450-b1-final/games450-capture.spec.ts`, which is not in the repo. `tests/browser/game-luma-capture.spec.ts` has no iron-crown target.
- The +6.4% Neo-Tokyo lift falls outside the CHANGELOG's "within about 5% inside the games" claim (`CHANGELOG.md:32`).
- Nobody has compared fused bloom (`separable`) against `?aura3d-render=production` for this game.
- Contact shadows on the spot-lit night stages modulate only the weak night key light (night-city key 0.7). No frame shows whether the 4.5.0 mask is visible here.
- The engine reports `fps 60` in diagnostics while the probe measured 0.12 to 0.29 fps of rAF. The warning that `step()` ran before the renderer mounted persists. This is an engine diagnostics issue, not a game-code issue.
- The page loads the shared 2.6 MB `aura-assets-BECYe7eh.js` catalog through `../../../../src/aura-assets` (`src/game/stages.ts:2`). Of the catalog URLs that were checked, 522 return 404.

**Dropped on verification**

- C12 "techChance dead code" was kept, but merged into IC-13 and held at P3. The probe's lopsided score comes from software GL and is not proof of an AI bug.
- The probe item "idle/crouch use Block1" was downgraded from P2 to P3 (IC-12). The stand-in is a deliberate, commented choice (`fighters.ts:109-110`) and is not broken.
- RP-04 (a URL-option typo stops the mount) is an engine-wide debug-switch robustness issue. It does not affect normal Iron Crown players, so it is not listed as a game defect.

## 06. Pip's Big Leap (`pips-big-leap`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-pips-big-leap/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-pips-big-leap` · **Genre:** platformer

**State:** The game loads on production with no console errors and no failed requests (65 resources, none >= 400). It reports `data-aura3d-ready=true` on the production runtime. In the Prism cloud browser (SwiftShader software GL, so 0.8 fps is not a real-GPU number) World 1 held a steady mean luma of 163.3-163.4 with 0% black and 0% clipped pixels, and 157.4 after moving. W moved Pip, Space jumped, the timer ran and an acorn was collected. The game plays, but one controller bug soft-locks it: touching any non-respawn hazard or the boss leaves Pip frozen in the `hurt` state for good. Strafing also runs Pip in a circle. Overall health is degraded.

**4.5.0 impact:** The release did not touch game code. `git diff --stat v4.3.7 v4.5.0 -- apps/showcase-pips-big-leap` lists only `package.json` (1 line). Every change comes from the engine. Contact shadows (world.ts:317, all 7 worlds) are now a real screen-space mask. Real-depth AO now reaches every look preset. The w6 `polar-night` look gains an explicit neutral fill at 0.85. Bloom fuses on `auto`. The measured luma shifts are moderate: w1 157.4 to 166.9 (+6.0%) and w6 108.1 to 111.1 (docs/upgrade-4.5.0/pips-big-leap.md:34). The final screens and posters date from 2026-10-25, before the release. The 4.5.0 frames were never recaptured or inspected for contact-shadow or AO artefacts, and bloom fusion was never diffed against the production chain. No regression is proven, but the release is unverified for this game.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| pips-big-leap-C1 | P0 | Touching a non-respawn hazard or the boss locks Pip in `hurt` for good: he cannot move or jump. | `damage()` sets `p.state="hurt"` (player.ts:391). `hurtTimer` is decremented (player.ts:117) but never read. Landing skips hurt (player.ts:277, 280), and `busy` includes hurt (player.ts:115), so `wantMove` and jumps stay off. Boss contact calls the same `damage(...,"hit")` (player.ts:317). The hazards without `respawn` include thorns, splinters, hail, embers and void-1 across worlds.ts. Re-verified: importing player.ts with `node --experimental-strip-types`, flat floor, one `respawn:false` hazard, forward held, jump every 30 frames: `hurt at 15`, then `hurt z 0.95 hearts 2 g true` at frames 100 through 600. The only ways out are falling below `lowerBound`, a spring, dying, or Pause > Restart. Autoplay jumps over hazards, so captures never reach this state. | After the timers tick: `if (p.state==="hurt" && p.hurtTimer<=0) p.state = p.grounded ? "grounded" : "air"`. Add a unit test that moving works again within 0.6 s of a hit. | done 62c075312 [4.6.0] |
| pips-big-leap-C2 | P1 | Holding A/D (strafe) runs Pip in a circle about 2.3 m across instead of sideways. | Input is camera-relative (player.ts:121-124), and `camYaw` chases `yaw` at 2.2/s (player.ts:151-154), so the strafe axis keeps rotating. Re-verified by sim, `moveX=1` for 4 s: (x,z) -2.00,-1.16 -> -1.11,-3.37 -> 1.15,-2.63 -> 0.56,-0.32 -> -1.78,-0.77..., yaw -2.66 to -13.21 rad. The footer advertises WASD/arrows. | Chase camYaw only when `moveZ > 0.3`, or chase the velocity heading slowly. Add camera orbit on mouse drag or right stick. Test: holding D for 2 s moves Pip more than 5 m camera-right. | done 62c075312, 012c0e706 [4.6.0] |
| pips-big-leap-C3 | P2 | On descending lifts Pip leaves the platform every few frames, so `land` fires repeatedly (FOV punch, fall clip). | player.ts:256 carries vertical motion only `if (s.vy > 0)`. Re-verified with a sine lift (amplitude 2.1, period 3.4, the w4 geyser values) over 408 frames: 169 airborne frames, 25 `land` events. Each `land` triggers `juice("land")`, a 0.5 deg punch (main.ts:175). Vertical movers: w2 blade-lane, w3 fan-lift, w4 geyser-1, w5 lift-1. | Apply `p.y += s.vy*dt` in both directions while grounded on `s`, or keep a 0.1 m ground probe on the previous `groundId`. | done 62c075312 [4.6.0] |
| pips-big-leap-C6 | P2 | The Sun-Gate lock icon disappears while the gate is still locked. | The seed branch hides `gateLock` once seeds >= `gateSeeds`, even while the boss is alive and `gateUnlocked` is false (main.ts:362-364). The boss-down branch hides it even with too few seeds (main.ts:410-411). | Hide `gateLock` only when `run.gateUnlocked` becomes true. | done 012c0e706 [4.6.0] |
| pips-big-leap-C9 | P2 | Atlas and Studio opened from Pause unpause the sim, so platforms, hazards and the trial timer keep running behind the overlay. | `bind("pbl-pause-atlas", () => { setPaused(false); showAtlas(true); })`, and the same for Studio (main.ts:860-861). `onFrame` only stops on `run.paused` (main.ts:902). | Treat any visible overlay as paused in `onFrame`, and leave `run.paused` true when opening overlays from Pause. | done 012c0e706 [4.6.0] |
| pips-big-leap-C8 | P2 | Pause, Restart, Atlas and Studio cannot be reached on touch. Pause is bound to Escape only. | `pause: ["Escape"]` (main.ts:137). The touch bindings map only move, jump, dash and attack (main.ts:145-154). The pause toggle exists only in the keydown handler (main.ts:880-893). | Add a 44 px HUD pause button that calls `setPaused(true)`, and bind KeyP. | done 012c0e706 [4.6.0] |
