**Tests and evidence gaps**

- No sim unit tests exist for player.ts, boss.ts, save.ts or the gate logic. The only dedicated spec is `tests/browser/shotsd-pips-big-leap.spec.ts`, a screenshot run, which is how C1-C3 shipped. All three reproduce in a few lines of node against player.ts.
- The final screens (2026-10-25) predate 4.5.0, and `meta.json` records "results: not reached", so there is no completion frame (RH-05).
- The upgrade guide's contact-shadow checks were never ticked: no `contactShadows.passExecuted` reading and no recapture of all 7 worlds (RH-04, C13).
- AO is on through the look preset in every world, and only the w1 and w6 mean luma were measured. No crops of the contact areas (feet, pickups on the ground) were checked (LIGHT-06).
- Bloom fusion `separable` was never diffed against `?aura3d-render=production` for this game (RP-05).
- The live `geometryCertification` failure (13.264 s traversal < 30 s minimum, `assetBoundLevel=false`, all `presentationCounts` 0) means the asset-bound certification is not met on the live build. Do not claim it.
- A typo in `?aura3d-render=` stops the mount (RP-04, engine-wide, P3).

**Dropped on verification**

- C13 (4.5.0 contact-shadow regression, P2): the change was measured for this game (w1 +6.0%, w6 108.1 to 111.1 luma, docs/upgrade-4.5.0/pips-big-leap.md:34), and the live frame is not dark or clipped. No regression is shown, so the item was merged into the evidence gaps above.
- C12 (test gap) was merged into "Tests and evidence gaps" rather than listed as a defect.
- Live-probe jumpsLeft 1 to 0 on first landing: this affects the evidence snapshot only, with no player effect.

## 07. Aura Hoops 3D (`aura-hoops-3d`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-aura-hoops-3d/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-aura-hoops-3d` · **Genre:** sports

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. In the Prism cloud browser (SwiftShader software GL, about 0.2-0.5 fps) it showed 0 console errors, 0 failed requests out of 65 resources, `data-aura3d-ready=true` and 326 draw calls. Mean luma was 80.7, 86.1 and 100.7 at t=52, 463 and 859 s, with 0% black and 0% clipped pixels. Keyboard input works: a held shot released, the CPU rebounded and dunked, and the check, live, pass, loose and check cycle ran. Desktop play is healthy. Touch play is broken because the touch controls are mounted inside a hidden container. The rules have several input and possession defects that make the shot meter trivial and dead balls go to the wrong team.

**4.5.0 impact:** The game source did not change (`git diff --stat v4.3.7 v4.5.0 -- apps/showcase-aura-hoops-3d` shows only `package.json`, 1 line). All 4.5.0 changes reach Hoops through the engine:
- `effects.contactShadows` (main.ts:783) is now a real mask on the shadow-casting key light that every look carries.
- The look's ambient occlusion now reads real depth.
- Shanghai (`night-city`) and Orbit (`neon-arcade`) get the explicit neutral fill.

The upgrade guide records remote steady-state luma: Shanghai 53.5 to 55.6 (+3.9%), Orbit 109.4 to 113.3 (+3.6%), Rucker 82.8 to about 88 (+6.3%) (`docs/upgrade-4.5.0/aura-hoops-3d.md`, section 2). The two night courts are inside the CHANGELOG's 5% target and Rucker is slightly outside it. Manila, Athens and the sixth court were not measured, no 4.5.0 frames are committed, and the contact mask was never inspected visually. The change looks acceptable, but the release has no receipt for these courts. Debug URL switches (`?aura3d-render=`, `?aura3d-bloom-fusion=`) now throw on a typo and stop the mount, which affects every game.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| aura-hoops-3d-C1 | P0 | Phones cannot play because the touch controls are mounted inside a container that CSS always hides. | `index.html:761-763` `.touch-controls { display: none; }`, with no media-query override (the only `touch-controls` rule in the file). `index.html:958` `<div class="touch-controls" id="touch">`. `main.ts:1198-1206` mounts `mountTouchControls(..., { root: document.getElementById("touch") })`. `packages/game/src/touch/TouchControls.ts:128-129` appends the host into `deps.root`, so its `a3g-hidden` toggle cannot show a child of a `display:none` parent. Autopilot is off by default, so a touch user's player stands still. The 390x844 evidence frames all ran with AUTO: ON. | Remove the rule or show `#touch` under `@media (pointer: coarse)`. Add a browser assertion that `.a3g-touch` has a non-zero box under coarse-pointer emulation. Add a Rematch/Menu touch button. | done b465166bd, 681b77cba [4.6.0] |
| aura-hoops-3d-C2 | P1 | `buffered()` is used as a one-shot press and never consumed, so each press fires on every frame for 140 ms. As a result Escape can toggle the menu an even number of times (no net change), one crossover stacks about 10 velocity kicks, and E switches defenders repeatedly. | `main.ts:1296-1297` `pressed = inputCtl.pressed(a) \|\| inputCtl.buffered(a) \|\| keysPressed.has(c)`. `GameRuntime.ts:2037-2039` `buffered()` is true while `time - lastPressedAt <= windowMs`, and `main.ts:1215-1236` sets `bufferMs: 140`. `consume()` exists (`GameRuntime.ts:2040`) and the route never calls it. Escape: `main.ts:2896-2898` `classList.toggle("open")`, which runs about 9-10 times per press at 60 Hz. Crossover: `main.ts:2212` `me.vx += sin(lat)*4.6` on each repeated frame, which gives a lateral speed far above the sprint cap. | Use `inputCtl.pressed()` for one-shot actions, or call `inputCtl.consume(action)` once an action is handled. Add a unit test at 60/120/144 Hz asserting one menu toggle and one crossover per press. | done b465166bd [4.6.0] |
| aura-hoops-3d-C3 | P1 | The "SPACE — CHECK BALL" press also opens the shot meter, and a quick release auto-fires a near-certain make from the check spot. | `main.ts:2908-2919`: Space calls `enterLive()`. On the following frames the press is still buffered (C2), so `main.ts:2254-2261` calls `openMeter()`. A release before t=0.48 sets `tapRelease` (`main.ts:2303-2305`) and fires at quality 0.94 (`main.ts:2314-2315`). `aimError` (`shotPhysics.ts:78-82`) at q=0.94, d=8.6 m and skill 0.06 gives sigma 0.034 m against a 0.118 m clearance, so P(make) = 1-exp(-0.118²/(2·0.034²)) ≈ 99.8%. | Consume `shoot` when it checks the ball, or ignore `shoot` for about 0.25 s after `enterLive`. | done b465166bd [4.6.0] |
| aura-hoops-3d-C4 | P1 | A quick tap, or a hold past the meter's wrap, always releases at 0.94. That counts as green, so the shot cannot be blocked, and it beats almost any real timed release. The meter has no skill value. | `main.ts:2303-2316` (tap → 0.94). `main.ts:2310` held quality `1-\|t-0.72\|/(hw*2.2)`. `main.ts:2931-2933` `if (t >= 1) t = 0`, so a late release wraps to t<0.48 and takes the tap path ("brick territory" comment). `main.ts:1552` `inGreen = quality > 0.9`, and `main.ts:1579` allows a block only `!inGreen`. Contested from 1.5 m, the tap still gives sigma ≈ 0.047 m, about a 96% make. | Map an early tap to its real early quality and clamp the meter at 1 (late = brick). Limit unblockability to the perfect window. Unit-test the tap, hold, late and wrap paths. | done b465166bd [4.6.0] |
| aura-hoops-3d-C5 | P1 | A stale `ball.flightT` turns steals and post-dunk loose balls into instant dead balls, which then go to the wrong team. | `flightT` is reset only by the `Object.assign` calls in `releaseShot` (`main.ts:1563-1565`) and `throwPass` (`main.ts:2073-2076`). `startDunk` (`main.ts:1604`) and the steal path (`main.ts:2040-2046`) do not reset it. `main.ts:1667` keeps adding to it while the ball is loose. `main.ts:1735-1737` `flightT > 4` → `enterCheck(servingTeam === "home" ? "away" : "home")`. That ignores who touched the ball last, and after a dunk the scorer is `servingTeam`, so the scored-on team gets the ball. | Reset `flightT` and `trailHist` on every ball state change. Use a separate loose-ball timer and award dead balls against `lastTouchTeam`. | done b465166bd [4.6.0] |
| aura-hoops-3d-C6 | P1 | The take-back rule is cosmetic. The CPU can dunk a defensive rebound immediately, and the player can score without clearing. | `main.ts:2343-2353` sets `mustClear = true`, shows "CPU BALL — TAKE-BACK", then sets `a.aiPlan = "drive"`. `main.ts:1937-1939` `if (dRim < DUNK_RANGE && (a.aiPlan === "drive" \|\| …)) startDunk(a, "POWER JAM")`. `mustClear` is read only at `main.ts:2107` (autopilot) and `main.ts:3018-3027` (prompt/clear check). Neither scoring path checks it. | While `mustClear` is set, give the CPU plan "clear" and block player shots and dunks with a prompt. Otherwise remove the rule and its banners. | done b465166bd [4.6.0] |
| aura-hoops-3d-C8 | P1 | The two teams use the same two athlete bodies. A faint floor-ring colour is the only team cue, which also fails colour-blind players. | `main.ts:630-639` picks the model only by `/[124]$/.test(id)`, with no tint or material override. The team ring is the only team marker (`main.ts:620-624`, opacity 0.42, orange vs blue). `SquadSpec.color/alt` are not used for kits. | Tint jersey and shorts per squad with material overrides and add a shape cue (chevron or numbered ring). | done b465166bd, 05982e025 [4.6.0] |
| aura-hoops-3d-C9 | P1 | Player Studio "APPLY FIT" changes nothing, and the 8 squads are names only. | `game_.fit` is written at `main.ts:3189` and read only by the pills and the evidence (`main.ts:2864`). No material uses it. `homeSquad` is fixed at `SQUADS[0]` (`main.ts:456`) and nothing reassigns it. The squad shoot, dunk and defense stats are unused. Only `awaySquad.speed` affects play (`main.ts:1921`). | Apply the fit to the controlled squad's materials, add a squad picker and feed its stats into aim skill, dunks and steals. Otherwise remove the card and stats. | done b465166bd, 05982e025 [4.6.0] |
| aura-hoops-3d-C7 | P2 | Dunks always score and cannot be contested, so every CPU drive that reaches the rim is two automatic points. | `startDunk` and `finishDunk` (`main.ts:1598-1638`) commit to the arc and always call `awardPoints`, with no block or defender check. Reset the tour when the card starts after an elimination or a title. | done b465166bd [4.6.0] |

| aura-hoops-3d-C20 | P3 | The game allocates objects every frame and builds the court from about 62 separate primitives. | `publishEvidence` (`main.ts:2817-2874`) runs every frame and snapshots up to 48 events. `awayTeam().sort` runs per CPU defender (`main.ts:1980`). The court lines and ticks are separate boxes (`main.ts:511-547`). The live probe measured 326 draw calls. | Publish evidence every 10 frames, cache the team arrays, write the DOM only on change, and draw the ticks with `instances.box`. | done b465166bd [4.6.0] |
| aura-hoops-3d-C22 | P3 | Player-facing enhancements, in priority order. | Follows from C4, C7, C8 and C9. Game over is a 30 s centre message (`main.ts:1454`) and the README says the results screen was never captured. The dunk reuses `rimClank` (`main.ts:1099`) and `goldBall` is never used. | (1) Team kits plus number rings. (2) Early/late/perfect meter feedback. (3) Contested dunks and blocks. (4) A results card with FG%, dunks, steals, Rematch and the tour bracket. (5) A squad picker using SquadSpec. (6) A distinct slam cue and a crowd swell. | done b465166bd, 05982e025 [4.6.0] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- No tests cover the game rules in `main.ts`. `tests/unit/aura-hoops` has 5 files and 20 tests, all passing, covering assets, dribble, hit-stop, locomotion and shot physics. Nothing tests meter quality, input edges at 60/120/144 Hz, dead-ball possession, `mustClear`, scoring, the tour or mode switching, which is how C2-C6 and C13 shipped. `src/hitStop.ts` is dead code, imported nowhere.
- No browser check covers touch play under coarse-pointer emulation (C1). Every 390x844 capture ran with autopilot on.
- No results-screen frame exists, which checklist line 38 requires. `route-health.json` dates from 2026-10-17 and has no draw-call or triangle numbers (checklist line 40).
- The exposure-parity entry has identical before and after means (34.80530789686004), a 404 and a 500 console error, and only the Rucker court.
- The 4.5.0 upgrade guide's checklist is still open (release audit RH-04). The screens and posters predate 4.5.0 (RH-05).
- The live probe ran on SwiftShader at about 0.2-0.5 fps, so its fps and its "release — 0%" shot quality reflect frame starvation, not the game.

**Dropped on verification**

- C12's claim that "no committed number exists for Hoops Shanghai or Orbit after the final fill" is wrong. `docs/upgrade-4.5.0/aura-hoops-3d.md` section 2 records 55.6 and 113.3 on 4.5.0, so C12 is downgraded to P3 and limited to the unmeasured courts and the missing frames.
- The code audit's note that the touch layout uses the `fight` preset is wrong. The route uses `preset: "dpad-4btn"` (`main.ts:1199`). The bindings themselves are fine.

## 08. Aura Diamond 3D (`aura-diamond-3d`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-aura-diamond-3d/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-aura-diamond-3d` · **Genre:** sports

**State:** The page loads on production (`data-aura3d-ready=true`, 71 resources, none failed, all 41 `/aura-assets` URLs return 200) and boots into Quick Play in Harbor Sandlot. The Prism probe ran on SwiftShader software GL. It measured steady-state mean luma 86.2 to 88.2, 0% black, 0% clipped, 658 to 662 draw calls, 0.08 to 0.21 fps and a JS heap of 305 to 423 MB. During the first session the tab went to about:blank on its own after about 650 s; the cause is not proven. The game renders correctly, but manual play cannot finish a match in four of six parks because every human swing is forced into a perfect home run (C1). Health: broken for manual play, fine for rendering.

**4.5.0 impact:** The game's own source changed by one line between v4.3.7 and v4.5.0 (the `package.json` version), so every 4.5.0 change here comes from the engine. The scene has post effects, transparent materials and particles, so it stays on the HDR chain. The no-HDRI looks used by Midtown (night-city) and Lunar (polar-night) now get the neutral 0.85 fill; the upgrade notes report Skyline mean luma 71.0 to 74.5. `effects.contactShadows` (`main.ts:979`) went from a no-op to a real screen-space mask. Real-depth AO also changes every look-preset game. Neither change has been captured for this game: the final screens are dated 2026-10-25 and the 4.5.0 visual-regression run checked assertions only. Nothing looks broken live, but the release needs a recapture before it can be called verified here.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | P0 | Manual batting is automated. Every swing grades PERFECT and leaves the park, so in Harbor, Midtown, Ivy and Dome the top of the 1st never reaches 3 outs and Derby never ends. Not a 4.5.0 regression (added 2026-10-17). | Autopilot is off by default (`main.ts:1350`). Any WASD, arrow, Space or 1-4 press in `between` calls `cpuChoosePitch()` and sets `G.manualSwingQueued = true` (`main.ts:2728-2736`). Movement during windup does the same (`main.ts:2748`). Once `p.t >= 0.28` the game auto-swings (`main.ts:2815-2816`). `triggerSwing` clamps `p.target` to within 0.2 m of zone centre, scales the break by 0.2, sets `G.pci` to the ball's own crossing point and passes `timingErr: 0` (`main.ts:2666-2675`). `resolveSwing` therefore gets `dist = 0`, `q = 1` and grade PERFECT (`main.ts:1936-1952`). Re-running the game's own integrator (`/tmp/audit-diamond-scripts/fly.mjs`) gives carries of 113 to 157 m against centre fences of 96 to 102 m. | Make the swing a real input. Do not queue a swing on movement or digit presses. In `triggerSwing`, compute `timingErr` from `p.t` at the moment of the press, pass the player's `G.pci`, and stop rewriting `p.target` and the break. Add a test that a pitch with no input is a called strike in the zone and a ball outside it. | done 2ad404991, 38d16705e [4.6.0] |
| C2 | P1 | World Classic restarts the bracket every game, so the player can never get past the Round of 32. | The resume check is `G.result.startsWith("advanced")` (`main.ts:1677`). The stored result is `` `${reason} — advanced, ...` `` (`main.ts:2299`), where `reason` is `final after N` (`main.ts:2280`) or `mercy rule — ...`. The string never starts with "advanced", so `startClassicBracket()` runs again (`main.ts:1680`). | Track continuity with an explicit flag such as `G.classic.pendingFixture`, set in `concludeMatch` when the player advances. Add a test that wins the R32 game and expects the ROUND OF 16 fixture next. | done 2ad404991 [4.6.0] |
| C3 | P1 | Crowd figures, the centre bleacher, the Lunar grandstands and two props stand inside the field of play. Bleacher row 0 of the centre section sits 1.5 m inside the wall in four parks, 6.0 m in Canyon and 11.4 m in Lunar. The stadium shells use fixed positions (`main.ts:637-645`). The sandlot tower is at r 75 m against a 78 m fence (`main.ts:748`), and the lunar comms dome is at r 92 m against 113 m (`main.ts:837`). | Place every set-dressing node from `fenceRadius(park, theta)` plus a positive offset, and scale the shell positions by `park.fenceCenter`. Add a unit test that every set-dressing node lies outside the fence in all six parks. | done 2ad404991 [4.6.0] |
| C4 | P1 | A ball whose predicted flight hits the wall passes through the wall on screen. | `simBattedLanding` returns the predicted `crossedFence`, `hrCrossed` and `wallHit` flags (`main.ts:2118-2122`). `resolveSwing` keeps that object as the live ball (`main.ts:1994`). `stepBatted` bounces only `if (!ball.crossedFence && r >= fenceR)` (`main.ts:2886`), and for a wall-hit ball that flag is already true at launch. | Store the prediction separately and start the live flight with the fence flags false, or replay the same fixed 1/120 s steps live. | done 2ad404991 [4.6.0] |

The file header (`main.ts:18`) claims team-colour accents. | Tint per team through a second material slot with `model(..., { materialOverrides })`, or attach cap and belt primitives to the Head and Hips bones. **Tests and evidence gaps**

- Only `contact.ts` and `batter-pose.ts` have unit tests (`tests/unit/aura-diamond/`). Count, outs, runners, scoring, mercy and the bracket live inside the `boot()` closure in `main.ts` and cannot be tested, which is how C1, C2, C6 and C7 shipped. Move that logic into a pure module and test strikeout, walk, two-out sac fly, walk-off, mercy and bracket advancement.
- The final screens are dated 2026-10-25, before 4.5.0, and `evidence/aura-diamond-3d/screens/final/README.md` says results screens were not reached. Recapture on 4.5.0 at 1280x720 and 390x844 in all six parks, including a results screen and AO/contact-shadow on-off pairs (RH-05, LIGHT-06).
- The 4.5.0 visual-regression run checked `diamond-live-432` assertions only, with no pixel diff (`evidence/visual-regression-4.5.0/prd2.md:92-94`).
- `route-health.json` has no draw-call or triangle record.
- No motion-gate result exists for this game.
- Real-time batting could not be tested live: the Prism probe ran at about 0.1 fps on SwiftShader, so a pitch takes minutes of wall time. The live input result (Space held, called strike) is a frame-rate artefact, not evidence against C1.

## 09. Aura FC 3D (`aura-fc-3d`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-aura-fc-3d/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-aura-fc-3d` · **Genre:** sports

**State:** Production loads and Quick Play works. The live probe ran on SwiftShader software GL: `data-aura3d-ready=true`, 379 draw calls, no console errors, no failed requests (48 resources). Holding KeyD moved `h-fw` 3.08 m and play went from kickoff to live. Steady-state mean luma was 111.4 at t=114 s and 114.9 at t=594 s, with 0% black and at most 0.1% clipped, so the frame is never blank. Frame rate was about 0.15-0.3 fps on software GL, which says nothing about real GPUs. The boot path is healthy. Under ordinary play the sim has real defects: the Touch Lab freezes, shootouts are one-sided, and a slide tackle on a keeper loops every frame. The goal net is drawn at about 4x size, and phone touch controls cannot move up or down.

**4.5.0 impact:** For this app the release changed only `package.json` (4.3.7 to 4.5.0). The game uses look `interior-industrial` (an HDRI plus a shadowed directional key light), 4 authored spot lights, and `effects.bloom`, `motionBlur` and `contactShadows` (`src/game/scene.ts:390-392`). It therefore stays on the HDR post chain, and the "authored lights only" and no-HDRI fill changes do not apply to it. Four 4.5.0 engine changes alter its frames: contactShadows is now a real screen-space mask, motion blur and the look's ambient occlusion now read real depth (AO read zero depth on 4.3.7), and bloom uses separable fusion by default. Nobody has captured FC since 4.5.0. The final screens date from 2026-10-25, and FC has no 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 luma or AO comparison. The live luma looks sane, but the size of the shift is unmeasured.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FC-01 | P0 | Touch Lab freezes on the first shot, or whenever the ball goes out or into the goal. A TypeError is thrown every frame. | `sim.ts:1246` sets `state.players = [me]`, so team 1 has no players. `outOfBounds()` (`sim.ts:607-634`) still hands restarts to team 1 or `defendingTeam`. `nearestOf(1)` (`sim.ts:675-684`) returns `undefined`, and `stepRestart` then reads `taker.x` (`sim.ts:644`). A goal leads to `startKickoff(1)`, which calls `giveBall(undefined)`. Auditor probe: `goal-line {steps:3, err:"Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'x')"}`, `into-goal {steps:242, err:"...reading 'id'"}`. The phase stays `restart`, so the athletes, camera, HUD and lab timer all stop. | In `mode==='touch'`, skip out-of-bounds and goal handling and re-feed the ball instead. Guard `nearestOf`, `stepRestart` and `startKickoff` against a team with no players. Add a unit test that drives `spawnLab('touch')` and puts the ball over the touchline, over the goal line and into the goal. | done 17a295faa [4.6.0] |
| FC-02 | P1 | Shootouts: the home keeper takes the dive meant for the away keeper, so home penalties are never saved. | `sim.ts:1038-1051`: any keeper with `so.gkDive !== 0` and `p.diveT <= 0` dives and clears `gkDive`, with no `p.team !== so.takerTeam` check. `h-gk` is first in the player list. Auditor probe over 200 CPU shootouts: home 744/744 scored, away 181/637. | Only the defending keeper may dive: add `p.team !== so.takerTeam` to the CPU branch. Add a test of 100 CPU shootouts asserting similar conversion rates for both sides. | done 17a295faa [4.6.0] |
| FC-03 | P1 | In sudden death the shootout ends after the home kick, before the away side replies. | `sim.ts:537-540`: after five kicks each, `rem1 = 0`, so `scores[0] > scores[1] + rem1` returns 0 at taken `[6,5]`. Probe: the sudden death ended `taken:[6,5], scores:[6,5]`. | Apply the early-decision checks only while `taken[i] < 5`. In sudden death, decide only when `taken[0] === taken[1]`. | done 17a295faa [4.6.0] |
| FC-04 | P1 | A slide tackle steals from a keeper who is holding the ball and then steals again every frame of the slide. Each steal fires save and tackle juice. | `sim.ts:852-860` steals from any owner, including a keeper in `mode 'held'`, and has no per-slide latch. The loose ball (4.4 m/s, `lastShot` still `"shot"`) is caught again at `sim.ts:765-776`, which emits `save` and increments `state.saves`. Auditor probe over 20 CPU matches: 73 shots, 486 saves, 1194 "slide tackle wins it". The live probe also saw three tackle wins in frames 69, 70 and 71 from one slide. | Skip the steal when `b.mode==='held'`. Allow one dispossession per slide (latch it or clear `slideT`). Count a save only for a shot in flight, and clear `lastShot` on a tackle. | done 17a295faa [4.6.0] |
| FC-05 | P1 | Each goal-net GLB is scaled twice and drawn about 35 x 13 x 12 m, reaching about 4.8 m into the pitch. | `scene.ts:112-115` uses `targetMaxDimension: 8.8` together with `.scale([7.6/1.897, 2.85/1.282, 2.6/0.8])`. `model()` passes `targetMaxDimension` through (`nodes/model.ts:30`). `sceneMath.ts:179` gives fitScale 8.8/1.897 = 4.64, and `:162` multiplies `nodeScale * fitScale`. The result is 4.0 x 4.64 = 18.6 per unit on x, about 35 m of width. The huge white net is visible in `evidence/aura-fc-3d/screens/final/1280x720/03-action-06`. | Remove `targetMaxDimension` (use `scaleMode: "world"` or keep only the per-axis scale), or keep the fit and drop `.scale()`. Recapture the goal mouth from the broadcast and penalty cameras. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |
| FC-06 | P1 | On touch devices the virtual stick never mounts, so phone players can only move left and right. | `main.ts:227-233` mounts preset `dpad-4btn` and binds `stick.*`. `TouchControls.ts:177-183` looks up `preset.rects["stick"/"rstick"]`, but `TouchLayouts.ts:117-118` names those rects `leftStick`/`rightStick`, so `rect` is undefined and the sticks are skipped. The preset's only movement is `hold("left")`/`hold("right")` (`TouchLayouts.ts:109`). There is no touch binding for switch (Q) or pause (P). This is an engine bug and hits every game that uses `mountTouchControls` sticks. | Engine: map `stick` to `leftStick` and `rstick` to `rightStick` in `TouchControls.ts`, with a unit test that the stick element mounts. FC: use a layout with a movement stick plus switch and pause buttons. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |
| FC-07 | P1 | The HUD kit chips and possession bar use club colours the players never wear. | `hud.ts:228-241` uses `ctx.home.primary`/`ctx.away.primary`. Players always wear the fixed `TEAM_KITS` (red home, sky away; `rig.ts:66-69`, `main.ts:351-360`). In `03-action-06` the HUD shows pink vs teal while the players wear red/white vs white/navy. | Colour the chips and the bar from `TEAM_KITS[0/1].shirt`, or make the club choice drive the kits. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |
| FC-08 | P1 | Player Studio choices (club, boots, celebration) have no visible effect, although the card says the choice "lands on your squad instantly". | `modes.ts:160` has that copy. `refreshKits` (`main.ts:351-360`) uses fixed kits. `celebrateKind` is set at random (`sim.ts:372`) and nothing reads it (`rg celebrateKind` finds only the type, the init and that line). The club `press` field is unused. | Apply the studio club to the home kit (with a contrast guard) and map celebrations to clips, or cut the Studio down to what works and fix its copy. | done 17a295faa, 614721d38 [4.6.0] |
| FC-09 | P1 | The portrait phone camera shows about 7 m of pitch, so players fall out of frame. | Broadcast camera: fov 38 with the eye about 22.7 m from the target (`main.ts:330-332`), and nothing adapts it to aspect. At 390x844 the horizontal FOV is 18.1°, which covers about 7.3 m. The target is `b.x*0.8`, not the ball. `screens/final/390x844/03-action-08` shows no players. | Derive fov or camera distance from aspect so the frame covers at least 20 m, and track `b.x` directly. Recapture at 390x844. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |
| FC-10 | P1 | In the Favela venue the 1.5 m end walls run straight across both goal mouths. | `scene.ts:151-152`: walls `w`/`e` at x=±30.35, 36.6 m long, which covers the full goal line between posts at ±30 and the net back at ±32.6. The goal test (`sim.ts:751`) needs `|x| > 30.1`, but the walled bounce (`sim.ts:596-597`) clamps the ball back at `|x| > 30 - BALL_R`. A goal therefore counts only when one step jumps the ball from inside the clamp to past 30.1, about 0.3 m per step, which is roughly 18 m/s at 60 fps. Slower shots bounce off an invisible line in the goal mouth, and fast ones pass visually through the wall. | Split the end walls around `|z| < GOAL_HALF_W + 0.2`, and exempt the goal mouth from the bounce. Add a Favela goal test. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |
| FC-11 | P2 | The user's player always sprints and auto-runs, so Shift does nothing and stamina drains with no player choice. | `sim.ts:1165` returns `{ move: mv, sprint: true }`. The auto-dribble and chase logic is at `sim.ts:1159-1163`. The menu advertises "Sprint SHIFT" (`modes.ts:247`). | Use `sprint: input.sprint`, and put auto-movement behind an assist option. | done 17a295faa [4.6.0] |
| FC-12 | P2 | A difficulty floor of 0.65 makes Quick Play and every group-stage match play the same, and it applies to the user's teammates too. | `sim.ts:1097` calls `planCommand(..., Math.max(0.65, cfg.difficulty))` for every AI. Quick Play uses 0.6 and the group stage 0.5/0.56/0.62 (`modes.ts:274-276, 582`). | Remove the floor, apply difficulty only to the opponent, and give teammates a fixed value. | done 17a295faa [4.6.0] |
| FC-13 | P2 | The penalty miss model is computed but never applied, so a placed penalty never goes wide or over. | `firePenalty` (`sim.ts:553-562`) computes `err` and `pendingMiss` but launches straight at `aim[0]`. `pendingMiss` is only read on the 2.6 s timeout. Probe: 30/30 aimed lab penalties had `pendingMiss=false`. | Apply `err` to the launch direction and loft, and derive loft from the target height. | done 17a295faa [4.6.0] |
| FC-14 | P2 | During the golden-goal banner play continues with the clock running, then the game teleports to a kickoff that always goes to the home side. | `sim.ts:1022-1025`: the `extratime` case only waits for `phaseT > 2.4` and then calls `startKickoff(0)`. Play is not frozen in the meantime. | Hold all players as at half-time, keep the clock at 0, and alternate the kickoff. | done 17a295faa [4.6.0] |

The banner hides at 1400 ms (`main.ts:432`). No `visibilitychange` handler exists in `src`. | Skip lab ticking while paused. Show a persistent pause panel. Auto-pause when the tab is hidden. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |
| FC-16 | P2 | The crowd loop starts on the first goal and never stops, and the whistle, kick and post sounds are reused basketball sounds. | `main.ts:387` is the only `cue("crowd")` call, and there is no stop call. The cue list uses `hoopsCrowdSfx`, `hoopsBuzzerSfx` and the hoops thud and clank sounds (`main.ts:163-171`). | Start a crowd bed at kickoff, duck it in menus and pause, and swell it on goals. Resolve football-specific whistle, kick and net sounds. | done 614721d38, d6ae2caef [4.6.0] |
| FC-17 | P2 | Most CPU matches (the Demo attract mode) end 0-0. | Auditor probe, 10 CPU matches: nine ended 0-0, one 0-1, with 29 shots. The FC-04 loop turns shots into "saves" and inflates the tackle count. | After FC-04, tune `attackOwner` shot range and probability and the keeper reach to 2-4 goals per match. Add a sim test for that range. | done 17a295faa, 2cc85c5ae [4.6.0] |
| FC-18 | P2 | The sim uses a variable dt and unseeded `Math.random`, although the evidence claims a seed. | `main.ts:407` steps once per frame with dt clamped to 0.05. At 20 fps a 24 m/s shot moves 1.2 m per step, while the post band is 0.76 m wide (`sim.ts:734-736`). `Math.random` appears at `ai.ts:181, 271, 277-306`, `sim.ts:158` and `modes.ts:419-592`, while evidence publishes `seed: "lcg:0xf0a7ba11"`. | Use a fixed-step accumulator, swept collision against the goal frame, and the seeded `rng` everywhere. | done 17a295faa, 614721d38 [4.6.0] |
| FC-19 | P2 | The trophy floats 0.9 m in the air and the spare ball 0.1 m. | Fitted models are placed base-at-origin (`sceneMath.ts:164`). `scene.ts:323-328` puts them at y=0.9 and y=0.1 with no plinth. | Set y to 0, or add a plinth under the trophy. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |
| FC-20 | P2 | The shootout ledger paints all goals first rather than kick-by-kick history, and rebuilds its DOM every frame. | `hud.ts:265-276` marks dot `i` as a goal when `i < scores[team]` and resets `innerHTML` every frame. The background is fixed at `#0d1a24` and the look at `interior-industrial` (`scene.ts:381-382`). `applyVenue` (`scene.ts:446-474`) does not change lights or crowd colours. | Set the look preset, sky, spot colour and crowd palette per venue. | done 614721d38, fa446fdf5 [4.6.0] |
| FC-22 | P3 | Shot charge tops out at 0.85, and `shotsOn` counts every attempt. | `sim.ts:1151, 1171` auto-fire at 0.85. `sim.ts:962` increments `shotsOn` when the swing starts. | Fire at 1.0 or rescale the meter. Count shots on target only when the path crosses the frame. | done 17a295faa [4.6.0] |

Live: `__AURA3D_GAME_EVIDENCE__.hud = {frame 0, values [], changedIds []}` at t=585 s. | Pass real bindings (score, clock, stamina, possession) so capture tooling can read the HUD. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |

Re-enable runtime effects once the bubble root cause is known. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |

The 390x844 final screen ends at UYUNI. | Collapse the venues into one "Venue" dropdown on phones, or add an edge fade. | done 614721d38 [4.6.0] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- FC has had no capture since 4.5.0, which changed four effects the game uses: contactShadows, motion-blur depth, AO real depth and bloom fusion. The final screens date from 2026-10-25. The release audits LIGHT-02, LIGHT-06, RH-04, RH-05 and RP-05 all name FC.
- No 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state luma result exists. FC is missing from `tests/browser/game-luma-capture.spec.ts` TARGETS, and no AO-on/AO-off pair has been made.
- `tests/unit/aura-fc-3d/` covers only the rig, ball physics, gait, kicks, locomotion and pass flight. No test covers the shootout (FC-02, FC-03), the Touch Lab (FC-01), a tackle on a keeper (FC-04), extra time, pause, circuit progression, the HUD or Favela goals.
- No recorded frame shows a goal mouth with the net at its real size, a half-time or results screen, or the portrait camera with players in view.
- Debug URL switches `?aura3d-render=` and `?aura3d-bloom-fusion=` reject the mount on a typo (RP-04, all games). This is a P3 shared engine issue, not specific to FC.

**Dropped on verification**

- None of the code-audit findings was refuted. C4 (code audit) and the live probe's slide-latch observation describe the same defect and are merged into FC-04. The live probe's note that two defenders started a slide in the same frame has no mechanism behind it yet, so it is not listed.

## 10. Aura Links 3D (`aura-links-3d`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-aura-links-3d/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-aura-links-3d` · **Genre:** sports

**State:** Production loads cleanly. The live probe (Prism, SwiftShader software GL) saw `data-aura3d-ready=true`, 141 draw calls, no console errors and no failed requests out of 43 resources. Steady-state mean luma was 138.1 at both 123 s and 998 s, with 0.1% black pixels and 0% clipped. Aim, swing, out-of-bounds penalty and re-tee all work. The game cannot be finished in normal play. The swing logic presses power and accuracy for the player in every mode, and the auto power sends every putt about 50% past the cup, so no hole ends. The health rating is broken.

**4.5.0 impact:** The release changed only `package.json` in the game folder (`git diff --stat v4.3.7 v4.5.0 -- apps/showcase-aura-links-3d`: 1 file). Engine changes still alter every course. `effects.contactShadows` (main.ts:944-951) is now a real mask instead of a no-op. Real-depth AO comes in through the look presets. On Neon Bay (night-city) and Mare (space), the hemisphere light now counts in the ambient term, which grows it 2.29x and 4.09x (main.ts:920, 922). No Links frame has been captured since the release. The 4.5.0 luma check covered only the default boot (St Auroras hole 1, one frame at 480x300). CHANGELOG.md:32 claims "within about 5% inside the games", but docs/upgrade-4.5.0/aura-links-3d.md:53 says "this game was not measured". There is no evidence of a regression, and none that the courses are fine. Each course needs a capture.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | P0 | Holes cannot be finished. The swing is auto-played in every mode and auto-sized putts never drop. | main.ts:1997-2017 runs whenever `phase==="swing"`. It is not gated on `autopilot` (main.ts:1912, 1973). It ticks the meter at `dt * 3.0`, auto-presses power at `targetPower` and auto-commits accuracy at meter <= 0.04. The putt target `clamp(cupDist/22, 0.35, 0.88)` (main.ts:2001) feeds a putter with carry 30 (sim.ts:102), so the intended roll is about 1.36x the cup distance. Launch is sized with `GREEN_FRICTION = 1.9` (sim.ts:42, 257), but the green decelerates at 1.7 (sim.ts:64). That adds another 1.12x, for about 1.5x in total. The minimum power 0.35 rolls about 11.7 m, so a 1 m putt finishes about 10 m past the cup. The code audit ran the sim with tsx over all 54 holes and holed 0 of them in 25 strokes. There is no stroke cap (`rg maxStrokes\|pickup` finds nothing). The live probe shows the same: a single Space hold produced a full "Driver · 97% · PURE · PURE" shot, because the auto-press did the second and third clicks. The bundle contains `u.meter<=.04&&i.swing.press()`. | Gate the auto-press on `autopilot` and remove the `*3.0` speed-up. Size putts with `FRICTION.green` (or set `GREEN_FRICTION = 1.7`), and set the autopilot putt power to `d*1.05/(30*1.11)`. Add a pickup at par+5 that records the score and moves to the next hole. | done f645ced8d, 07e76763a, 281cd0b18 [4.6.1] |
| C2 | P1 | The three-click meter plays itself, so a player press can only make a shot worse, and difficulty never affects the player's shots. | With `tick(dt*3)` the accuracy auto-commit at 0.04 always falls inside `ACC_DEAD_ZONE` 0.12 (sim.ts:122), so every shot comes out PURE with zero spin. Any player press comes earlier, with the needle further from centre. `DIFF_GREEN_ZONE` (main.ts:171) is read only to draw the meter bar (main.ts:1848). The HUD still says "SPACE ×3 — POWER · ACCURACY · SPIN" (index.html:904, 991). The final frame 390x844/03-action-03-accuracy.jpg shows "PWR 91% … PURE CONTACT" with no input. | After C1, let the player's presses decide power, accuracy and spin. Map `DIFF_GREEN_ZONE` to the pure strip. If one-press play is wanted, offer it as an explicit "assist" toggle with matching hint text. | done f645ced8d [4.6.1] |
| C4 | P1 | A dead-centre putt that arrives too fast reverses straight back toward the player. | sim.ts:491-501 computes `h = v/|v| - 1.4*u`. When the ball is heading at the cup, `v/|v|` is roughly `u`, so `h` is about `-0.4u` and normalises to `-u` at full speed. The code audit measured a 4 m putt ending 7.68 m behind the cup. | Deflect sideways by 25-60° and keep 60-80% of the speed, without letting the ball reverse. Use a swept closest-approach test so the outcome does not depend on the frame rate. | done f645ced8d [4.6.1] |
| C3 | P1 | From hole 2 on, the tee address and swing use the celebrate orbit around the far cup. | main.ts:1189 tests `cam.mode === "celebrate"` before the green, address and aim branches. startHole (main.ts:1734-1742) resets eye, target and fov but never `cam.mode`. The only other writes are main.ts:1176, 1196, 1206 and 1219, all after that branch. Only the flight branch clears it. | Set `cam.mode = "aim"` in startHole and beginAddress, or gate the branch on phase only. | done f645ced8d [4.6.1] |
| C5 | P1 | Pro Circuit cannot be continued, and every round uses the first course. | `menu-run` calls `setMode(state.tour ? "tour" : "quick")` (main.ts:2274). setMode("tour") always runs `state.tour = createTour(0x4a32)` and `selectCourse(courseForRound[0]!)` (main.ts:1810-1813). `tourHoleStart` is written but never read. | Add a "Next round" action that keeps `state.tour` and selects `courseForRound[roundIndex]`. Create a new tour only when none exists or the current one has finished. | done f645ced8d [4.6.1] |
| C6 | P1 | The visible pond has less than half the radius of the water hazard. | The cylinder size convention is diameter (packages/engine/src/agent-api/nodes/primitives.ts:33). The pond uses `[w.radius*0.92,…]` and the rim `[w.radius*0.99,…]` (main.ts:859-860), so their visible radii are 0.46r and 0.495r. The sim zone is the full radius r. Balls splash on what looks like bank. | Use `w.radius*2*0.98` for the pond and `w.radius*2` for the rim. Add a check that each hazard mesh matches its zone radius. | done f645ced8d [4.6.1] |
| C7 | P1 | Short approaches always fly long because auto power has a 0.65 floor. | main.ts:2003 uses `clamp(cupDist / max(12, carry*0.94), 0.65, 0.92)`. The code audit measured 30 m and 50 m wedges both stopping at 60 m. | Fixed by C1 and C2. For autopilot, size power from the carry with no floor. | done f645ced8d [4.6.1] |
| C8 | P2 | Opening the menu mid-shot or after holing out breaks the hole. | Escape opens the menu in any phase (main.ts:1951-1953). `menu-close` sets `state.phase = "address"` (main.ts:2273) without calling beginAddress or clearing `state.ball`. The ball then hangs mid-air, or the player can putt again from the cup and `recordHole` runs a second time. | Save the phase on open and restore it on close. Block Escape during `card`, or treat it as "next hole". | done f645ced8d [4.6.1] |
| C9 | P2 | The Pro Circuit bracket never shows or highlights the player. | The player row is appended last (sim.ts:721). renderBracket shows only `rows.slice(0, 8)` and compares `c.name === "YOU"` (main.ts:2205), but `PLAYER_NAME = "You"` (sim.ts:626). | Pin the player row in every round and compare against an exported `PLAYER_NAME`. | done f645ced8d [4.6.1] |
| C10 | P2 | Putting Lab never counts a miss. | The puttinglab rest branch (main.ts:1573-1577) re-tees without touching `state.lab`. attempts, hits and streak change only in onHoled (main.ts:1598), and the message there prints the old streak (main.ts:1599). | On a rest, increment attempts, reset the streak and move to the next spot. Print the updated streak. | done f645ced8d [4.6.1] |
| SI-1 | P2 | Instanced conifers sample cascade 0 and get no sun shadow. | The node origin of the 63-instance conifer nodes is the world origin, so the whole draw picks cascade 0 (0-13.4 m live). The instances sit at z 20.5-277 m, and the lookup returns lit outside that cascade (packages/rendering/src/forward/Lighting.ts:486-498, ShaderLibrary.ts:230). This predates 4.5.0. | Engine fix: select the cascade per fragment, or from the instanced world bounds. | done d3550c133 [4.6.0] |
| RH-06 | P2 | The page downloads a 2.6 MB shared asset catalog, and many of its URLs return 404. | The live page loads `assets/aura-assets-BECYe7eh.js` (2,619,707 B raw, 360,990 B gzip). The release audit found 522 of its URLs return 404 (assets of removed routes). | Import only the per-app manifest, or split the root manifest per route. | done f645ced8d, c15812d9d (game), 4f19c980c, bdbd5a4a6 (engine or shared) [4.6.1] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- There are no unit tests for createSwingMeter, stepBall cup capture and lip-out, putt sizing, advanceTour, the lab scoring or the camera modes. The 33 tests in tests/unit/aura-links-3d cover stroke-commit, posture, clubs, arm sweep and aim. This gap is why C1, C3, C4, C5, C9 and C10 shipped. Add a sim test that autoplays every hole of every course and requires each hole to be holed in 12 strokes or fewer.
- No evidence reaches a hole-out or the scorecard (evidence/aura-links-3d/screens/final/README.md). new-game-checklist.md:38 requires a results frame.
- No 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state capture exists for any course (LIGHT-02, RH-03, RH-04). Capture all six courses with ?course=<id> at 1280x720 and 390x844 on the remote runner, starting with Neon Bay and Mare (ambient up 2.29x and 4.09x, LIGHT-03), and compare the golfer region as well as the mean luma.
- The final screens and the index poster date from 2026-10-25, before the 4.3.4 arm fix and before 4.5.0 (RH-05). Retake them after C1 is fixed.
- route-health.json claims "Immediate playable boot with smart autopilot" and "shot-shaping via accuracy/spin clicks" and lists the replaced ball-rack asset. It is still marked `unreviewed` (generatedAt 2026-10-17). game-inventory.json marks the game "playable". Downgrade both until a hole-out is captured (C16).

**Dropped on verification**

- Live-probe note that one Space hold producing a full three-click swing was a Prism key-repeat artefact: refuted. The auto-press at main.ts:2008-2015 performs the power and accuracy clicks, so this behaviour is C1 and C2 seen live.
- Live-probe note on stale `swing.impactFired=true` at address: dropped. startSwing resets it (main.ts:1467) and nothing reads it before then.
- C13 per-substep BallState allocations in stepBall: merged into C13 and downgraded to P3, because no frame-time cost was measured.

## 11. Bunker Zero (`bunker-zero`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-bunker-zero/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-bunker-zero` · **Genre:** action

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. The Prism probe (SwiftShader software GL, so no perf numbers stand for real GPUs) saw no console errors and no failed requests. Steady-state mean luma was 75.7 (3.5% of pixels below 12, none clipped). Movement, firing, enemy attacks, the HUD and Quick Play reset all worked. Floor 1 is fine, but the sim has run-ending bugs. When the boss summons guards, the floor 6 game logic freezes, so the campaign cannot be finished. All audio and feedback stops after about 17 s of play on any floor. Health: degraded.

**4.5.0 impact:** The game source did not change (`git log v4.3.7..HEAD -- apps/showcase-bunker-zero` is empty), so every change comes from the engine. The hemisphere light now adds to the authored ambient 0.9. LIGHT-03 computes the ambient term rising from 0.592 to 0.845 (x1.43), and a sibling game with the same setup measured +18% luma. Look AO now reads real depth, and `effects.contactShadows` is now a real mask. No one has captured Bunker Zero on 4.3.7 vs 4.5.0, so the size of the shift is unknown. The live render path is `hdr-msaa`, but the engine drops SSAO (`droppedPasses ['ssao']`, `missingInputs ['ssao:normals']`). The game pays for the HDR AO path and gets no AO pixels. Needs a measured capture, and probably a hemisphere or exposure retune.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | P0 | Floor 6 logic freezes when the Iron Warden summons guards, which makes the final floor unwinnable | `sim.ts:586-595` pushes `mkEnemy("guard", gx, gz, …)`, which sets `sx/sz` to the new cell (`sim.ts:232`). `main.ts:755-757` calls `req(\`f${slot}-en-${e.sx}-${e.sz}\`)` → `app.nodes.require`, which throws for ids that have no scene node. Nodes exist only for authored enemy cells. Probe: summoned ids `f5-en-12-9`, `f5-en-10-9` are not among the authored cells. The throw is caught by the engine (`createAuraApp.ts:355-365`), so the canvas still renders, but the rest of `syncScenePose` and the transition, death, HUD and evidence code (`main.ts:980-1006`) never run again. The 66% summon always fires before the boss can die. | Pre-build hidden summon nodes per floor (for example `f5-summon-0..3`) and map `state.summons` to them. Use `app.nodes.get` and skip missing nodes in per-frame pose code. | done eb37e9d9c, e56094444 [4.6.1] |
| C2 | P1 | All SFX, hit markers, kill feed and juice stop after 40 events on a floor (about 17 s of walking) | `sim.ts:180` caps `events` at 40 with `shift()`. `main.ts:977` drains by index, and its trim only runs at `length > 60` (`main.ts:978`), which never happens. Once `lastEventIdx` hits 40 the loop never runs again. Probe: 60 s of walking drained exactly 40 events. Footsteps alone emit every 0.42 s (`sim.ts:818`). Same code in live bundle `showcase-bunker-zero-Sda4OldM.js`. | Keep a monotonic event sequence number and drain `seq > lastSeq`, or have main drain and clear `state.events` each frame. | done eb37e9d9c, e56094444 [4.6.1] |
| C6 | P1 | Boss rockets damage the player wherever they explode | `sim.ts:679` `if (r.fromEnemy \|\| hitPlayerTarget) dealDamage(true, 20 + rng()*15)` runs on wall hits and timeouts too. Give enemy rockets player splash with distance falloff. | done eb37e9d9c [4.6.1] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- No sim unit tests exist. `tests/unit/bunker-zero/` holds only enemy-facing, weapons-view and local-assets tests. Add `sim.test.ts`: summoned ids resolve to nodes (C1), 200 events produce 200 drains (C2), Kill House timeout gives a lose outcome (C4), per-weapon magazines (C5), enemy rocket falloff (C6), closed doors block LOS (C7), floor 6 winnable with the starting loadout (C14).
- No 4.5.0 capture exists. The final screens date from 2026-10-25 (before 4.3.7 and 4.5.0), the results screen was never reached, and every box in `docs/upgrade-4.5.0/bunker-zero.md` is unchecked (RH-04, RH-05, RH-03, LIGHT-02, LIGHT-06). Run the remote steady-state luma capture on 4.3.7 vs 4.5.0. Also run a separable bloom vs `?aura3d-render=production` diff (RP-05).
- Engine, all createGameApp games: `diagnostics().fps` is hard-wired to 60 (`packages/engine/src/agent-api/app/frameLoop.ts:219` `fps || 60`). The live probe got 60 from it while rAF measured 1.3 fps, and `frame.frames` stayed 0, so frame timing (p50/p95) is empty.
- Engine: SSAO is requested but dropped live (`missingInputs ['ssao:normals']`), so AO adds HDR cost and no pixels.
- Draw calls (210-217 on floor 1, live probe) and triangle counts are not recorded in `evidence/showcase-bunker-zero` (checklist §3).
- The page loads the shared 2.6 MB root asset catalog, in which 522 URLs return 404 (RH-06). Import only the per-app `src/aura-assets.ts`.
- Startup warning "step() was called before the WebGL renderer finished mounting": the first frame renders nothing (engine, P3).

## 12. Slingshot Siege (`slingshot-siege`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-slingshot-siege/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-slingshot-siege` · **Genre:** action

**State:** The game loads and plays on production with no console errors and no failed requests. The live probe ran on SwiftShader software GL at 1440x900. Steady-state mean luma was 111.6 at 3 s and at 12 s, with 3.2% of pixels dark, 0% clipped and about 120 coarse colours, so the frame is lit and not blank. The probe counted 394 draw calls, the highest of the five games probed, and the page ran at about 0.3 fps. Keyboard aim (W) and launch (Space) work, but R, P, M, Tab, Escape and Q do nothing, and the probe found several other player-visible bugs in the game's own code. Health: degraded.

**4.5.0 impact:** 4.5.0 changed only `package.json` (the version bump) in this app, so any effect comes from the engine. The look is `outdoor-day`, which has an HDRI, so the lights-only and no-HDRI fill changes do not apply. The quality profile is `production`, so the cascade skip is off. No instanced node reaches 1,024, so dense depth draws are off. All primitive options are known keys, so nothing raises OPTION_IGNORED. Real-depth AO is on through the look preset and changes the image. Nobody has measured that change for this game (LIGHT-06), and there is no 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 luma comparison (LIGHT-02). The authored hemisphere light (`stage.ts:145`, 0.4) is dropped because an environment is present. 4.3.7 behaved the same way, so this is not a regression (LIGHT-05). None of the bugs below were introduced in 4.5.0.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SS-1 | P1 | The R (reset), P/Escape (pause), M/Tab (map) and Q (ability) keys never fire. | `main.ts:1145` calls `input?.update(dt)` again after `GameAppRuntime.ts:224` has already updated owned inputs. The second `update()` runs `pressedEdges.clear()` (`GameRuntime.ts:1887`), and the first call already cleared the pending sets (:1904-1905), so `pressed()` is always false. Live: R held for 6 s left shotsUsed at 1, and P held for about 9 s left paused false. The index.html footer advertises "R reset · M map · P pause". | Delete `main.ts:1145`. Add a browser spec that presses R and P. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-2 | P1 | The mute button changes only its icon. Sound keeps playing. | `main.ts:817` `onMuteToggle: () => { muted = !muted; return muted; }`. `muted` is read only by the HUD (:1117), and `audio.ts` has no mute path. | Add bus volume / setMuted to `siegeAudio` and call it from the toggle. Persist the setting. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-3 | P1 | The ammo dock dims the wrong chips and always highlights chip 0 as next. | `hud.ts:197` marks a chip `ready` when `i < ammoLeft` and `current` when `i === 0`, but shots consume `level.ammo[shotsUsed]` from index 0 (`main.ts:420`). The 390x844 evidence frame `03-action-02-state-shot-in-flight` shows chip 0 highlighted and chip 2 dimmed. | Pass `shotsUsed`. Mark `i < shotsUsed` as spent and `i === shotsUsed` as current. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-4 | P1 | Pooled block nodes keep the glass/ice material on later levels, so wood and stone blocks render see-through. | `main.ts:190-192` calls `setMaterial` only for glass/ice, with no else branch. Nodes are picked by piece index (`blockNodeId(i)`, :186). Example: L14 Grand Conservatory (46 glass) is followed by L15 First Spin (wood) on the normal Next path. | Call `setMaterial` for every block: `blockMaterial(material)`, or reset to the GLB material. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-5 | P1 | Level 25 "Gull Keep" has 83 pieces but the pool has only 72 block nodes, so the extra glass blocks are solid but invisible. | `stage.ts:31` `blocks: 72`. `main.ts:186` indexes nodes by piece index. tsx probe: `Gull Keep 83 {"stone":69,"glass":12,"wood":2}`. The sim still creates Rapier bodies for indices 72-80. | Use a separate block counter, raise `POOL.blocks` to at least the largest block count, and add a unit assert that every level fits the pools. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-6 | P1 | The result card's `{once:true}` listener is used up by any click on the card, which leaves the buttons dead. Stale listeners can also fire Next twice and skip a level. | `hud.ts:254-257` adds the listener on every `showResult`. A click on the title or stars removes it. Leaving through the topbar map leaves the listener in place, so the next result card stacks a second one. | Add one delegated listener in `build()` and ignore clicks with no `data-act`. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-7 | P1 | On portrait phones neither the slingshot nor the fort is on screen. | Fixed CAM poses (`main.ts:254-259`, vertical fov 55) with no aspect handling. At 390x844 the horizontal FOV is about 28°, while the sling is at x -7.4 and the fort at x 7.2. The 390x844 evidence frame shows neither. | Fit the camera distance to the horizontal FOV when aspect < 1, or use a portrait framing that follows the sling or the fort. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-8 | P2 | "Next Level" bypasses the star gate. | `main.ts:943` `hasNext: won ? hasNext \|\| level.id + 1 < LEVELS.length : true`. `onResult('next')` (:791-794) loads id+1 unchecked. The Siege Map does enforce the gate (`hud.ts:277-282`). | Use `hasNext` alone and guard `onResult('next')` with `isLevelUnlocked`. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-9 | P2 | Trajectory Range wins save stars under id 99, which count toward unlocks. Restart in range mode loads a real campaign level. | `endLevel` (`main.ts:921-936`) persists `saved.stars[level.id]` with no mode check. Range shots are free. `onReset` (:786) loads `realLevelId` while mode stays `range`. | Skip `endLevel`/persist in range mode, reload `SANDBOX_LEVEL` on reset, and have `totalStars` ignore ids ≥ `LEVELS.length`. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-10 | P2 | In range mode, shots after the 10th projectile record fly invisible, and dragging a slider fires a shot on every `input` event. | `main.ts:745` draws a projectile only if `i < POOL.projectiles`, and records are never reused. `hud.ts:339-342` calls `fire()` on slider `input`. | Map projectiles to pool slots modulo the pool size. Fire only from the LAUNCH button. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-11 | P2 | `pointercancel` fires the shot, there is no pointer capture, and a release while paused still launches. | `main.ts:565` routes `pointercancel` to `endPointer`. `fireShot` (:567-580) has no `canAim()` check. There is no `setPointerCapture` call. | Cancel the draw on `pointercancel`, capture the pointer on drag start, and check `canAim()` in `fireShot`. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-12 | P2 | A late topple that squashes the last gull after the shot settles does not end the level. | The win check exists only in `onShotSettled` (`main.ts:915`). The sim forces settle at 6.5 s of shot age (`sim.ts:764`). | Check `gullsAlive()===0 && !isShotActive()` in the frame loop and end the level after a short grace period. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-13 | P2 | The documented "drag empty sky to orbit" control does nothing. | `camYaw` is written at `main.ts:538` but never read (`rg camYaw`: lines 247 and 538 only). | Apply the yaw/pitch offset in `updateCamera`, or remove the code path and the footer line. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-14 | P2 | Keyboard-only players get no sound, and the wind ambience never plays. | `audio.unlock()` runs only in the stage `pointerdown` handler (`main.ts:490`), not on `keydown` (:516). `"wind"` appears only in `audio.ts`. | Unlock on the first window keydown or click, then start the wind loop. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-15 | P2 | Tumbling blocks can render up to about 26° off on two-axis rotations. | `main.ts:115-125` is a ZYX quat→Euler, while the engine composes XYZ (`physics.ts:514-527`). | SS-17 | P2 | 394 draw calls for a 7-piece level. Live ran at 0.3 fps on SwiftShader, and `loadLevel` stalled the page. | Live probe diagnostics. Software GL, so this is not a real-GPU number. | Instance the repeated pooled props and record draw calls in the evidence. | done 1f1afdd16 [4.6.0] |

Add `@media (prefers-reduced-motion)`, use a minimum of about 11px, and skip slow-mo. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-E3 | P3 | Per-frame DOM and allocation churn. | `hud.ts:190-202` rebuilds via innerHTML every 4 frames. `refreshVp` allocates every frame (`main.ts:452-476`). | Diff the HUD state before writing, and reuse the buffers. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-E4 | P3 | Onboarding and readability: the fort is small in the aim frame, there is no coach overlay, the WASD/arrow aim keys are undocumented, and the gull pips do not match the boar models. | Desktop evidence frame. `main.ts:1151-1167`, `hud.ts:192`. | Frame the sling and fort together, add a first-level coach overlay, and use a boar icon. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-E5 | P3 | `window.__SS_FIRE__` defaults to `stone`, which level 1 does not offer. | `main.ts:1258`. Level 1 ammo is pebble only (`levels.ts:271`). | Default to `currentAmmo()`. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-E6 | P3 | A typo in `?aura3d-render=` or `?aura3d-bloom-fusion=` stops the game from mounting. | RP-04 (`renderPath.ts:138-143`). | Warn and fall back for values that come from the URL. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |
| SS-E7 | P3 | The page downloads the shared 2.6 MB root asset catalog, in which 522 URLs return 404. | RH-06 | Import only the per-app manifest. | done 9b87a3a82 [4.6.0] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- `apps/showcase-slingshot-siege/tests/smoke.spec.ts` is outside the root vitest `include` (tests/unit, integration, assets, qr), and the app has no test script, so no gate runs it.
- No unit asserts cover pool sizes per level (SS-5) or the material reset (SS-4).
- `evidence/slingshot-siege/screens/final` is dated 2026-10-25, before the 4.5.0 engine changes. It never reached a results screen and records no draw calls, contrary to `new-game-checklist.md:38-40` (RH-05).
- There is no 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state luma comparison, no AO-on/off pair and no real-GPU capture (LIGHT-02, LIGHT-06). The 4.5.0 upgrade guide for this game says nothing was rendered (RH-04).
- The hemisphere light at `stage.ts:145` is silently dropped (LIGHT-05). A fix in the engine will brighten this game, so capture before and after.
- The live probe ran on SwiftShader only, so frame-time and draw-call cost on a real GPU are unmeasured.

## 13. Glint Courier (`dot-dash-3d`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-dot-dash-3d/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-dot-dash-3d` · **Genre:** arcade

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. A Prism probe (SwiftShader software GL) logged no console errors and no failed requests. It measured 39 draw calls on the `hdr-msaa` path and a steady-state mean luma of 30.0 at 1440x900, with 9.2% of pixels below 12 and none blown. That is a dark neon board, not a black frame. Movement, dot eating, sentry catches, lives and the R reset all worked. The run loop has two serious defects: after game over the card never closes, and clearing the last board ends on a frozen screen with nothing on it. The card art on the site is also still the pre-rework arcade clone.

**4.5.0 impact:** The scene authors `lights.ambient`, so 4.5.0 drops the `neon-arcade` neutral fill (`world.ts:111`). The lights-only branch now adds the authored `lights.hemisphere` (`world.ts:112`) to the ambient. The upgrade guide measured steady-state luma at 25.0 on 4.3.7 and 29.5 on 4.5.0, an 18% rise (`docs/upgrade-4.5.0/dot-dash-3d.md:27`). The live 30.0 agrees with that. The board still reads correctly, so the rise needs only a by-eye decision, not a fix, but the CHANGELOG claim of "within about 5%" is wrong for this game. The look's AO pass is dropped live (`ssao:normals` missing), so the AO real-depth fix has no effect here. Bloom, vignette and transparent materials keep the game on the HDR path, so the new direct path does not apply. Fused bloom (`separable`) is the default on this path and nobody has compared it per game. The shadow diagnostics disagree with each other (see DD-10).

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DD-1 | P0 | The game-over card is never hidden. Retry, R, Quick Play and Board Map all start the next run under a 72%-opaque blurred overlay. | `hud.ts:255` sets `dd-gameover.hidden = false`. Nothing sets it back: `closeOverlays()` (`hud.ts:118-123`) hides only `dd-circuit` and `dd-studio`. Retry (`hud.ts:66`) calls `startRun` and then `closeOverlays`. The overlay styles are at `styles.css:196-205` (inset 0, z-index 20, rgba(3,4,12,0.72), blur 4px). The code audit reproduced it live: after Retry, mode=play and lives=3 while `#dd-gameover` was still `display:flex`. | In `tickUI`, on every mode change set `dd-gameover.hidden = st.mode !== "gameover"`, and do the same for `dd-clear`. Also hide both in `closeOverlays()`. Add a browser check that reaches game over, presses Retry and asserts the card is hidden. | done 7afed014f [4.6.1] |
| DD-2 | P1 | The `win` end state has no screen. Finishing a Board Map or Sentry Lab run, or the sixth Quick Play board, leaves a frozen board with no message or buttons. | `sim.ts:946-948` sets `st.mode = "win"`. `tickUI` opens cards only for `cleared` and `gameover` (`hud.ts:168-169`). The clear card hides itself after 2100 ms (`hud.ts:246`). Keep the clear card up for runs that end. | done 7afed014f [4.6.1] |

At 390x844 (aspect 0.462) and about 22 units from the board, that shows about ±4.5 world units. The maze spans ±9.5 (19 tiles, `TILE = 1`). The inventory card points at `screens/final/card-2x.jpg` (`game-inventory.json:236`). The live poster `dot-dash-3d.webp` averages RGB (11,24,77), the old navy palette. | Re-run `tests/browser/shotsd-dot-dash-3d.spec.ts` on the remote runner against the current source. Live probe: two lives lost within about 5.5 s of the first input, with 2 dots eaten. `hasMoved=false` holds the board until the next input (`sim.ts:800-802`), and the only cue is a toast. `READY_TIME = 0.25` (`sim.ts:24`), so the intro banner is barely visible. | Spawn sly at least 10 path tiles away and release sentries in a staggered order on early boards. |---|---|---|---|---|---|

Otherwise remove the unused fields. | done 7afed014f, e050798fa, b747e0f67 [4.6.1] |
| DD-E2 | P2 | Touch players have no pause or menu button, and phones get no control instructions. | Pause opens only on Escape (`hud.ts:74-79`). The touch preset binds only directions and dash. `styles.css:313` hides the controls hint under 680 px. Autostart skips the title help. | Add a 44 px pause button. Show a first-run hint ("d-pad to move, collect shards, avoid sentries") until the first input. | done 7afed014f [4.6.1] |
| DD-E3 | P2 | The game's own flicker and strobe effects ignore the reduced-motion and reduced-flash settings. | The death flicker uses `sin(t*24)` visibility (`world.ts:327,342`), sentries strobe white near the end of a surge (`world.ts:357`), and every death runs a red fade (`world.ts:410-414`). `rg 'reducedMotion\|reducedFlash' src` finds nothing. The engine juice does honour both settings. | Read `g.session.reducedMotion` and `reducedFlash`. The game runs 60 fps on headless Prism, so this is spare capacity on low-end phones, not a measured stall. | Paint walls, rails and dressing once per board change and resubmit only the dynamic pools each frame. **Tests and evidence gaps**
- The 998-line `sim.ts` has no unit tests. `tests/unit` has only the quickbar and rng-consolidation files for this game, and no browser spec reaches game over or win, which is how DD-1 and DD-2 went undetected. Add `tests/unit/dot-dash-3d/sim.test.ts` covering: a cleared board advances and keeps the score, three catches end in game over, chain scoring, wrap edges and fruit. Add a browser case that asserts the overlays.
- `apps/showcase-dot-dash-3d/evidence/captures/` is stale. It holds a failed capture, and `metrics.json` still uses the old title "Dot Dash 3D — Neon Maze Chase". Delete it or regenerate it.
- Nobody has compared fused bloom against the production chain for this game (RP-05). Capture `?capture=1` against `?capture=1&aura3d-render=production` remotely.
- Under SwiftShader the probe counted 56 frames in 5 s, each with dt 1/60, so the sim ran at about 0.19x real time. `__gameEvidence` has no sim-time field, so tooling cannot tell a slow sim from a stalled one. Add `simTime` to the evidence beacon.
- The `hf432-live-d2` "dot-dash dpr2" capture times out at 120 s, cause not isolated (`evidence/visual-regression-4.5.0/prd2.md:55`).

## 14. Cross Hop (`cross-hop`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/showcase-cross-hop/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-cross-hop` · **Genre:** platformer

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. In the Prism probe (SwiftShader software GL, `hdr-msaa` path), it autostarted Quick Play, hops moved one row per tap, the hawk death and HOP AGAIN worked, and there were no console errors or failed requests. Steady-state mean luma was 110.7 at about 12 s, with 0.5% of pixels dark, none clipped and 118 coarse colours, so the frame is lit and varied. The game is playable, but its run logic has several real bugs: coin inflation, score farming, an unwinnable drill, oversized train hitboxes, rail warnings that are not tied to the trains, and a stale look after restart.

**4.5.0 impact:** `git diff v4.3.7 v4.5.0 -- apps/showcase-cross-hop` is empty, so the release reaches this game only through the engine. The one change that matters: the night-city (Emberline, theme 3) and neon-arcade (Neon District, theme 4) looks now carry a neutral fill (`lookPresets.ts:171,189`, `{ preset: "neutral", intensity: 0.85 }`). That fill is only dropped next to an authored ambient light inside `scene().toJSON()` (`scene.ts:157`, the only caller of `dropShadowedLookFill`). Cross Hop's `swapLook` adds the look through `app.nodes.add` (`core.ts:646-647`), so in those two themes the fill, capped at 0.58 by `buildLookGroup` (`core.ts:259-264`), stacks on the game's `lights.ambient` 0.42. Nobody has captured or measured either theme on 4.5.0. Contact shadows, AO and bloom fusion apply but were not compared against 4.3.7. The upgrade guide `docs/upgrade-4.5.0/cross-hop.md:64` still describes the pre-fix `night` fill.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CH-1 | P1 | Run coins are never reset, so every death banks the whole session's coins again | `startRun` (`core.ts:206-220`) resets score and row but never `st.coins`. `die()` does `profile.coins = profile.coins + st.coins` (`core.ts:235-236`). The HUD shows `st.coins + st.profile.coins` (`ui.ts:151`). Headless: a 20-coin run followed by a 0-coin run leaves profile at 40 while the HUD shows 60. The inflated coins buy critter unlocks (`core.ts:31-42`). | Set `st.coins = 0` in `startRun`. Bank coins once per run, in `die()` or on drill completion (`core.ts:579`), and also in `endToTitle`. Add a unit test: runs banking 20 then 0 must leave profile at 20. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-2 | P1 | Hopping back and forth farms score with no limit | `tryHop` adds `st.score++` on every forward hop (`core.ts:543`). Back hops reset `st.idle` (`core.ts:542`), so the hawk never comes. Headless: score 23 with best row 1. `profile.best` (`core.ts:237`) records the farmed score. | Score only new rows: `if (dir === "fwd" && nr > st.row) { st.score++; st.row = nr; }`. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-3 | P1 | Train Dodges drill cannot be won | The drill override (`core.ts:193-194`) changes type, kind and speed but keeps the base lane's `spacing` (meadow 6, road 4.2-8.2). `laneMovers` (`world.ts:115-121`, span 33) therefore spawns 4-6 trains per rail lane, each lethal over 2×(5.6+0.3) = 11.8 m. Measured: rows 3, 4 and 7 are 100% lethal at column 0 for seeds 1-3. | Set `spacing: 60` for rail in the drill override, and per-type spacing for the other types. Add a test that the autopilot clears every `DRILLS` script. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-4 | P1 | Rail warning lights are not tied to the trains, and tram warnings never fire | Normal rail lanes have speed 13-20 (`world.ts:74`), so `laneMovers` takes the looping branch: one train that wraps every 33/speed ≈ 1.6-2.5 s. The `railCycle` single-sweep branch (`world.ts:103-112`) only runs when speed is 0, so it never runs, yet `railWarning` still uses `railCycle` (`world.ts:136-138`). Sim (seed 7, row 4): a train is on the field in 6001/6001 samples and there are 4778 samples of a crossing with no warning. For trams the warning lights only when no tram is within ±16.5 (`world.ts:131-134`), but movers never leave ±16.5 (`world.ts:118`), so the glow lit in 0/6001 samples. | Let `railCycle` drive rail trains (special-case `moverKind === "train"` in `laneMovers`). For trams, warn when a tram will reach the player's column within about 1 s. Delete the inert `"grass"` branch (`core.ts:510-513`). Add a test that every train crossing comes after a warning window. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-5 | P1 | Train, tram and truck hitboxes are much larger than the drawn models | `moverHalf` gives train 5.6, tram 2.35, truck 1.85 (`world.ts:152-160`), plus 0.3 in the kill test (`core.ts:482,487`). Rendered lengths are train 3.2, tram 3.1, truck 2.75 (`targetMaxDimension`, `core.ts:376-378`). The player dies when a train's nose is still 4.3 m away (tram 1.1 m, truck 0.78 m). | Set `moverHalf` to the rendered half-length plus a small margin, or draw a 3-car train so the 11 m kill zone is visible. Add a test that `moverHalf` is within 0.2 m of half the rendered length. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-6 | P1 | Restarting after a theme change keeps the old theme's look, sky and floor | `startRun` sets `st.themeIdx = 0` but calls `swapLook` only when `forceTheme` is defined (`core.ts:216-217`). `landHop` swaps only when the theme differs from `st.themeIdx` (`core.ts:583-588`), which is already 0. After a run that reached Emberline or Neon District, or after a World tour, Quick Play, R or HOP AGAIN draws theme-0 lanes under the old night or neon look. | Track the applied look index and call `swapLook` in `startRun` whenever it differs from the starting theme. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-7 | P2 | Space or Enter re-clicks the last clicked button and restarts the run | Space and Enter are bound to `up` (`main.ts:18`). Nothing calls `blur()` or `preventDefault` (no match in `src`; `GameRuntime.ts:1926` onKeyDown has none). After HOP AGAIN (`ui.ts:77`), Quick Play or a World button (`ui.ts:56-64`) is clicked, it keeps focus, so each Space or Enter press activates it again and calls `startRun`. | Blur the button after the click (or focus the canvas), or call `preventDefault` for keys bound to game actions while a run is live. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-8 | P2 | R and HOP AGAIN drop the World Tour theme and seed | `ui.ts:51` and `ui.ts:77` call `startRun(st.runKind, st.drill)` with no seed or theme. The death auto-restart passes `st.forceTheme` (`core.ts:432`). Pressing R in a Neon tour starts a random theme-0 run, which CH-6 then draws in the Neon look. | Add one `restartRun()` that reuses kind, drill, seed and `forceTheme`, and call it from all three restart paths. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-9 | P2 | Any key press while dead restarts at once and skips the death card | `frame` restarts on any buffered direction with no lockout (`core.ts:424-433`). The input buffer is 140 ms (`main.ts:24`). In the live probe's 14-tap test, a death at row 7 went straight to row 1 without the 500 ms poll ever seeing `mode: "dead"`. | Accept a restart only after `st.deadAt + 0.6 s`, and consume the action that triggered it. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-10 | P2 | 4.5.0 adds a look fill in Emberline and Neon District on top of the authored ambient light | See 4.5.0 impact: `swapLook` → `app.nodes.add` (`core.ts:646-647`) bypasses `dropShadowedLookFill` (`scene.ts:157`). The fill (0.85, capped to 0.58) stacks with `lights.ambient` 0.42 (`core.ts:314`). Not measured. | Drop `lookFill`-marked children in `buildLookGroup`, or make the engine yield in environment resolution so runtime adds and `toJSON` agree (release item LIGHT-04). Then capture themes 3 and 4 against 4.3.7. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-11 | P2 | River and lava hops snap sideways to any platform, with no distance limit | `tryHop` moves `nc` to the nearest platform anywhere inside `FIELD_HALF - 0.5` (`core.ts:515-536`). Log spacing is up to 6.8 m, so one hop can shift about 3.4 m sideways, and hopping into open water never drowns the player while any platform is on screen. | Snap only if `bestDist ≤ 0.9` m; otherwise land in the water and die. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-12 | P2 | Spectators and fence posts stand inside traffic lanes, and vehicles wrap inside the drawn strip | Movers wrap at ±16.5 (`world.ts:117-120`) on a 34 m strip. Fence posts sit at x = 14.6 on every even row, roads and rails included (`core.ts:914,944-945`). Spectators sit at \|x\| 13.8-16.1 at the lane's top Y on any lane type (`core.ts:776-781`), so they can stand on water or lava. The comment at `core.ts:921` claims scenery stays beyond the fence. Vehicles disappear at one edge and reappear at the other while still on the visible ground. | Place spectators on meadow rows only, at \|x\| ≥ 17.5. Make the wrap span exceed the strip plus the vehicle length, and keep collision limited to `FIELD_HALF`. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-13 | P2 | The coin model shows two coins for one pickup | The coin bounds in `aura.assets.json` are [1.903, 0.705, 0.081], a 2.7:1 strip. At `targetHeight` 0.46 it renders about 1.24 m wide on a 1 m column. `390-02-early.jpg` shows two coins on one lane, but only `coinCol` collects (`core.ts:550`). The live GLB is 2.33 MB. | Admit a single-coin GLB with textures ≤512 and bounds near 1:1, checked with `assets inspect`. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
| CH-14 | P2 | Horn, rescue and UI sound cues are defined but never played | Cues are defined at `main.ts:49-52`, and a rescue juice at `main.ts:62`. Only hop, land, coin, theme and death cues fire (`main.ts:109-127`). The rail warning is visual only (`core.ts:895-898`). | Play `horn` when a nearby rail lane enters its warning window (after CH-4). Play `rescue` and fire its juice on pod pickup. Play `ui` on menu buttons. | done 0dfc082fd [4.6.1] |
