**Tests and evidence gaps**

- The tests cover lane generation only (`tests/lanes.test.ts`, 54 lines). Nothing tests `core.ts` scoring, coin banking, restart, drills, hitboxes or rail cycles, which is how CH-1 to CH-5 shipped.
- `screens/final` dates from d658eb2a1 (2026-10-25), before the last source commit c45022372 and before 4.5.0. There are no frames of Emberline, Neon District, a drill or the death card.
- The `crosshop` target in `tests/browser/game-luma-capture.spec.ts` captures theme 0 only, and no Cross Hop result is recorded anywhere. Capture `?theme=3` and `?theme=4` after a swap, on 4.3.7 and on 4.5.0, on the remote runner.
- Bloom fusion was never compared against `?aura3d-render=production` for this game (RP-05).
- The live probe saw `materials.textureBytes` grow from 114.3 MB to 126.9 MB (+11%) across two restarts in the same theme. Run a 20-restart loop to tell lazy loading from a leak.
- No draw-call or frame-time numbers are in evidence (checklist line 40). The live probe's figures come from SwiftShader only.

**Dropped on verification**

- The live probe's double hop from one 200 ms ArrowUp press: dispatched keydown/keyup pairs moved exactly one row each, so this comes from how Prism delivers a held key, not from the game.
- The live probe's note that `main.ts:79-81` drops the sim's 0.14 s window: both values are 140 ms today, so nothing goes wrong; this is a code smell only.
- The about 1 fps rAF rate in the probe: this is a SwiftShader artifact, not a game defect.

## 15. Craterline GP (`strange-orbit-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/craterline-gp/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-strange-orbit-port` · **Genre:** racing

**State:** The game loads and plays on production with a keyboard. The live probe ran on the Prism cloud browser (SwiftShader software GL) and saw no console errors and no failed requests. The page used renderPath `hdr-msaa`, 115 draw calls and 41 assets ready. Mean luma was 85.4 on the menu and 76.9 while racing, with 0.2% of pixels below 12, so the frame is not blank. Restart works. The game has three problems a player hits: there is no touch input at all, every difficulty is easy to win, and 6th place awards a bronze medal. The app is a 3-line wrapper around `examples/strange-orbit-port/` (about 3,900 lines). That source did not change between v4.3.7 and v4.5.0; the only change is the version bump in package.json.

**4.5.0 impact:** The game code is unchanged, but the engine under it changed. First, `renderer: { mode, qualityProfile }` (examples/strange-orbit-port/main.ts:479) no longer picks the pipeline, so the game now runs on quality `auto` with separable bloom fusion and the shadow-cascade skip. Nobody has rendered or compared it in that mode. Second, the scene authors hemisphere, key and fill lights and has no `environments.*` node (world.ts:181-190; `rg environments examples/strange-orbit-port` finds nothing). In 4.5.0 such a scene loses the implicit neutral-room IBL. The custom-GLSL track, sky and islands compute their own light (shaders.ts:28-41) and are unaffected. The eight lit Meshy/catalog GLBs (riders, pylons, boulders, lander, observatory, moon rocks) lose their diffuse and specular fill. docs/upgrade-4.5.0/strange-orbit-port.md recommends `environments.studio`, and that change was not applied. The only number is a whole-frame menu luma that went from 55.6 to 54.4 (-2%, evidence/exposure-parity/README.md:48), and it mostly measures sky. The game is not broken by the release, but its lighting needs a measured region check and probably an environment.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SO-1 | P1 | No touch or pointer controls, so the game cannot be played on phones or tablets | All input comes from `keydown`/`keyup` (main.ts:123-136). The only pointer handler is `addEventListener("pointerdown", () => sound.unlock())` (main.ts:500). `rg -n "touch\|pointer" examples/strange-orbit-port/*.ts` finds only that line, comments and CSS `pointer-events`. A phone user can tap "Let's ride" and then cannot pedal, steer, boost or pause. The 390 px final screens were driven through QA hooks, so they hide this. | Add a touch layer using apps/showcase-port-shared/quickbar.css: steer zones or drag-to-steer feeding `steerSmooth`, auto-pedal while touching, and 48 px Boost, Jump, Gravity and Pause buttons with safe-area insets. Set `touch-action: none` on the canvas. Add a remote spec that drives a race by touch. | done 3f5f54883, c73902f0e [4.6.0] |
| SO-2 | P2 | 6th of 6 (and 4th or 5th) gets a "Bronze medal"; 2nd and 3rd both get silver | race.ts:970 `e.place === 1 ? "gold" : e.place <= 3 ? "silver" : "bronze"`. Probe: a 1-lap race at reduced pace finished `place 6 medal bronze`. main.ts:210 prints it as the results heading. | Map places 1/2/3 to gold/silver/bronze. Widen `RaceEnd["medal"]` (race.ts:125, currently `"gold" \| "silver" \| "bronze"`, no null) to allow `null`, and show "No medal · finished 6th". Update the results test. | done 96e769acb [4.6.0] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- Nothing has been rendered since 4.5.0. The last commit to evidence/strange-orbit-port/screens/final is 9e89ef082 (2026-10-25), and 4.5.0 shipped 2026-10-28. route-health.json is from 2026-10-23. The index posters predate the release too (RH-05).
- The game has no 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state luma capture. Add the route `/apps/craterline-gp/` to tests/browser/game-luma-capture.spec.ts TARGETS and measure luma inside the rider region (LIGHT-01, LIGHT-02).
- Fused bloom (`separable`, on `auto`) has never been compared with `?aura3d-render=production` for this game (RP-05).
- The upgrade guide's checkboxes are open, and the guide says the GLB change was not rendered (RH-04).
- No browser spec drives a race by touch. The unit suite (131 tests) has no test of difficulty balance and no test of the medal-by-place mapping.
- Every browser run so far used SwiftShader. The game's fixed-step loop caps each frame at 0.08 s (main.ts:179-182), so on software GL the race ran at about 0.03x wall time. No real-GPU frame exists (tools/real-gpu/run.sh).
- A typo in `?aura3d-render=` or `?aura3d-bloom-fusion=` throws during mount (RP-04, engine-wide). This is not specific to this game.

**Dropped on verification**

- Code-audit C7's citation `README.md:44` was wrong; the claim is on line 42. It is kept as SO-6 with the corrected line.
- Code-audit C4's statement that "RaceEnd.medal allows null" is wrong: race.ts:125 is a non-null union. The fix in SO-2 includes widening the type.
- LIGHT-01's P1 rating is downgraded to P2 (merged into SO-4). The only measurement is -2% whole-frame, and no region capture shows the models visibly darker.
- LIGHT-08 (flat hemisphere mean) is an engine-wide item with no effect specific to this game, so it is not listed here.
- C3's suggested fix "add rival boost use" is not needed: rivals already boost (race.ts:853, +20 cruise). Only surge and rubber-banding are missing.

## 16. Chroma Roll (`splat-rush-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/chroma-roll/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-splat-rush-port` · **Genre:** platformer

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. The live probe found no errors, no failed requests (38 resources), webgl2 on the `hdr-msaa` path, and all 28 assets `ready`. Splash, play and game-over frames had mean luma 191-197 and 0% dark pixels, with 21-32% at pure white because the scene uses `toneMapping: 'none'` (main.ts:212). Clicking to start, holding ArrowLeft to steer (x 0 to -1.06 in 1.5 s), Space to restart, and best-score persistence all worked. The probe ran on SwiftShader software GL at 0.8 fps, so its frame rates say nothing about real GPUs. The game code is in `examples/splat-rush-port`; `apps/showcase-splat-rush-port/src/main.ts` only mounts it. Overall it is playable, but three P1 defects need fixing.

**4.5.0 impact:** The game's only 4.5.0 commit is a version bump. The engine change that matters is "authored lights only" (`compiler/renderer.ts:152` passes `implicitFill: false`, and `compiler/environment.ts:172-179` returns preset `authored-lights`). This scene authors a hemisphere light plus two directional lights and no `environments.*` node (backdrop.ts:49-52), so its Meshy GLBs lost the neutral-room IBL they had on 4.3.7. In the same replayed state, they render 39-50% darker than the pre-release capture. The toon road, gates and ball use custom GLSL and do not change. The other 4.5.0 changes (direct path, cascade skip, dense instancing, AO/DoF depth, bloom fusion, look fill, OPTION_IGNORED) do not reach this game: it authors output options and custom materials, and it has no `looks.preset`, shadows, post effects, `instances.*` or `primitives.*`. The release needs one fix here: add an environment.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 (merges LIGHT-01) | P1 | 4.5.0 regression: islands, towers, balloons and pickups render 39-50% darker | backdrop.ts:49-52 has hemisphere 1.15 + directionals 1.6/0.5 and no `environments.*` node. Live diagnostics report `preset: "authored-lights"`. Replaying the evidence "roll" state on live (frameCount 466, score 1839, matching the capture log) and comparing it with `card@2x.jpg` (built from 0d965903c, before 4.5.0), both at 720x450: tower luma 220.4 to 134.1, island underside 189.3 to 94.9, far island 173.3 to 117.7, balloon 168.7 to 99.9, road 151.2 to 150.8 (control). gem-double.glb has mean metalness 0.79, so it gets almost no light without IBL. | Add `environments.neutral({ intensity })` or `environments.studio()` in `addBackdrop` (backdrop.ts:48-56). Tune it until tower and island luma are within about 5% of `card@2x.jpg`. The toon shaders ignore engine lights, so the road and gates stay unchanged. | done bba38400d [4.6.0] |
| C2 | P1 | Popups stop for the rest of the page session after the 64th, and the PERFECT sound and flash fall back to the plain "match" ones | sim.ts:199 caps the list (`if (this.popups.length > 64) this.popups.shift()`), and `begin()` (sim.ts:287-302) never clears it. main.ts:91 shows popups by index (`while (popupShown < sim.popups.length)`), and restart sets `popupShown = sim.popups.length` (main.ts:257). Once the length is pinned at 64, the loop never runs. In the code audit's probe, the last popup showed at step 1172 (about 19.5 s of play) out of 478 created, and with idle restarts popups stop from run 16. `perfectNow` is set inside that loop (main.ts:93), so the perfect sound (audio.ts:44) and the 0.42 flash (fx.ts) never fire after that. | Give each popup a monotonic `seq` that is not part of the trace, and show the popups whose seq is above `lastShownSeq`. Add a test that runs more than 100 popups across 3 restarts. | done ecd274dbc, d36f9ee7b [4.6.0] |
| C3 | P1 | The "Built with Aura3D" link covers the bottom half of the mute button, so a click can leave the page | index.html:39: `.icon-btn` sits at left 16, bottom 16, 44x44, z-index 12. index.html:19: `.aura-credit` sits at left 8, bottom 8, z-index 2147483000, with `pointer-events: auto` on its link. Live at 1440x900: mute rect [16,840,44,44], link [17,871,99,14]. `elementFromPoint` at (26,878) and (54,876) returns the A element. The credit has no `no-steer` class, so the window pointerdown (main.ts:231-240) also starts the run from the splash as the page navigates away. | Move the mute button to `bottom: 48px` or move the credit to the right on this page. Add `no-steer` to `.aura-credit`. Add a hit-test assertion at the mute button's centre and corners. | done d36f9ee7b [4.6.0] |
| C4 | P2 | After a restart the ball drifts toward the previous run's steering target | `begin()` (sim.ts:287-302) resets `laneOffset` but not `this.ye` or `this.dragging`, and `frame()` lerps toward `ye` (sim.ts:441). The original has the same behaviour (game-module.formatted.js:416). | Set `this.ye = 0; this.dragging = false;` in `begin()`. The reference replays start from the splash, so parity holds. Add a restart test. | done ecd274dbc [4.6.0] |

There is no `setPointerCapture`, and `dragging` clears only on pointerup or pointercancel (main.ts:247-253). | On `blur` and when `document.hidden`: clear `held`, set `keyDir = 0` and `dragging = false`, and send `pending.up`. **Tests and evidence gaps**

- `evidence/splat-rush-port/screens/final` was built from 0d965903c on 2026-10-25, before 4.5.0. The live frame differs (C1). The posters are also stale (RH-05). Recapture both after fixing C1.
- `evidence/exposure-parity/README.md:42` says all 26 showcase games bring an environment and moved 0%. That is false for this game, which is `authored-lights`. Line 51 only measured the title screen at 320x200, with identical before and after values.
- `route-health.json:24` says "No audio" and that the flash and vignette are not ported, but `audio.ts` and `fx.ts` ship both. It was generated on 2026-10-23 and needs regenerating.
- The game is not in `tests/browser/game-luma-capture.spec.ts` TARGETS (LIGHT-02). Add a steady-state region-luma check on the backdrop models.
- The unit tests (`tests/unit/splat-rush-port/`) check `sim.popups` content only. Nothing covers the main.ts display path across restarts (C2), restart steering state (C4) or overlay hit-testing (C3).
- `docs/upgrade-4.5.0` has open checklist items for this game, and no 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 capture exists (RH-04). No real-GPU capture or frame timing exists either.

**Dropped on verification**

- C12 and C13 were folded into the gaps above. LIGHT-01 was merged into C1, and LIGHT-08 (flat hemisphere mean) is an engine-wide item with no measurement specific to this game. Nothing was refuted outright: every cited line matched the checkout at 31f8945ad.

## 17. Serpentine Sky Run (`serpentine-sky-run-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/serpentine-sky-run/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-serpentine-sky-run-port` · **Genre:** platformer

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. In the Prism cloud browser (SwiftShader software GL, 1440x900, about 0.7 fps), boot showed no errors or failed requests (42 resources). The canvas was never blank: mean luma was 188 on the menu, 182 in a run and 207 in the fall outro. Start, lane change, fall, results and restart (KeyR) all worked. The app folder is a 3-line shim, and the whole game lives in `examples/saiyan-snake-way-port`, which has had no commits since v4.3.7. Overall health is degraded. In 3 of 4 `?manual=1` loads, a single GL error permanently froze the renderer. The browser specs also point at a global name that no longer exists, so most of the game's browser coverage cannot run.

**4.5.0 impact:** The game's source did not change, so every 4.5.0 effect comes from the engine. The render path is `hdr-msaa` on `auto`, with fused bloom (`bloomFusion: separable`). That fusion was never compared against the production chain for this game. The game passes no option that 4.5.0 now ignores, so no OPTION_IGNORED diagnostic fires. The "lights-only scenes get no implicit environment" change applies: `world.ts:158-159` authors only a hemisphere light (1.1) and a sun (2.2), with no `environments.*` and no look preset. On 4.3.7 the neutral-room probe lit the PBR runner and the Meshy props. On 4.5.0 they get only the flat hemisphere mean, and nobody has measured the result. The 120/144 Hz interpolation fix is in place (`main.ts:244-256`). The fix that resizes the canvas after ready also applies. Cascade skip, dense instancing, AO/DoF and the contact-shadow mask do not apply to this game.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | P1 | One GL error freezes the game for the rest of the session behind "The road could not load". | `LegacyPost.ts:564-568` reads any pending GL error and throws `RenderDeviceError ... 0x502 [WEBGL_LDR_POSTPROCESS_FAILED]`. `main.ts:247-249` catches it and sets `renderFailed = true`. After that, `main.ts:242` returns early on every frame, and `main.ts:317-318` shows the loading overlay with the error. Live `?manual=1` runs failed in 3 of 4 loads (at s=-41, around 305-337 m, and at 315 m with `?aura3d-render=production`). Two normal-play sessions (479 m and 227 m) did not fail. After the freeze, draw calls stayed at 146 while the sim kept stepping. | Skip the frame and log the error. Latch failure only after N consecutive errors or a context loss. Bisect the pass that leaves `GL_INVALID_OPERATION` pending, using `?diag=1` (per-frame GL error check). It also happens with production quality, so fused bloom alone does not explain it. | done cb65cbbd9 [4.6.0] |
| C2 | P1 | The browser specs and the error global use the stale name `__AURA_SERPENTINE_SKY_RUN__`. The live hook is `__AURA_SAIYAN_SNAKE_WAY__`. | `main.ts:324` calls `installQaHooks("saiyan-snake-way", ...)`. `tests/browser/saiyan-snake-way-art.spec.ts:58-59` (and the -port, -live, -meshy, -match and `shotsb-serpentine` specs) wait for `__AURA_SERPENTINE_SKY_RUN__.ready`, which is never set. `main.ts:316` writes `__AURA_SERPENTINE_SKY_RUN_ERROR__`, and the hook's own error global is never set. Live `Object.keys(window)` shows only `__AURA_SAIYAN_SNAKE_WAY__`. The live chunk also ships the trademark id `saiyan-snake-way` (RH-07). | Rename the hook to `serpentine-sky-run`, which fixes the specs and removes the trademark id from the bundle in the same change. Send `reportError` through the reporter that `installQaHooks` returns. Add a unit test that checks the hook name against the specs. | done cb65cbbd9 [4.6.0] |

If they read darker, add `environments.neutral({ intensity })` and calibrate it to the 4.3.7 luma. Raise to P1 if the capture shows darkening. ## 18. Gridlock Courier (`drift-and-deliver-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/gridlock-courier/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-drift-and-deliver-port` · **Genre:** racing

**State:** The production page loads clean in the Prism cloud browser: 0 console errors, 0 failed requests out of 53 resources, all 94 asset entries ready, 20 programs compiled with 0 failures, 112 draw calls, render path `hdr-msaa`. The renderer was SwiftShader (software GL) at about 0.2 fps, so the probe proves correctness, not speed. Steady-state frames stay lit and varied: mean luma 110.3 to 110.4, 0% dark, 0% clipped, 88 to 90 colours at 175 s, 359 s and 1194 s. Driving works (holding W moved the car from z -120 to -107 and the HUD read 32 km/h), and so do pickups and the streak (score 0 to 10, '1 / 174'). The app folder is a 3-line shim over `examples/drift-and-deliver-port`. The deterministic sim passes its 130 unit tests. The game is healthy but incomplete: it has no touch input, no end state, and the canvas does not follow window resizes.

**4.5.0 impact:** 4.5.0 changed no game source, only the `package.json` version. Engine changes still reach the game, because `createAuraApp` (`examples/drift-and-deliver-port/main.ts:454-461`) sets no `quality`, so the game runs on `auto`. The live diagnostics confirm `{path:'hdr-msaa', quality:'auto', bloomFusion:'separable'}`. That turns on separable bloom fusion for the 0.35 bloom (`look.ts:181`) and cascade skipping for the 2-cascade 2048 px sun shadow (`look.ts:176`). Neither has been compared against `?aura3d-render=production` on this game. The 1.7-intensity hemisphere light (`look.ts:177`) sits next to `environments.hdri` (`look.ts:175`), so the engine still drops it, the same as in 4.3.7 (LIGHT-05). The heal ladder's `no-env` rung (`packages/debug/src/safety/blackFrame.ts:23`) removes the HDRI, so lighting on that rung now depends on the 4.5.0 lights-only path; that rung has never been captured. AO is opt-in (`?ao=1`, `look.ts:179`), so the real-depth AO change does not affect default frames. I found no regression in code, but no post-4.5.0 capture exists. The newest final screens come from build `0d965903c`, which is older than four later game fixes: the minimap fix `cb2933126`, the hatchback hero car `4507f5f19`, the cue bank and black-frame guard `c45022372`, and the road mapping fix `2073d570a`. I checked each one with `git merge-base --is-ancestor`, and none is in that build.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | P1 | No touch controls. Phones and tablets can tap Start but cannot steer, accelerate, pause or mute. | `rg -n "touch\|pointer" examples/drift-and-deliver-port/*.ts` finds nothing. The only input is keyboard: KEYMAP plus `keydown`/`keyup` on window at `main.ts:324-337`. The splash lists only keyboard keys. `#hud` has `pointer-events:none`, so the mute label cannot be tapped. The 390x844 evidence frames were driven through QA hooks. | For `(pointer: coarse)`, add a left steering zone and right-side 44 px gas, brake, drift and nitro buttons, plus pause and mute, all writing into the existing `keys` object. Show touch hints on the splash. Add a touch-emulated 390x844 spec asserting `speedKmh > 0`. | done 351fab8a9, 7a570744d [4.6.1] |
| C2 | P1 | Resizing the window or rotating a phone does not resize the canvas: it crops, or leaves bare background. | `main.ts:456` mounts with `resize: false`. At mount, `packages/engine/src/agent-api/app/canvas.ts:98-99` writes `canvas.style.width/height` in px, which overrides the page's `100vw/100vh`. `frameLoop.ts:156` returns early for `resize:false`, and no observer or listener is registered (`:175-179`). The game's own `sizeCanvas` (`main.ts:445-449`, wired at `:510`) reads `canvas.clientWidth`, which is now pinned, so the size never changes. Same code in 4.3.7. This is lesson R1. Source-only; not reproduced in a browser. | Remove `sizeCanvas` and mount with `resize: true` and `pixelRatio: Math.min(devicePixelRatio, low ? 1 : 1.5)`, so the engine owns CSS and backing size. Add a spec that changes the viewport from 1280x720 to 900x600 after ready and asserts the canvas follows. | done 351fab8a9 [4.6.1] |
| C3 | P1 | The run never ends: no objective completion, no results screen, no restart. | `game.phase` is only `"splash" \| "playing" \| "paused"` (`main.ts:89`). Nothing checks `collected === pickups.length`, and there is no timer. Reset exists only as a QA hook. The capture README:27 says 'Not reached: results (this capture spec has no results state)'. Breaks lessons G1 and G7. Confirmed live. | Add a timed shift (for example 3:00, or deliveries) and a results overlay showing score, collected/174, best streak and time, with a Restart button and an R key. Restart rebuilds car, pickups, traffic, combo, presenter and camera. Store the best score in localStorage. Add a unit test for the transition and a probe that reaches results. | done 351fab8a9 [4.6.1] |
| C4 | P2 | Engine drone, squeal and nitro rush keep sounding while paused and in a hidden tab. | `audio.update` runs only from rAF (`main.ts:498`). While paused it receives speed 0, so the engine gain stays at its idle level (`engineGain`). **Tests and evidence gaps**

- No capture since 4.5.0. The 14 final screens in `evidence/drift-and-deliver-port/screens/final` come from build `0d965903c` (README:3). They show the old blue hypercar instead of the shipped hatchback. In `drift-390.jpg` the minimap covers the speed readout; the current CSS should fix that, but no frame proves it. Every desktop frame was taken with `environmentReady:false`. The site card and poster come from the same stale frames (C7, RH-05). Recapture at 1280x720 and 390x844 on the remote runner from 4.5.0, after C3 adds a results state.
- No A/B of `auto` against `?aura3d-render=production` for this game, so the effect of separable bloom fusion and cascade skipping is unmeasured (C8, RP-05). Record mean and max pixel difference on a fixed teleport frame.
- No 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state luma comparison, and the game is not in the `tests/browser/game-luma-capture.spec.ts` TARGETS list (LIGHT-02). No real-GPU run.
- The heal-ladder `no-env` rung has never been captured on 4.5.0. `tests/browser/drift-and-deliver-heal.spec.ts` predates the release. Add a mean-luma check against rung 0.
- `docs/upgrade-4.5.0/drift-and-deliver-port.md` still has 8 unchecked items (RH-04).
- Missing specs: touch driving (C1), viewport resize (C2), reaching a results screen (C3), audio silent when paused (C4), zero same-lane traffic overlap (C6).

**Dropped on verification**

- C7 and C8 were merged into the evidence-gap bullets above. They describe missing receipts, not defects. I also dropped C8's claim that degraded heal rungs are 'darker than before': with the HDRI removed, the scene may switch to the path where the hemisphere light contributes, so the luma could move either way, and nobody has measured it.
- RP-04 (a typo in a debug URL switch stops the mount) is an engine-wide robustness item. It is reported in the release section, not per game, and no player reaches it.
- LIGHT-05 and LIGHT-06 are covered under 4.5.0 impact. LIGHT-05 behaves the same as 4.3.7 on this game. LIGHT-06 does not apply to default frames, because AO here is opt-in (`?ao=1`, `look.ts:179`) and the game uses no look preset.

## 19. Ion Circuit (`heatsink-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/ion-circuit/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-heatsink-port` (mounts `examples/heatsink-port`) · **Genre:** racing

**State:** The live route loads cleanly. The page makes 44 requests with none failing, and all 9 Meshy GLBs return 200 at 0.95 to 1.93 MB each. Diagnostics report backend webgl2 on the hdr-msaa path with 68 to 90 draw calls and errors `[]`. In a Prism cloud browser (SwiftShader software GL, 0.27 fps at 1440x900), Start ran the 3-2-1 countdown, and holding W reached 117 km/h with dist going from -25 to 85 m and 0 crashes. Steady-state luma was 48.4 to 54.0 mean, with a 63 to 66 centre, 9 to 11% of pixels below 12 and 0% clipped. That is the darkest of the probed games and has the lowest colour variety (36 to 45 coarse colours). The game plays, but three defects reach every player: the "GO" banner never clears, barrels are mostly unhittable, and there are no touch controls.

**4.5.0 impact:** The app folder only got a version bump, and the game code in `examples/heatsink-port` did not change. Two engine changes do apply to it. (1) The scene authors only `lights.hemisphere` and `lights.directional` (world.ts:101-102), with no `environments.*` or `looks.*`. Under 4.5.0 the lit Meshy models (drone, barrels, pickups, pylons, scenery) therefore lose the implicit neutral-room IBL and get only a flat hemisphere mean, about 0.207 linear. Nobody has measured this, and the exposure-parity README states the opposite. (2) Fused bloom (`bloomFusion: 'separable'` on quality auto) replaces the 4.3.7 production bloom chain, and no per-game comparison was made. Both changes need a before-and-after capture before 4.5.0 can be called fine for this game.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| HP-01 | P1 | "GO" banner stays up for the whole race and hides the objective / next-target line | `race.ts:97-103` lowers `countdown` only while `state === "countdown"`, so it freezes just below 0 (sim: -0.0167 at steps 192 to 399). `main.ts:102` shows "GO" while `countdown > -0.8 && state === "race"`, which is always true. `apps/showcase-heatsink-port/index.html:54` `body:has(#count:not(.hidden)) #objective { visibility: hidden; }` (added in 4.3.7, cb2933126) then hides the objective all race, under a 96 px "GO" (`index.html:49`). | Record time since the race state began (`goT` in race.ts) and show GO only while it is under 0.8 s. Add a browser assertion that `#count` is hidden and `#objective` is visible 1 s after GO. | done 25a7e790f [4.6.1] |
| HP-02 | P1 | Bolts fly about 3.9 m above the barrels' hit centre, so "shoot barrels" rarely works | `operation.ts:200` fires at `p.position.y` with `dy = 0` (`operation.ts:198`). The hover height is `ground + 5.3` (`config.ts:4` `hover: 5.3`, `vehicle.ts:105`). The barrel sphere is centred at `bar.y + 1.1` (`operation.ts:226`) with radius barrelRadius + assistSlack = 3.3. Lane-follow bot: median dy 3.84 to 3.96 and p10 3.63 over the 40 m before each barrel, with 0 to 9 of 12 barrels shot. `tests/unit/heatsink-port/strike.test.ts:188` places the player at `barrel.y + 1.1`, which hides the problem. | Give barrels a vertical capsule from ground to ground + 6, or slope `dy` toward the nearest barrel inside the assist cone. Add a unit test that fires from the real hover height. | done 25a7e790f [4.6.1] |
| HP-03 | P1 | No touch controls, so the game cannot be played on a phone or tablet | `main.ts:60-70` binds the keyboard plus `canvas pointerdown` for fire only. rg for `touch`, `pointercancel` and `matchMedia` in `examples/heatsink-port` finds nothing. Without throttle the speed stays 0. The menu lists keys only. A cancelled touch leaves `pointerFire` stuck true, because there is no pointercancel handler. | Add coarse-pointer controls (steer pad or zones, auto-throttle, Fire, Boost and Drift buttons of at least 44 px, safe-area insets) and handle `pointercancel`. Until those exist, show a "keyboard required" notice on touch devices. | done 84445d206 [4.6.1] |
| HP-04 | P2 | 4.5.0 lights-only rule applies to this game and is unmeasured; the evidence claims it does not apply | `world.ts:101-102` has hemisphere 1.1 (#6fa8d8/#2a1a10) plus directional 2.6, with no environment. `evidence/exposure-parity/README.md:42` says "All 26 showcase games ... bring an environment ... luma moves by 0%", which is false here. The app-route parity frame recorded a 404 with identical before/after stats, and games-luma.json shows `RENDERER_MOUNT_FAILED` for `/apps/showcase-heatsink-port`. The examples route moved only -2% in whole-frame luma, which is dominated by unlit custom shaders. This merges LIGHT-01. | Capture 4.3.7 against 4.5.0 on the app route with the GLBs staged, and measure the drone and barrel regions. If they read darker, add `environments.neutral({ intensity })` calibrated to the 4.3.7 luma. Correct README line 42. | done 317c449a2 (in part: the `evidence/exposure-parity/README.md:42` line is an open handoff to E5) [4.6.1] |
| HP-05 | P2 | Any exception in `render()` kills the frame loop for good, with no error panel | `main.ts:136-143` `tick()` calls `render()` before `requestAnimationFrame(tick)`, and `render()` (`main.ts:114-122`) has no try/catch. Live probe: after a non-finite vertex throw (`VertexBuffer.ts:40`), the loop advanced 0 steps in 8 s, `data-aura3d-error` stayed null, and `reset()` did not recover because later `stepFrames` calls threw again. splat-rush and saiyan ports wrap render in try/catch. | Schedule rAF first or wrap render in try/catch, report through the boot error panel, and make reset rebuild the dynamic buffers. | done 84445d206, 619ba5f9a [4.6.1] |
| HP-06 | P2 | Hull/crash budget, gun lockout and crash reason are never shown, and `#notice` is never written | `hud.ts:77` computes the crash budget and `hud.ts:94-102` exposes `medal` and `gunLocked`, but `updateHud` (`main.ts:95-110`) writes none of them. `#notice` (`index.html:99`) has no writer anywhere. DNF arrives after the 4th crash (crashBudget 3, `config.ts:4`) with no warning. | Add a HULL n/4 pip row and a "LOCKED" gun state. Write the crash reason and "n crashes left" to `#notice` for 1.5 s. | done 84445d206 [4.6.1] |
| HP-07 | P2 | Pause has no UI, keeps animating, and does not auto-pause | `main.ts:64`: Esc toggles `paused` with no overlay. `main.ts:141` still advances `renderTime` and renders, so targets, rings and streaks keep moving. On blur, `main.ts:69` clears keys but does not pause, and there is no `visibilitychange` handler. | Add a pause panel (Resume/Restart), freeze `renderTime` while paused, and auto-pause on blur and on hidden during the race. | done 84445d206 [4.6.1] |
| HP-08 | P2 | Drone hovers 5.3 m above the road with no shadow or contact cue; the shadow evidence is wrong | `models.ts:30,33,36,67,90` set `castShadow: false` on the drone, barrels, pickups, scenery and pylons. `evidence/heatsink-port/shadows/README.md:9` says "the drone, gate pylons and props do [cast]". Live `appliedLook.shadows` reports `mapRendered:false`, yet the directional light still configures 2 cascades (`world.ts:102`). | Add a blob shadow quad under the drone, scaled and faded by height, or enable the drone caster. Fix the README, or drop the unused shadow config. | done 84445d206, 0ef109652, 9fc48bd66 [4.6.1] |
| HP-09 | P3 | Pressing Enter during loading starts the race behind the menu | `main.ts:65` calls `begin()` on Enter when `!started`, even before `ready`. `boot()` then un-hides `#menu` (`main.ts:261`), and `index.html:59` hides the HUD and count under it while the countdown runs. | Guard the handler with `ready`, or skip the menu when `started` is true. | done 84445d206 [4.6.1] |
| HP-10 | P3 | QA hook `stepFrames(n, Partial<Input>)` does not merge a partial input, which produces NaN | `main.ts:181` `manualInput = (input as Input \| undefined) ?? heldInput ?? IDLE`, so a missing `steer` becomes undefined and player.x becomes NaN. This triggered HP-05 live. | `{ ...IDLE, ...input }`. | done 84445d206 [4.6.1] |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- `tests/browser/shotsb-circuit.spec.ts:21-24` force-hides `#count` before every frame, which masked HP-01. `heatsink-strike.spec.ts` checks only `textContent`.
- `evidence/heatsink-port/screens/final` (9e89ef082, 2026-10-25) predates the countdown CSS fix and 4.5.0. Its "results" frames show a race in progress, and no finished or DNF card has ever been captured. Draw calls were not recorded.
- There is no 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 luma capture of the app route (LIGHT-01/02, RH-04). The upgrade guide `docs/upgrade-4.5.0/heatsink-port.md:51` says "I did not measure" the bloom change (RP-05). `game-render-path-parity.spec.ts` defaults to Thermal Drift, not this game.
- No unit test fires at real hover height (HP-02), and there is no browser test for touch or pause.
- No real-GPU run exists. All live numbers come from software GL at 0.27 fps.

**Dropped on verification**

- RP-04 (a typo in a debug URL switch blocks the mount) is engine-wide, not specific to this game, and is covered in the release section.
- LIGHT-08 (hemisphere applied as a flat mean) is an engine enhancement and is covered in the release section. Its effect here is folded into HP-04.

## 20. Shadowstep Gauntlet (`ninja-runner-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/shadowstep-gauntlet/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-ninja-runner-port` (game code in `examples/ninja-runner-port`) · **Genre:** action

**State:** The game loads and plays to the end on production. In the Prism probe (SwiftShader software GL, 1440x900) the page showed no errors and no failed requests across 65 resources. `diagnostics()` reported 93 draw calls, renderPath `hdr-msaa` with `bloomFusion: separable`, and 0 failed programs. Mean luma was 125.6 on the menu and 122.9 on the won screen, with 0% dark and 0% clipped pixels. The autopilot run reached `won` at 2,640 m with 74 defeated, a real KeyD press changed lane, and Enter restarted the run. Health is good. The main costs are a heavy first load (26.75 MB transferred) and a few gaps in pause and input.

**4.5.0 impact:** The release changed no game code. `git diff --stat v4.3.7 v4.5.0` touches only `apps/showcase-ninja-runner-port/package.json`. Rendering did change. `main.ts:423` passes `renderer: { mode: "production", qualityProfile: "production" }` and no `quality`, so 4.5.0 resolves quality to `auto` (`renderPath.ts:151-156`). That turns on separable bloom fusion and the unsampled-cascade skip, which the live probe confirmed (`bloomFusion: separable`). The high tier's `effects.ambientOcclusion` (`world.ts:235`) now reads real depth. No frame of this game has been taken on 4.5.0: every file in `evidence/ninja-runner-port/screens/final` dates from 2026-10-25. The live frames look correct, but the size of the shift from 4.3.7 is unmeasured. The 4.3.0 shadow-fit opt-in (S-01) dates from 4.3.2 (commit 3405d2082), not 4.5.0.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S-01 | P2 | The game opts the whole page into the 4.3.0 shadow fit only so the 4000 m sky dome is dropped as a shadow caster. The CHANGELOG records that this fit rendered Aura Slam dark and striped on a real Mac GPU. | `world.ts:218-219` sets `globalThis.__A3D_SHADOW_FIT__ = "current"` whenever the sun shadow is on (the default). The live bundle `showcase-ninja-runner-port-B9k0XEgJ.js` contains it. `world.ts:289` creates the `sky` sphere with no `castShadow`, and `primitives.ts:88` defaults it to true. `ShadowSystem.ts:132` skips oversize casters only outside legacy mode. CHANGELOG 4.3.2 line 84 and `shadowLegacy.ts:3-9` describe the real-GPU stripes. This game has no real-GPU frame. Opt-in since v4.3.2, so this is not a 4.5.0 regression. | Add `castShadow: false, receiveShadow: false` to the sky sphere and delete `world.ts:218-219`, so the game uses the default legacy fit like the other 24 games. Recapture with the sun shadow on, plus one `tools/real-gpu/run.sh` frame. | done 2bf1ae759, 85e1458f4 (in part: the sky dome no longer casts; the shadow-fit opt-in stays (measured) and no real-GPU frame was taken) [4.6.1] |
| S-02 | P2 | Hiding the tab or blurring the window does not pause the game. On return, the run resumes at full speed and the wind loop keeps playing. | `rg "visibilitychange\|blur\|pagehide"` over `examples/ninja-runner-port` and `apps/showcase-ninja-runner-port/src` finds no handler. The only pause is Escape (`main.ts:258`). The wind bed loops (`audio.ts:84-88`). | On `visibilitychange` (hidden) and window `blur`, call `setPaused(true)` while the phase is `running`. Suspend `audio.ctx` on hide and resume it on Resume. | done 6ed4b3074 [4.6.1] |
| S-03 | P2 | Touch players cannot pause. | Pause is bound only to keydown Escape (`main.ts:258`). In `index.html:62,89-90` the only top-level control is `#mute`, and `#pause` only offers Resume. | Add a pause button of at least 44 px next to `#mute`. Show it with the HUD and call `setPaused(true)`. | done 6ed4b3074 [4.6.1] |
| S-04 | P2 | The first load is heavy and shows no progress. Recapture if the engine starts applying it. | not a defect: the HDRI texture is a plain object, so the scene lights through the neutral room, which already counts the hemisphere; removing the light would remove fill (comment added in 4678c26df) |

None of these is true now. | Regenerate `route-health.json` with the current route and limits, and correct the header comment. | done e2db084ac, 2bf1ae759, 4678c26df [4.6.1] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- No 4.5.0 frame exists. Retake run, gate-hit, mid-action and results at 1280 and 390 on the remote runner with `?capture=1`, and repeat with `&aura3d-render=production` to size the bloom-fusion change (RP-05). Treat a mean difference over 2/255 as a regression. Decide whether to pin `quality: "production"`.
- The game is not in the `tests/browser/game-luma-capture.spec.ts` TARGETS. Add it, record the steady-state luma on 4.3.7 and 4.5.0 (LIGHT-02), and record an AO-on/AO-off pair on the high tier (LIGHT-06).
- There is no real-GPU frame (`screens/final/README.md`: "Nothing here is real-GPU evidence"). This matters most for S-01.
- No browser spec covers pause and resume, touch swipes, the countdown, Run again after a win or loss, a `visibilitychange`, or `diagnostics().renderPath` and `droppedFeatures`. The 102 unit tests in 11 files cover the sim and presentation only.
- The open boxes in `docs/upgrade-4.5.0/ninja-runner-port.md` (RH-04) and the index poster (RH-05) are from before 4.5.0. Close them after the captures above.

**Dropped on verification**

- C1 is not a 4.5.0 regression. `git log -S__A3D_SHADOW_FIT__` dates the opt-in to commit 3405d2082 (first tag v4.3.2), so it is kept as S-01 at P2 because no stripes have been seen in this game.
- C2 is merged into the tests and evidence gaps: it is a missing measurement, not an observed defect.
- C14 is merged into the tests and evidence gaps.

## 21. Lamplight Line (`last-train-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/lamplight-line/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-last-train-port` · **Genre:** racing

**State:** The game loads and plays on production with 0 console errors, 0 failed requests and 105/105 model nodes decoded. The Prism probe ran on SwiftShader (software GL, so about 0.25 fps, not a real-GPU number). Steady-state luma with `?capture=1` was 86.3 by day, with 0% black and 0% clipped pixels and 96 coarse colours, and about 30 at night. The autopilot drove a full six-town circuit to a Silver results card, and a real R press reset the service. The renderer is healthy. The problems are in game logic and input. Phones cannot drive at all, the results card misreports score and riders, and two HUD-advertised keys do nothing in the state where players press them.

**4.5.0 impact:** The game's source did not change in the release. `git diff --stat v4.3.7 v4.5.0` shows only the `apps/showcase-last-train-port/package.json` version bump. The scene authors an HDRI (`examples/last-train-port/world.ts:176`) and no look preset, so the new authored-lights-only rule and the look fill do not apply. Live `renderPath` is `hdr-msaa` / `quality: auto` / `bloomFusion: separable`. Three things from 4.5.0 do reach this game. (1) `preserveDrawingBuffer` is now off outside `?capture=1`/`?qa=1`, so the `pixels()` QA hook reads a black canvas (D-07). (2) The 4.5.0 real-depth AO fix does nothing here, because the authored AO pass is dropped for a missing normals input (D-08). (3) Fused bloom (`separable`) has never been compared with the production chain for this game. Nobody has measured a 4.3.7 vs 4.5.0 steady-state luma for it either.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| D-01 | P1 | No touch controls. A phone player can leave the title card but cannot depart, brake or balance. | The only gameplay input is `keydown`/`keyup` (`examples/last-train-port/main.ts:230-245`). The single `pointerdown` handler (`main.ts:229`) only unlocks audio and calls `startPlay()`. `rg -n "touch\|pointer" examples/last-train-port/*.ts` finds nothing else. new-game-checklist.md:24 requires 44 px touch targets. | Add a hold-button bar (Go/Doors, Brake, Lean L, Lean R, each at least 44 px) shown on `(pointer: coarse)`. Feed it into the same `keys` Set through pointerdown/up/cancel with `setPointerCapture`. Add a 390x844 touch-play spec. | done 0531bb112, d1a617de3 [4.6.1] |
| D-02 | P1 | "Enter to keep riding" leaves the tram stuck at the platform for good. | The results card says "Press R for a new service · Enter to keep riding" (`apps/showcase-last-train-port/index.html:90`). Enter only hides the card (`main.ts:233`). Once `attempt.status === "complete"`, `actionDoors` and `actionDepart` return early (`sim.ts:143`, `sim.ts:152`). A Node run of the shipped sim gave speed 0 and distance 0 after Enter plus 10 s holding W. | Make Enter start a free-ride attempt that keeps position and manifest, or change the text to "Enter to look around". Add a unit test either way. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |
| D-03 | P1 | The results Score and the saved best add up every service played in the session, so every service after the first says "new best". | `actionNewService` copies `fresh.score = s.score` (`sim.ts:163`). The card shows and stores `sim.score` (`main.ts:186`, `main.ts:189`). Node: the 2nd service had attempt fares 1800 + tips 1939, yet the card showed 8078 (the 1st showed 4039). Live: after R, the new service kept score 3957. | Score each attempt on its own (fares + tips + bonuses of `sim.attempt`). Use that for the card and for `aura3d.lasttrain.best`. If a session total is wanted, show it as a separate row. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |
| D-04 | P1 | "Riders lost" and the "A rider left unhappy" toast count tip points, not riders. | On a catch, `lost += before - tipOf(t)` and then `a.lost += lost` (`sim.ts:339-341`), and no passenger leaves. The card labels it "Riders lost" (`main.ts:187`) and the toast fires on each `lost` cue (`main.ts:173`). A Node hold-W run ended with `catches: 3, lost: 477` on a 12-seat tram. | Rename the row to "Tips lost" and the toast to "Lurch! Tips lost: N", or count riders whose tip fell to 0. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |
| D-05 | P2 | R ("new service") does nothing while a service is running, although the HUD always shows it. The only way to restart a bad run is to finish all six legs (454 to 706 s). | `index.html:67` always shows `R new service`. `actionNewService` returns when `attempt.status === "active"` (`sim.ts:161`). The live probe pressed R mid-route and saw no change and no message. | Make R ask for confirmation ("Press R again to abandon this service") and then force a new service. Or show the R hint only on the results card. | done 0531bb112, bb3795a3b [4.6.1] |
| D-06 | P2 | Gold can never be reached, so Silver is the real top grade and players are not told. | Gold needs `a.requests >= 3` (`sim.ts:210`). `requests` is set to 0 (`sim.ts:73`) and never written. `sim.ts:11` lists requests as not ported. The autopilot's perfect run (96.8% comfort, 0 catches) grades Silver. | Drop the `requests` term (Gold = comfort >= 90 and 0 catches), and show the grade thresholds on the results card. | done 0531bb112, fa68a79f9 [4.6.1] |
| D-07 | P2 | The `pixels()` QA hook reads an all-black canvas on 4.5.0 unless the page uses `?capture=1`/`?qa=1`. | `pixels()` calls `drawImage(canvas)` without rendering first (`main.ts:274-283`). Live plain URL: `pixels()` returned mean 0 and litFraction 0. After `frame()` in the same task it returned mean 86.7 and litFraction 0.997. Specs pass only because they call `frame()` first. docs/upgrade-4.5.0/last-train-port.md recommends this fix, and there is no commit on the game since. | Call `render()` inside `pixels()` before `drawImage`, as `frame()` already does. | done 25aadb0ad, 1e52498d1 [4.6.1] |
| D-08 | P2 | The authored ambient occlusion never draws. | `effects.ambientOcclusion` is authored at `world.ts:180`. Live `diagnostics().post`: requested bloom, color-grade and ssao. Submitted only bloom and color-grade, with `droppedPasses:[ssao]` and `missingInputs:["ssao:normals"]`. No warning reaches `diagnostics().warnings`. The authored vignette (`world.ts:183`) does not appear in `requestedPasses`. It may be folded into the grade, but that was not verified. | Engine: give ssao a normals input on the hdr-msaa path, or emit an EFFECT_DROPPED warning. Game: remove the AO node until it runs. Check the vignette with a `?fx=novignette` luma A/B. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |
| D-09 | P2 | About 400 MB of GPU memory for a small tram line. | Live `memory()`: `approximateGpuMemoryBytes` 401,198,286, 65 textures, 11 render targets, 165-167 draw calls by day. The tram GLB has 3 maps at 2048². `world.ts:426` creates 4 pax models per seat (48 models, 7k to 20k triangles each, mostly hidden). The committed `evidence/last-train-port/last-train-port.json` still shows the procedural-era 20 MB. | Resize the tram maps to 1024² and tower/windmill to 512². Use one pax model per seat with a tint swap. Decimate the houses. Re-record memory. | done 2dabdbde7, 53bd07d1e [4.6.1] |
| D-10 | P2 | Braking above 14 km/h always counts as harsh, so the advertised Space brake only costs comfort. | The brake removes `5 * brake` = 5 m/s² (`sim.ts:256`). `harsh` fires when deceleration > 3.8 m/s² and p2 > 4 m/s (`sim.ts:297`), at -18 comfort/s (`sim.ts:300`). Arrival speed is already capped by `sqrt(3.6*remaining)` (`sim.ts:271`). In Node, a brake-using policy ended at 67.7% comfort against 77.7% without braking. | Ramp the brake to about 3.5 m/s² over 0.4 s, or toast "Harsh brake" when the penalty applies. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |
| D-11 | P2 | The docked results frame uses a stale tram heading. | At a dock (`distance < 1`), the pose uses `smoothedForward` (`world.ts:508`), which updates only on rendered frames. `results.1280x720.jpg` came from `advance(30000)` and shows the tram tilted into the station canopy (evidence README problem 4). Ordinary play is probably unaffected. | Compute the dock heading from `sampleRoute(leg, 0..4)` and re-capture the results frame. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |
| D-12 | P2 | Ambient audio keeps playing in a background tab. | The noise loops and pad oscillators start once in `unlock`. Their gains change only from the rAF-driven `update` (`main.ts:224`), which stops in a hidden tab. `rg visibilitychange examples/last-train-port` finds nothing. | Suspend the AudioContext on `visibilitychange` and resume when the tab is visible again. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |
| D-13 | P3 | Deprecated renderer options with no effect. | `main.ts:363` passes `renderer: { mode: "production", qualityProfile: "production", fallback: "safe-basic" }`. Under strict mode, `createAuraApp.ts:121-127` throws AuraMigrationError for `mode`/`fallback`. Live the route runs `quality: auto`, so `mode: production` pins nothing. Live warning `post-field-deprecated:bloom.radius` comes from `world.ts:181`. | Replace with `quality: "auto"`. Drop `radius` from the bloom options. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| E-01 | P2 | The core skill, lean balance, has no gauge or warning before a catch. | The HUD (`main.ts:130-152`) shows speed, aboard, fares, tips and score, but no roll value. The streak multiplier and the lantern count are never shown. | Add a lean meter (green/amber/red at 0.13/0.22/0.34, plus a fill for the 1.5 s critical window), a streak readout and lanterns. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |

The 1-px rails and catenary (`world.ts:203-208`) blur when upscaled. | Use `Math.min(devicePixelRatio, 1.5)` and let adaptive render scale handle slow frames. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |
| E-03 | P3 | A service takes 7.5 to 12 minutes, has no short mode and no fail state. | Node runs: the autopilot took 706 s. Holding W with no balance still finished in 495 s with Bronze despite 37 catches. Lanterns drop (`sim.ts:341`) but nothing happens at 0. | Add a 2-leg short run. At 0 lanterns, end the service at the next station with the grade capped. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |

Skip the rain/snow updates when their amount is 0. | done 0531bb112 [4.6.1] |

Mute (M) could also be saved in localStorage (`main.ts:234`). | done 0531bb112, d1a617de3 [4.6.1] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- Every browser spec loads `/examples/last-train-port/` on the example dev server (`tests/browser/last-train-play.spec.ts:16`, `last-train-ui.spec.ts:23,48`, `last-train-port.spec.ts:18`). None loads the built `/apps/lamplight-line/` route. `evidence/exposure-parity/frames/scenes.json` records that route with a 404 and mean 22.6.
- `tests/unit/last-train-port/sim.test.ts` has no test for keep-riding, R during a service, the per-service score, the `lost` unit or Gold reachability (D-02 to D-06).
- `screens/final`, `route-health.json` (2026-10-23) and `last-train-port.json` (20 MB memory) all predate 4.5.0, as does the index poster (RH-05).
- There is no 4.3.7 vs 4.5.0 steady-state luma comparison, no fused-bloom vs production-chain diff and no real-GPU capture for this game (LIGHT-02, RP-05).
- Engine-wide items that touch this game: instanced sleepers pick one shadow cascade from the node centre (SI-1). The per-instance cull allocates new arrays every frame for the 890-instance sleepers (SI-2). A typo in `?aura3d-render=` stops the mount (RP-04).
- During an 8 s W hold, the first `?capture=1` probe session dropped to `about:blank`. A second session ran for more than 20 minutes without it. The cause is unknown, possibly SwiftShader memory pressure at about 400 MB (see D-09).
- `diagnostics().fps` reads 60 while frames advanced at about 0.25 fps, and `info.render.triangles` reads 0 with 165 draws. Both counters mislead perf triage.
- The QA hook `runCircuit()` throws "stuck waiting for ready" if called while the tram is stopped mid-route, because `runLeg` drives with empty input (`main.ts:86-93`).

**Dropped on verification**

- C19 "throws AuraMigrationError": the throw happens only under `strict`, and this game does not set strict. Kept as P3 dead-option cleanup (D-13).
- C12 "duplicate texture uploads": not isolated. Folded into D-09 as unverified.

## 22. Saltwind Highway (`roadster-hours-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/saltwind-highway/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-roadster-hours-port` · **Genre:** racing

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. The live probe (Prism, SwiftShader software GL) saw no console errors and no failed requests. The render path was `hdr-msaa` with 121 draw calls, 9 programs compiled and 0 failed. Steady-state luma held at mean 78.6 to 79.0, about 6% below 12, 0% clipped and 73 colours, at both 3 s and 12 s. Driving, steering, pause, a full autopilot finish (59.65 s, Silver), the results card and restart all worked. Health is degraded: two P1 bugs affect players, R recovery fires about 12 times per tap and there are no touch controls, plus a set of audio, coaching and copy gaps.

**4.5.0 impact:** The game's source did not change. The release only bumps the version in `apps/showcase-roadster-hours-port/package.json`. No `quality` option is passed (`examples/roadster-hours-port/main.ts:337-342`), so the game resolves to quality `auto`. Under `auto`, 4.5.0 turns on separable bloom fusion and shadow-cascade skipping for this bloom-plus-sun-shadow scene. Live luma looks normal, but neither change has been compared against 4.3.7 or `?aura3d-render=production` for this game. The deprecated `renderer.qualityProfile: "production"` (`main.ts:339`) no longer selects the pipeline and only logs a warning. Nothing is broken, but the release effect on this game is unmeasured.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | P1 | One tap of R recovers about 12 times. Each recovery adds a crash and costs 150 points. | `main.ts:85` maps `recover: held("KeyR")`. `held()` (`main.ts:74`) reads `down`, which stays filled until keyup. `stepRun` calls `recoverRun` on every 1/120 s step while R is down (`sim.ts:303`), and each call does `crashes++` and `score - 150` (`sim.ts:142-143`). Measured: a 100 ms hold (12 steps) took crashes 0 to 12 and score 1250 to 0. The live bundle contains `recover:iA("KeyR")`. The original game does `W.delete("KeyR")` after one step (fixture `game-qAzJLoNH.js:649`). | Use `recover: pressed.has("KeyR")`. `pressed` is already cleared after each step (`main.ts:119`). Add a keyboardInput unit test: a 12-step R hold must give `crashes === 1`. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |
| C2 | P1 | No touch controls, so the game cannot be played on phones or tablets, yet 390x844 frames are published. | The only input listeners are keydown, keyup and blur (`main.ts:59-72`). The one pointer handler (`main.ts:367`) only unlocks audio. `rg touch` finds nothing outside CSS. PORTING-NOTES lists touch as "not ported". `key-drive.390x844.jpg` shows a HUD with no controls. The original had `G.throttle`/`G.thrust`/`G.nitro` touch input (fixture `:466`, `:563`). | Under `(pointer: coarse)`, add pointer zones for steering plus thrust, nitro and pause buttons of at least 44 px with safe-area insets. Merge them into `keyboardInput` and replace "R TO RECOVER" with a touch prompt. | done 0ba61efcf, fd868fd16 [4.6.1] |
| C3 | P2 | The procedural lighthouse and plaster-cube town render underneath the Meshy models in the same spots. | `world.ts:491-509` adds `town`, `lighthouse-rock` and seven `lighthouse-*` cylinders on every coast route with no `hasMeshy` guard. `world.ts:531-543` then adds `lighthouse-model`, `town-villas` and `town-townhouses` at the same positions. The comment at `world.ts:521` claims "each falls back to the procedural pieces above", but the code never removes those pieces. The final capture's node list contains both sets. | Guard the procedural town with `!hasMeshy("villa") && !hasMeshy("townhouse")` and the lighthouse tiers with `!hasMeshy("lighthouse")`. Re-count draw calls afterwards: about 9 fewer nodes plus their shadow draws. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |
| C4 | P2 | The finish fanfare never plays. | `stepOnce` sets `screen = "results"` in the step that completes the run (`main.ts:120`). `render()` calls `sound.update` only when `screen === "play"` (`main.ts:204`), so `diffRunEvents` never receives the `complete` frame that emits `"finish"` (`audio.ts:35`). `rg finish main.ts` finds no direct play call. | Call `sound.update` with the final frame, or play `"finish"` directly, before switching screens. Add a flow test that asserts the cue. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |
| C5 | P2 | The menu promises a nitro "green window" that is never drawn, and the per-act coaching hints are not shown, so PERFECT NITRO and SHOCKWAVE cannot be discovered. | `index.html:106` says "Nitro (tap on the green window)". `run.nitroWindow` (`sim.ts:287`) is never read by `main.ts`, `world.ts` or `index.html`. Every act in `data.ts` has a `hint`, but the HUD shows only `run.act` (`main.ts:170`). The original showed a priority coach line and toggled a `window` class (fixture `:282`, `:326`). | Port the coach line into a `#coach` element. Turn the thrust bar green while `nitroWindow` is true, and add a text or pulse cue so the signal does not rely on colour alone. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |
| C6 | P2 | Many gameplay events are silent: scrapes, barrier and vent impacts, overheat, tricks, trailer rides, shortcuts, nitro levels and missed relays. | `diffRunEvents` (`audio.ts:30-44`) derives only 11 cues from counter diffs and never reads `run.events`. The sim pushes scrape, overheat, impact, trick, roll, hop, trailer, nitro and land events. A non-clean landing does not increment `cleanLandings`, so it makes no sound. | Collect `run.events` from every step into a per-frame queue and map each event type to a cue. Have a person listen to the result. | done 0ba61efcf (in part: no person has listened to the new cues) [4.6.1] |
| C8 | P2 | Music plays in note clusters while the tab is hidden. | The scheduler runs `setInterval` and loops `while (this.nextAt < ctx.currentTime + 0.25)` (`audio.ts:216-228`) without skipping late steps. Hidden-tab throttling (about 1 s) makes each tick schedule several notes whose start times have already passed, so they play at once. `rg visibilitychange` finds nothing. | Suspend the AudioContext when the tab is hidden and resume it when visible. Before the loop, set `nextAt = max(nextAt, currentTime + 0.05)`. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |
| C9 | P2 | Public copy is stale: "92 vehicles in one batched mesh", "no shadows", and "the cars are CC0 models". | `tools/site/games.catalog.json:229`, `apps/showcase-roadster-hours-port/package.json:6` and `index.html:6` all say "92 vehicles in one batched mesh", and the catalog says "no shadows". The in-game credit (`examples/roadster-hours-port/index.html:105`) says "cars are CC0 models (Quaternius, Kenney)". Measured from `course.ts`: 33 to 35 vehicles per route, drawn as per-model batched meshes. Sun shadows are on (`world.ts:444`). By default the cars are Meshy-generated (`world.ts:512-581`). | Rewrite the catalog entry, meta description, route-health limits and credit line from the code. Run `marketing/scripts/check-site.mjs`. | done 0ba61efcf (in part: `tools/site/games.catalog.json` and `names.json` copy is an open handoff to E5) [4.6.1] |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C7 | P2 | The chase camera is locked rigidly to the road frame, with no smoothing, shake or FOV kick. | `world.ts:765` calls `cameras.chase()` with no `lag`, so `approach()` snaps to the target. A hazard's 1.8 m lateral push (`sim.ts:552`) jumps the camera 1.44 m in one step. The original added trauma and FOV kicks per event (fixture `:636`). | Pass `lag: { eye: 9, look: 12, fov: 4 }`. Cut the camera on begin and recover. Add event trauma and FOV kicks, and turn shake off under reduced motion. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |

The live diagnostics show `post-field-deprecated:bloom.radius` (from `look.ts:62/73/84` and `world.ts:450`), `BLOOM_THRESHOLD_BELOW_HDR_WHITE` and `bloom-noise-risk threshold=0.88`. | Remove `qualityProfile`, or replace it with `quality: "production"` to keep the 4.3.7 pipeline. Migrate `radius` to `scatter`. Retune the threshold after the bloom-fusion capture. | done 0ba61efcf (in part: `bloom.radius` stays because `effects.bloom` has no public `scatter` field (same as WW-16)) [4.6.1] |
| C12 | P3 | No reduced-motion handling, key remapping, on-screen pause button or auto-pause on blur. Buttons are 32 px tall. | `rg matchMedia\|reduced-motion` finds nothing. Keys are hard-coded (`main.ts:79-85`). On blur the game only clears held keys (`main.ts:72`), and the run keeps going. `index.html:53` sets `height: 32px`. | Add a pause button, auto-pause on blur and visibilitychange, a reduced-motion switch, WASD/arrow remapping and 44 px targets. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |
| C13 | P3 | Best times and medals are not saved, and "Next route" reloads the whole page. | The `career` object lives only in memory (`main.ts:43-44`). The `next` button sets `location.search` (`main.ts:366`), which re-fetches about 6.9 MB of Meshy assets and waits through the 1.5 s settle again. | Save `career.results` in localStorage and show the best time and delta. Optionally rebuild the world in place for the next route. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |
| C14 | P3 | Allocations and DOM writes on every frame. | `viewOf` copies the Run object (`presentation.ts:41-47`). `matrixFor` allocates a Float32Array per traffic slot per frame (`world.ts:424-427`, `833-836`). `updateHud` writes about 15 DOM values every frame (`main.ts:165-180`). | Reuse buffers and the view object, and write to the DOM only when a value changes. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |
| C15 | P3 | A fixed 1.5 s loading delay replaces a real readiness signal. | `main.ts:347-353` steps for 1500 ms because, per its comment, the engine exposes no completion signal. The QA hook already uses `summarizeTextureUpgrade` (`main.ts:318`). | Wait until `summarizeTextureUpgrade(...).pending === 0` and `warmPrograms(app)` finishes, capped at about 8 s. | done 0ba61efcf [4.6.1] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- No 4.5.0 capture of this game exists. `screens/final` dates from 0d965903c (2026-10-25), before v4.5.0. Neither `RESULTS-4.5.0.md` nor PRD-3 mentions it. Needed: `?capture=1` against `?capture=1&aura3d-render=production` on the remote runner to measure bloom fusion (RP-05), and a 4.3.7-vs-4.5.0 steady-state luma row in `game-luma-capture` (LIGHT-02). Then refresh the screens and posters (RH-05) and close the per-game upgrade guide checks (RH-04).
- Instanced scenery (palms, pines, boulders) picks one shadow cascade from the node centre. Spread-out instances can fall outside that cascade and render unshadowed (SI-1, an engine issue that predates 4.5.0). Check it in the same capture.
- The browser spec has one test (`tests/browser/roadster-hours-port.spec.ts:54`). There are no unit tests for `keyboardInput`, pause, restart or the step-to-results flow, which is why C1 and C4 shipped.
- A typo in `?aura3d-render=` or `?aura3d-bloom-fusion=` stops the game from mounting (RP-04, engine-wide).
- The live probe ran on SwiftShader at about 6.5 real fps, and the countdown ran in slow motion because each frame is capped at 0.1 s. There is no real-GPU frame-time number.

## 23. The Bellkeeper's Walk (`where-the-wind-wanders-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/the-bellkeepers-walk/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-where-the-wind-wanders-port` · **Genre:** arcade

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. The live probe ran in the Prism cloud browser on SwiftShader software GL. It recorded no console errors and no failed requests, and the render path was `hdr-msaa` with 17 draw calls. Steady-state mean luma was 168.8 at about 12 s, with 0.1% near-black pixels and 0% clipped. The probe walked the full objective (three letters, then the bell), and the bell completion and its caption fired. The desktop build is healthy. The defects are on phones and come from the live wrapper page: `apps/.../index.html` never received the mobile CSS fix that went into the examples page, and the site credit link overlaps the touch joystick.

**4.5.0 impact:** 4.5.0 changed no game code. `git diff --stat v4.3.7 v4.5.0` over the app, example and port-shared folders touches only the app's `package.json` version. The scene has no lights, no environment and no look preset, so the lighting and fill changes do not reach it. Exposure-parity measured mean luma 188.3 both before and after. One change does apply. `main.ts:302-309` passes `qualityProfile: "production"` but no `quality`, and `renderPath.ts:152-153` treats `qualityProfile` as a deprecated preset, so quality resolves to `auto` (`renderPath.ts:157`). The scene has `effects.bloom` (`world.ts:376`), so it now runs the fused `separable` bloom chain. The only pixel check of fusion is the 1/255 measurement on the PBR viewer. This game's upgrade guide says outright: "nothing was built or run" (`docs/upgrade-4.5.0/where-the-wind-wanders-port.md:3`). The change is probably fine, but it has not been measured on this game (see the tests section).

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WW-1 | P1 | The live page is missing the mobile HUD fix. On phones the keyboard hint overlaps the quest counter and the Journal/Sound bar, and touch users see keyboard-only text. | af597118d added `@media (max-width:560px)` (moves `#quest` and `#hint`) and `@media (pointer:coarse){#hint{display:none}}` only to `examples/where-the-wind-wanders-port/index.html:20-24`. In `apps/showcase-where-the-wind-wanders-port/index.html:31`, `#hint` is still at `top:12px; right:12px`. `curl -sL https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/the-bellkeepers-walk/ \| grep -c 'max-width: 560px'` returns `0`. Under 560px, `ui.ts:54` moves `#wwui-bar` to top 8px right 8px, on the assumption that the hint has moved down. The evidence screens were taken on the examples page, so they show the fixed layout. | Copy the two media queries into the wrapper's `index.html`, or generate the wrapper's style block from the example. | done febd9dbb7 [4.6.1] |
| WW-2 | P1 | On touch devices the "Built with Aura3D" credit link sits inside the joystick area. At 390px the 85-character credit wraps to 2 lines (12px/1.4), so the link sits about 30-50px above the bottom edge. `#wwui-pad` (`ui.ts:55-56`) is 128x128 at left 16px, bottom 14px. At the payoff moment only the glow quad and the audio react. | Give the belltower model a runtime id and apply the same swing curve to it, or to a bell sub-node. | done 7698289b3, febd9dbb7 [4.6.1] |

The pause handler (`main.ts:168`) only flips `paused`. | Queue captions on the game clock and advance them only while not paused. **Tests and evidence gaps**

- The fused bloom chain is unmeasured on this game (shared findings RP-05 and RH-04). Capture `?capture=1` against `?capture=1&aura3d-render=production` on the letter-glow frame on the remote runner and record the mean and max diff. Alternatively, pass `quality: "production"` to stay on the 4.3.7 chain.
- No test covers the live wrapper route, touch input or the credit overlap. `tests/browser/where-the-wind-wanders-port.spec.ts` drives the examples page at 960x540 with keyboard only. Add a 390x844 `hasTouch` run on `/apps/the-bellkeepers-walk/` that taps Begin, drives the pad, checks that no navigation happens, and checks that the HUD boxes do not intersect.
- No test plays the real-time ending (captions, epilogue, Continue, timers). The final `results` screen was forced with `epilogue(6)` and was not reached by play.
- The final screens date from e62b5fd42 (2026-10-25), before the 4.5.0 bloom-fusion change (shared finding RH-05). Measured luma is unchanged (188.3), so the drift is likely small, but the screens should be taken again once the fusion pair above is recorded.
- No real-GPU capture exists. The live probe ran on SwiftShader, where each frame took about 2 s, and fps was not measured on hardware.

**Dropped on verification**

- C8 as a P2 "regression" is downgraded to a test gap. The fused bloom is designed to look the same as the production chain, and no measured difference exists on this game. It is listed under tests, together with RP-05 and RH-04.
- LIGHT-02 does not apply to this game. It has no lights or environment, and exposure-parity already holds a before-and-after luma pair for it (188.32 in both runs). Only the general real-GPU gap remains, listed under tests.
- C11 is merged into the tests section, and RH-05 is merged into the stale-screens bullet.

## 24. Duneglide (`silt-and-signal-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/duneglide/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-silt-and-signal-port` · **Genre:** arcade

**State:** The game loads and plays on production. The probe ran in Prism on SwiftShader software GL and saw 0 console errors and 0 failed requests, with renderPath `hdr-msaa`, 118 draw calls and 15 programs (3,392 ms compile). The title frame dropped from mean luma 173.5 at 3 s to 101.2 at 12 s. In play after 380 m of flight it read mean luma 114.4 with 0% black, and Begin, W-throttle, ring progress (1/8 to 4/8) and Esc pause all worked. Desktop chapter 0 works. Progression has hard faults: touch players cannot finish objective 2, and every later chapter reloads into QA mode. Chapters 1-6 also let the rider fly through hills. Health: degraded.

**4.5.0 impact:** The code needs no changes for the render-path updates, but the result is unmeasured. The scene authors `environments.neutral` (world.ts:177), `output`, bloom, colour grade, toon and custom shaders, so it stays on `hdr-msaa`. The implicit-environment and no-HDRI look changes do not apply. `main.ts:396` sets only the deprecated `qualityProfile`, so quality resolves to `auto`. That means separable bloom fusion and the cascade skip apply to its 3-cascade 4096 sun (world.ts:184). No 4.5.0 frames exist: the final screens were built at `0d965903c` on 2026-10-25. The `lights.hemisphere` at world.ts:178 is still dropped because an environment node is present (LIGHT-05). This matches 4.3.7, so it is not a regression.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | P0 | Touch players cannot send `interact` (or brake), so chapter 0 objective 2 ("care") never completes and no later chapter is reachable on a phone. | `examples/silt-and-signal-port/main.ts:168-178` `readPointer()` returns only throttle 1, steer, lift and boost. The interact sources are `KeyF` and gamepad X (main.ts:211). `sim.ts:246` care/resonate/relay need `speed < 5 && input.interact`, and `sim.ts:249` the portal needs interact too. A held pointer forces throttle 1. The page has no on-screen buttons. | Add a coarse-pointer button cluster (hold-to-interact that also brakes, plus pause and boost), with 44 px targets and safe-area insets, and feed it into `sampleInput()`. Add a touch spec that completes ch0 care and the portal. | done dcd9c6b2b [4.6.1] |
| C2 | P1 | Every chapter change, chapter jump and "Fly again" reloads with `start=1`, which the code treats as QA mode. After that, Esc, blur and gamepad-Start pause are dead, keyboard players get no audio, and mute cannot be reached. | `main.ts:40` `qa = ... \|\| params.get("start") === "1"`. `main.ts:123` sets `?chapter=N&start=1`. `!qa` guards Esc (main.ts:144), blur (main.ts:149) and Start (main.ts:187). Mode starts in `play` (main.ts:43), so `beginPlay()`, the keyboard `audio.resume()` path (main.ts:133-136), never runs. Only a canvas pointerdown resumes audio (main.ts:158). The only in-game mute button is `#pause-mute` on the pause screen. | Keep `qa` for objective/autopilot/manual only, and make `start=1` mean "skip the title". Resume audio on the first keydown or pad button. Persist mute in sessionStorage. | done dcd9c6b2b [4.6.1] |
| C3 | P1 | In chapters 1-6 the sim floor is a flat 0.6 m while the rendered terrain rises to 96.6 m, so the rider and the chase camera pass through hills. | `sim.ts:157` `floor: () => 0.6`, and `world-ch0.ts:88-90` `chapterWorld()` returns a floor only for chapter 0. **Tests and evidence gaps**

- No Duneglide frames exist from 4.5.0. Recapture title, mid-fly and results at 1280x720 and 390x844 on `/apps/duneglide/`, and record `diagnostics().renderPath` (quality `auto`, bloomFusion) (RH-05, LIGHT-02).
- Separable bloom fusion was never compared with `?aura3d-render=production` for this game (RP-05).
- No test covers `start=1` player mode, touch progression, the sim floor against `heightAt` in ch1-6, or results totals across chapters. The final results frame was forced with `setMode('done')`.
- The `stepFrames` QA hook takes a fixed input and cannot drive the autopilot, so a deterministic chapter-completion run is not possible on the live loop at 0.07 fps. Let it fall back to `sampleInput()` when no input is given.
- The game has not been run on a real GPU or a real phone.
- RP-04 applies: a typo in `?aura3d-render=` stops the mount. Engine-wide, tracked in the release section.

**Dropped on verification**

- C4's "4.5.0 regression" label: the HTML gap predates 4.5.0, and the 4.3.x claim never reached the app page. The finding stays at P1 as a visual defect.
- C11 is merged into the gaps above (same evidence as RH-05/RP-05).
- PROBE blur/Start double-toggle concern: both paths call `setMode` on separate events, and Start is edge-triggered (`padStartHeld`, main.ts:186-188). No mechanism for a double toggle was found.

## 25. Cumulus Dash (`cloud-runner-port`)

**Live:** https://aura3d.auraone.ai/apps/cumulus-dash/ · **Source:** `apps/showcase-cloud-runner-port` (3-line shim mounting `examples/kid-goku-beyond-the-clouds-port`) · **Genre:** platformer

**State:** The game loads and plays on production with 0 console errors and 0 failed requests. The probe ran in the Prism cloud browser on SwiftShader software GL, using the hdr-msaa path at quality `auto`. Menu frames read mean luma 121-132 and in-play frames 155.4, with 0% black and 0% clipped pixels. Steering, Play, the result card and Enter-to-retry all work. A real run ended correctly after 3 gap falls. Two logic bugs remain. Dying on an obstacle soft-locks the run. "Run it again" after a win restarts near the finish with every pearl kept. The scene is heavy for a lane runner: 164 draw calls, 647k triangles and about 252-333 MB of GPU memory.

**4.5.0 impact:** The app folder changed only in its `package.json` version. The engine changes do reach the game. In 4.3.7 the authored `sky-fill` hemisphere light was turned into a direct spot proxy (`observations.ts` fallthrough in v4.3.7). Commit 5f0fd854f now drops it as a direct light (`renderer.ts:188` `hemisphereAsEnvironment: true`, `observations.ts:333`). Because the look preset's HDRI exists, only the neutral-room source (`environment.ts:561`) reads hemispheres, so the light now contributes nothing. Live diagnostics show `lightsEvaluated 2` with `hemisphere:false`. The brightness change was never measured. The 4.5.0 exposure-parity record has identical before and after means (172.2815437219224) and a 404 on both. The real-depth AO change has no effect here, because the look's SSAO pass is dropped for missing normals. Bloom fusion (`separable`) was not compared against the production chain for this game. The upgrade guide says "No change needed" and still has 8 open checkboxes. This needs a measured capture before anyone calls it fine.

**Fix (bugs and defects)**

| ID | Sev | Issue | Evidence | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CR-1 | P1 | Losing the last heart on a rock or log never ends the run. The runner jogs in place, the score keeps rising and no result screen appears. | `game.ts:176-179` handles worldPass `"hit"` but never checks `s.dead`. `damage()` sets `state.dead` (`sim.ts:461`). Only `encounterEvent` (`game.ts:312`) and the fall path (`game.ts:342`) call `end(false)`. After that, `step()` returns `[]` while dead (`sim.ts:270`), but `game.ts:183` still adds `s.speed*t*0.6` to the score each frame. Live repro: `place(40,{lane:2.6})`, hearts=1, `play(120)` gives snapshot `dead`, game phase `playing` and result null. A further `play(600)` moved the score from 129 to 216 with `s` unchanged. | At the end of `playing()`, add `if (s.dead && this.phase === "playing") out.push(...this.end(false))`. Skip score accrual when `s.dead` or `s.finished`. Add a unit test: hearts=1, hit obstacle 0, expect phase `result` and `won:false`. | done d6ad6ce4f [4.6.1] |
| CR-2 | P1 | "Run it again" after a win restarts at the last checkpoint with all pearls already collected. | `ui.ts:131`/`:144` and `main.ts:69` call `game.retry()`, which runs `start(s.checkpoint, s.balls)` (`game.ts:99-102`) whatever `result.won` is. Live: a win at `place(1950)` followed by retry gave `{phase:'playing', s:1850, balls:[1,2,3,5,6,7,4]}`. `end(true)` pays 500 per pearl (`game.ts:118`), so a short 110 m replay earns the full pearl bonus. | When `result.won`, call `game.start(0)`. Keep `retry()` for losses and label the buttons "Run again" for a win and "Continue from checkpoint" for a loss. Add a test that retry after a win gives `s=0, balls=[]`. | done d6ad6ce4f, f61c5f403 [4.6.1] |
| CR-3 | P2 | The authored `sky-fill` hemisphere (0.7) contributes nothing in 4.5.0. It was a direct spot proxy in 4.3.7. | See 4.5.0 impact. `world.ts:61` next to `looks.preset("outdoor-day")` (`world.ts:59`, which has an HDRI). `prd02.explicit` calls `prd02AmbientTerm(snapshot)` without `includeHemisphere` (`environment.ts:~545`). Live: `lightsEvaluated 2`, program keys `hemisphere:false`. The pixel delta is unmeasured, which is why this is P2 and not P1. The same engine gap affects 8 other games (release finding LIGHT-05). | Engine: pass `{ includeHemisphere: true }` on the explicit-environment path, or emit an OPTION_IGNORED-style warning. Game: delete `sky-fill` or recalibrate it after a before-and-after luma capture. | done 0a699af24 [4.6.1] |
| CR-4 | P2 | The look preset and the authored nodes stack: two suns 14° apart, two colour grades, SSAO dropped every frame, and a vignette with no consumer. | `world.ts:59` look sun (az 40°, el 38°, key 3.0) plus `world.ts:60` authored `sun` 3.2, which is the only shadow caster. `world.ts:63-65` adds a second bloom, grade and vignette. Live: effects list both `look color grade` and `grade`, `droppedPasses ['ssao']` with `missingInputs ['ssao:normals']`, and the vignette consumer is `none`. The shading direction and the shadow direction disagree. | Point the look sun along `SUN_POS` and drop the authored sun, or the reverse, keeping the total intensity. Move the grade values into the look overrides. Remove the vignette or wire it to a consumer. Recapture. | done 0a699af24, 9ac2e9262 [4.6.1] |
| CR-5 | P2 | Finale: the letterbox bars cover the hearts, pause button and gauge, and the camera frames the hut wall without the hero. | `screens/final/results.640x360.jpg`, final README "Problems still visible" #1. `ui.ts:166` keeps the HUD on outside the menu. `ui.ts:189-190` only adds the bars. `world.ts:493-497` looks at lane 8.0 (the hut) while the hero stays on its own lane. There is no camera clearance (checklist M9). | Hide the HUD top row, gauge, combo and toast in `finale`. Aim the camera midway between the hero and the hut, pull it back, and run `createCameraClearance` against the hut. | done f61c5f403, 0a699af24 [4.6.1] |

**Tests and evidence gaps**

- The unit tests (`tests/unit/kid-goku-beyond-the-clouds-port/game.test.ts:132`) cover death only by a gap fall. Nothing covers obstacle death at 1 heart (CR-1), retry after a win (CR-2), blur or visibility pause, or the touch handler. Pause also ignores the `falling` and `finale` phases (`game.ts:96`).
- The exposure-parity record for this game is void. Before and after are both 172.2815437219224 with a 404 (`evidence/exposure-parity/frames/scenes.json`). Steady-state luma at fixed `place()` points (60, 520, 1100, 1420, 1700) on 4.3.7 and 4.5.0 is needed, with and without the hemisphere fix (LIGHT-02, LIGHT-06).
- There is no per-game comparison of fused bloom against `?aura3d-render=production` (RP-05).
- The final screens (`evidence/kid-goku-beyond-the-clouds-port/screens/final`, last commit 2026-10-25) predate the boulder grounding fix and 4.5.0 (RH-05). They are 640x360 only. They contain no real won or lost result card (results.640x360 is a forced `place(1000000)` cutscene), and no capture emulates `pointer: coarse`, so the touch controls are never shown.
- `docs/upgrade-4.5.0/cloud-runner-port.md` has 8 unchecked items and says "No change needed" without a render (RH-04).
- Nothing has run on a real GPU. All evidence is SwiftShader.

**Dropped on verification**

- C3 as a P1 "regression": downgraded to P2 and merged with LIGHT-05 as CR-3. The light did change (a spot proxy in 4.3.7, dropped in 4.5.0), but the 4.3.7 proxy was itself a mis-aimed spot and the visible size of the change is unmeasured. LIGHT-05's claim that the behaviour "matches 4.3.7" is wrong for this game.
- C8 split: the keyboard-only hints became CR-9 (downgraded to P3 because the touch buttons are labelled) and the sound toggle became CR-E4.
- C10 split: the hidden boot error became CR-7 and the missing warmPrograms became CR-E3 (P3, because no hitch was measured).
- RP-04 (a URL typo stops the mount): an engine-wide debug switch, not specific to this game. It belongs in the release section.
- Probe note that the `main.ts:1-4` header reverses the names: cosmetic comment only, not an audit item.
